# My Little Disaster (was My Tiny Disaster, Z-Day): project notes

READ THIS FIRST after a compaction. Current as of 2026-10-26.

## Where things stand (summary for picking up after a compact)
- The game is **My Little Disaster** (renamed from Z-Day on 2026-10-26, then from My Tiny Disaster on 2026-10-28; the repo and URL stay devon-cadaver/zday, the save key stays zday_profile so progress is kept).
- A warm sepia tilt-shift miniature city (procedural Three.js) that disasters hit. Sean plays on an older iPhone through GitHub Pages and wants finished, pushed work with screenshots sent via SendUserFile.
- Modes (menu = default page):
  - **Commander** (main game): each 10 minute round is a run of 2 to 5 random incidents (zombie outbreak, quake, riot, flood... up to meltdown as you level) that the player answers with rescue units (police, evacuation bus, fire truck, water and rescue helicopters, army APC, airstrike, nuke). The next incident comes early once the city is quiet; clearing them all secures the city. XP, levels (12), unlocks, upgrades, stars.
  - **Be The Disaster** (src/be.js + src/be-more.js, was Storm mode): pick any of 18 disasters (unlocked by level) and drive it with one finger for 3 minutes, each with its own controls; two fingers pan and zoom. Score, ranks, best per disaster.
  - **Sandbox**: drop zombies, Disasters panel triggers any of the 14 disasters and 9 weathers.
  - showcase.html: roster of every model.
- Disasters (src/disasters.js registry HAZARDS + start()): tornado, quake, flood, tsunami, riot, aliens, meteor, sinkhole, volcano, haboob (dust storm), coldwave, solar, nuclear, meltdown, blackhole, plus zombie outbreaks (19 zombie types from Sean's sheet; only radioactive ones glow). Weather: clear, rain, storm, fog, snow, heat, ash, dust, sand.
- Living city features: traffic (roundabouts, diagonal avenue, superblocks), ~10k people with states (walk, flee, shelter indoors, roofs via stair huts, film disasters on phones, freeze, riot...), fire spread, day/night, weather, debris, rubble, craters, outskirts (desert E, beach and ocean S with a nuclear plant, mountains N, forest W).
- Testing workflow: dev server launch config "zday" (port 5191); Playwright (mcp__playwright__*) with window.__z hooks; step the sim by hand with `g.speed = 1; g.update(0.1, 0)` (each call = 0.1 s), set g.speed = 0 to freeze for screenshots; screenshots must be saved under shots/ in the project; check the live site with ?v=<commit> after a push (Pages deploys in about 1 minute).
- Patch workflow: node .cjs scripts in the scratchpad that throw if an anchor is missing (bash heredocs mangle backticks and quotes in shader code: use Write or Edit for those).
- Commit messages end with: Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

## File map
- main.js: renderer, camera, lights, render loop, camera shake per frame, window.__z.
- game.js: shared layer (HUD, toasts, tap input, speed, fixed 1/30 s step, menu, unlock screen) and the Sandbox mode with the Disasters panel.
- commander.js: the main game round. profile.js: XP, levels, UNLOCK, UPGRADES, HAZARD_LEVEL, WEATHER_LEVEL, difficulty().
- be.js: Be The Disaster mode (BE table, one controller class per disaster, BeTheDisaster mode).
- holes.js: real pits (ground materials discard inside open holes; cutGround(mat), addHole/setHole/removeHole). blackhole.js: the mini black hole. boats.js: rescue boats.
- city.js: layout (grid, superblocks, roundabouts, diagonal avenue), buildings (records with door, roof, hut/hatch, vertex ranges), street furniture, destruction API (hideBuilding, extractBuilding, addRubble, takeStreetNear, restoreAll, char).
- outskirts.js: terrain biomes, sea, highways, trees, the nuclear power plant (plant.group, towers, reactor, lightMat, steam()).
- traffic.js: cars on the node graph (insets, roundabout polylines, don't-block-the-box, closed roads), mission vehicles, parked cars, flinging.
- people.js: the agent sim (states in ST), walk grid (block(), grid0), rooftop refuges and watchers, filming onlookers (poi), freeze, fallIn, riots, aliens, officers, firefighters, soldiers.
- figures.js (procedural figure kit), vehicles.js (vehicle library), aircraft.js (helicopters, A-10, B-2), fire.js (GPU flame/smoke/mist/dust pools, building fires), daylight.js, post.js (DOF, grade, flash, black-light band), textures.js.
- disasters.js: Disasters manager (events list, leftovers, debris, decals, weather, water, splash, poiAt, destroyBuilding, strike, light levels, afterLight).
- weather.js, debris.js, water.js (WaterSurface, Splash, SweepFront, Flood, Tsunami), twister.js, quake.js, riot.js, aliens.js, impact.js (Hole, Meteor, Sinkhole), volcano.js (LavaField, Volcano), fronts.js (BillowWall, Haboob, ColdWave), solar.js, nuke.js (Mushroom, nuclearDamage, NuclearStrike, Meltdown).

## Original concept (2026-10-25)
- Living miniature city. At first the player dropped zombies to spread an infection; it became an emergency-commander game, then a disaster game.
- Look target: warm sepia tilt-shift miniature city (Sean's reference: 4 aerial Midjourney-style shots of a dense sand-coloured mid-rise city, rooftop greenery, terracotta roof patches, tiny people and cars, strong depth of field top and bottom).

## Stack
- Three.js 0.170 + Vite 6, plain JS, same as Dead City (010) and Valhalla's Tide (012). No Blender: everything is procedural.
- Dev server: `.claude/launch.json` config "zday" on port 5191.
- Live: https://devon-cadaver.github.io/zday/ (GitHub Pages, deploys on every push to main via .github/workflows/deploy.yml, about 1 minute).
- Repo: https://github.com/Devon-cadaver/zday (public, branch main). Sean approved publishing on 2026-10-25.
- No gh CLI on this PC. The repo was created through the GitHub REST API with the token from git credential manager (git credential fill), never printed.
- The claude.ai artifact link does NOT open on Sean's older iPhone (viewer says browser unsupported). Use the GitHub Pages link for phone testing.

## Files
- `src/main.js`: renderer, MapControls camera (fov 18, distance ~470, pitch 48, yaw 45), sun + hemisphere light, shadow camera follows the view target, render loop. Debug hook `window.__z`.
- `src/city.js`: street grid (12x12 blocks, 10 m road + 3 m sidewalks), lot subdivision, buildings (shop band ground floor, 4 facade styles, setbacks, balconies, parapets, roof huts, AC units, tanks, roof gardens, terracotta patches), parks, plazas, parking lots, street trees, lamps, crosswalks, parked cars. Geometry merged per material, trees/tanks/lamps instanced.
- `src/traffic.js`: cars on the intersection graph (right-hand lanes, bezier turns), pedestrians walking block perimeters, static crowds.
- `src/post.js`: scene to HDR target with depth, half-res golden-angle depth-of-field, composite with glow, ACES, sepia/split-tone grade, vignette, grain.
- `src/textures.js`: canvas textures (facades, shopfronts, roof, asphalt, sidewalk, grass).

## URL params
- `mode=commander|be|sandbox` (no mode = menu); `mode=be&kind=lava` starts that disaster straight away (tornado, sinkhole, quake, meteor, lava, tsunami, saucer); `mode=storm` still opens the tornado.
- `q=low|high` (low is auto on touch devices: 10x10 blocks, fewer people/cars, no MSAA, 2048 shadows).
- `x`, `z`, `d`, `p`, `a`, `fov`: camera target, distance, pitch, yaw, field of view.
- `dof=0`, `grade=0`, `blur=0.022`, `sun`, `hemi`, `fog`, `seed`.

## Screenshots
- `shots/`: shot3/shot4 desktop, shot5-low phone quality (sent to Sean 2026-10-25).

## Outbreak (v0.2, 2026-10-25)
- src/people.js: typed-array agent sim for every person. States WALK (block loop), IDLE (crowd), FLEE, RETURN, GRABBED, TURNING, ZOMBIE, FALLING. inf flag + incubation timer.
- Walk grid at 0.5 m (street corridors + open lots: plazas, parks, parking) with wall sliding. Spatial hash (6 m cells) of civilians, rebuilt every step.
- Shambler (ZTYPES[0]): wander 0.55, chase 1.3, lunge 2.9 under 2.2 m, sight 12, notice 5.5 m at 30% per think, bite 1.1 s, incubation 10-18 s. Bites scream-panic civilians within 12 m.
- panic() must re-check isCivilian: states change mid-tick (this caused bitten victims to be cured; fixed).
- Spread with one Shambler, no defenders: first bite from seconds (crowded drop) to ~3 min (quiet street); ~800 zombies at 7 min; city overrun ~15 min.
- src/game.js: tap a street to drop (3 Shamblers per run), HUD counters, outbreak clock, speed 1/2/4/8x (fixed 1/30 s substeps), Follow (zooms to 170 m and tracks a zombie, cancels on drag), Reset.
- Markers: green ring = zombie, amber = infected; drawn through buildings; scale with zoom. Blood decals at bites.

## Chaos (v0.3, 2026-10-25)
- Cars (src/traffic.js): lane following with braking, intersection reservation taken at the stop line (STOPLINE 3.4 m), stale reservations dropped, U-turn only mid-block with a clear opposite lane. Panic when 2+ zombies within 22 m or a bite within 20 m (game calls traffic.panicNear on bite events): x1.6 speed, weak late braking, ignore pedestrians and right of way. Run-overs kill civilians; zombies hit under 11 m/s are knocked down 65% of the time and get back up. Crashes (relative speed > 5 m/s) become sliding spinning wrecks (REST when stopped) with 28-63% fire chance; crash panics drivers within 18 m for 10 s. Spawn placement keeps cars 10 m apart (overlapping spawns caused phantom crashes).
- Rooftops (src/people.js): city.buildings gives each street-facing building a door, wall anchor, outward normal, roof rect, roofY and vertex ranges. Panicked civilians (60% when 30+ zombies or 2+ within 18 m) SEEK the nearest refuge door within 35 m, go INSIDE (hidden, stairs time), then ROOF (lvl = building index; interactions only on the same lvl). Capacity area/5.
- Piles: ground zombies with no prey head for buildings with living roof survivors within 60 m, join a PILE on the door side (pileH = 1.7 sqrt(n) + 0.5, pileR = 0.65 H + 1). Mound mesh (half cone, tangled-bodies canvas texture) per pile. When pileH reaches the roof the building is breached: top 8 climb (CLIMB) onto the roof, later arrivals climb straight up. Roof zombies hunt roof survivors; idle roof zombies eventually step off the edge (FALLING). When a roof has no living survivors for 3 s the pile collapses (everyone falls back to the street).
- Fire (src/fire.js): GPU point sprites, teardrop flames (additive) and smoke (alpha), pools with free-list recycling. Building fires: overrun at breach (25%) or at collapse (30%) after 3-11 s; burn 90-160 s, spread to buildings within 2 m at 0.6% per 4 s, char vertex colours (city.char, undone by city.uncharAll on reset). Survivors on a burning roof jump (JUMP, die on landing). Car fires burn 50-100 s.
- Corpses (DEAD) lie flat, darkened. HUD has a Dead counter. Camera min distance 200 (Follow zooms to 230). Camera edge clamp margin 10 m.
- Test harness note: when stepping the sim by hand call people.sync() every simulated second; roof counts (bRoof) come from sync and piles collapse without it.

## Commander mode phase 1 (v0.4, 2026-10-25) - DEFAULT MODE
- Default page is Commander; ?mode=sandbox keeps the zombie-drop sandbox. src/game.js is the shared layer (HUD scaffolding, toasts, tap input, speed, fixed-step loop, follow camera) with a Sandbox class; src/commander.js is the round.
- Round: 3 outbreak sites (random walkable, 140 m apart, 50 m from the edge) fire at 3/14/26 s via people.infectAt(x, z, 3) (nearest civilians bitten, incubate 3-8 s). No markers, no pins: Sean wants finding the outbreaks to be the fun. Hints: a 911 toast naming the district (3x3 grid, North/South x West/East/Central) at each start, then every 25-40 s a caller from a random zombie's district.
- Alert: once a second count zombies per street block; a block with 5+ is overrun. Alert 1 any zombies, 2 at 2+ blocks or any fire, 3 at 5+, 4 at 10+. Never drops. Shown in a HUD pill.
- Points (RP): start 100; income 1 + humans/2000 per second (about 6/s); +1 per zombie shot. Police card costs 50, cooldown 20 s.
- Police cruiser (traffic.dispatch): BFS route on the intersection graph from the nearest city-edge node to the node nearest the tap, parks beside the tap (stopAt), or wherever it is after 4 s stuck within 30 m of the stop / 15 s stuck anywhere. Mission cars never panic, skip intersection reservations, brake for civilians but not zombies, drive at 14. dispatchTo picks an edge start with no car within 14 m (up to 6 tries) and new cruisers get a 2.5 s collision grace. Calm traffic waits at the stop line while a driving cruiser is within 22 m of the intersection (sirenNear); cruisers ease to 6 m/s through busy crossings. Headless: 26 of 28 cruisers arrive, the rest are wrecked in traffic (intended drama). Separate cruiserMesh (white, navy hood stripe, red/blue bar) with emissive flashing while any cruiser is driving. Wrecked cruiser = onLost toast.
- Officers (people.js state COP, kind OFFICER): spawn on the sidewalk by the car, post = car. Hunt zombies within 60 m (160 m with no leash once zombies <= 12), advance to 12 m at 2.8 m/s, shoot within 22 m every 0.6 s, hit chance 1.05 - 0.025*d (capped 0.95). Fall back (FLEE 4 s) when 8+ zombies within 6 m. Gunfire draws idle zombies within 25 m toward the shooter (fx 3 = alerted, chase speed). Officers can be bitten (keep shooting while infected, then turn) and run over.
- Balance from headless runs (3 cruisers per outbreak, resent twice): sent at 20 s the police grind 3 outbreaks down to stragglers; sent at 45 s they hold ~2 min then lose; sent at 75 s they are swallowed. That is the intended timing pressure.
- End: contained (all 3 started, t > 40, zombies 0, infected 0), lost (humans < 20%), holdout (600 s). Score = RP earned + 10/human + 20/intact building. Stars: contained 3 (>= 90% alive) or 2; holdout 2 (>= 70%) or 1; lost 0. Overlay #zd-end (needs the [hidden]{display:none} rule because it is display:flex).
- Figures (src/figures.js): merged low-poly person (92 tris: icosahedron head, hair cap, torso, 2 arms, 2 legs), one instanced mesh, per-instance aShirt/aPants/aSkin/aAnim attributes, MeshStandardMaterial patched in onBeforeCompile: legs/arms swing by aAnim (1 walk, 2 run, 3 shamble, 5 arms out, 6 aim, 7 aim+walk) using gl_InstanceID as phase; zombies have arms forward and head tilted. people.sync writes rotation matrices by hand (Ry(yaw)*Rz(tilt)). Zombie look = green skin, dulled clothes; infected skin lerps to green; dead = darkened. Rings/markers removed.
- Perf: 61 fps at 390x844 on the dev PC in low mode. Not measured on Sean's phone yet.

## Roster, zombie types, hunt aids, day and night (v0.5, 2026-10-25)
- Sean sent four reference sheets (civilians, zombies, police/military/rescue vehicles, jets, civilian vehicles). They are reference only; models are procedural box kits.
- src/figures.js: one figure kit (92 base tris + hat, vest, backpack, hand item props) with per-instance aKit (hat type, flag bits VEST/PACK/ITEM/BALD/GLOW, torso girth, scale), aAccent (hat), aAccent2 (gear), aAnim. Props collapse in the vertex shader when off. ARCHETYPES (casual, businessman, construction, nurse, student, homeless, shopper, traveler, off-duty officer) roll looks; OFFICER_LOOK; ZTYPES (walker 62%, runner 12%, crawler 8%, bloater 6%, juggernaut 3%, screamer 3%, skeleton 6%) with per-type speeds, hp, anim, tilt and look(). Zombies glow faint green (emissive via FLAG.GLOW, uGlow uniform; brighter at night).
- src/people.js: per-agent zombie type (zt) and hp; becomeZombie() on turn; kill() handles damage: shots 1 hp, cars knock walkers down or kill, juggernaut armoured; bloater pops (blood, scare, infects civilians within 3 m); screamer screams every 8-13 s (panics civilians within 28 m, draws zombies within 45 m); crawler is hard to hit (hitMod -0.3). People keep their archetype gear when they turn.
- src/vehicles.js: vehicle library. CAR_TYPES for traffic (sedan, hatchback, suv, pickup, van, taxi, sports, weighted) built white so instance colour tints; named VEHICLES for showcase/missions: police interceptor, Tahoe, SWAT Bearcat, fire truck, ambulance, HMMWV, MRAP, Stryker, M1 Abrams, helicopters (police, air ambulance, search and rescue, wildfire with bucket, Black Hawk; groups with userData.rotor), jets (A-10, F-16, B-2, B-52), city bus. Only the interceptor is used in gameplay so far.
- src/traffic.js: one InstancedMesh per car type (typeMeshes) for moving and parked cars; car.type/car.slot; tint() helper.
- Hunt aids (Sean: outbreaks were too hard to find): every 911 call (outbreak start, then every 15-25 s from a random zombie's position) draws a red search-zone ring on the ground (radius 70/55 m, centre jittered up to 45% of the radius, fades over 45 s) and fills the #zd-call strip with a GO button (g.lookAt at 330 m). Still no exact markers.
- src/daylight.js: phase 0..1 per round (commander t/600, sandbox clock/600): sun elevation 45 deg -> sunset at 0.4 -> night 0.5-0.82 (moonlight, blue) -> dawn 0.9 -> morning. Drives sun/hemi/fog/background, post night uniform (cool grade, more exposure), lit windows (emissiveMap from textures.js facadesLit/shopLit, intensity 0.75 at night), street lamp light pools (instanced additive quads at city.lamps), car headlight pools, zombie glow.
- showcase.html + src/showcase.js: roster page, one WebGL canvas with scissor viewports per tile, sections Civilians / Responders / Zombies / Police / Fire and rescue / Military / Aircraft / Civilian. Live at /zday/showcase.html.

## Commander phase 2: fire response (v0.6, 2026-10-25)
- Cards: Fire truck 80 pts / 30 s, Water heli 150 / 60 s, Rescue heli 120 / 45 s, all from alert 2.
- src/fire.js: bFire 3 = put out. douse(k, dt) accumulates wet, extinguish at 20 s; extinguish() ends flames, soaks neighbours (soaked[k] = simT + 150 s, no ignition or spread while soaked), stats.out++, event fireout. dropWater(x, z, 40) extinguishes every fire within 40 m, soaks all buildings in it for 200 s, big mist splash, stats.drops++. Mist pool (white smoke shader with uColA/uColB uniforms) for hose spray and drops: spray(x, z, y, n, spread, size).
- Fire truck: traffic MISSION_KINDS { police: cruiser cap 12 cruise 14, fire: VEHICLES.fire_truck cap 8 cruise 11 }, one InstancedMesh per kind, car.kind/car.slot. Commander.sendFire routes to the door of the nearest fire within 80 m of the tap, else to the tap (truck stands by). On arrival people.spawnFirefighters(x, z, 2): kind FIREFIGHTER, state FIREMAN, post = truck. firemanStep: every 1 s pick the nearest burning building within 110 m of the post, walk to door + normal*1.6 (detours), then face the wall, fire.douse and spray every 0.35 s; fall back (FLEE) when panicked, return to FIREMAN; can be bitten.
- Calm drivers (panic <= 0, incl. mission cars) no longer run over non-zombies: they brake to 0.4 m/s on contact. Panicked drivers still kill. This was needed because crews crossing streets died instantly.
- src/aircraft.js Helicopters: send(kind, x, z, {k, onDone}) spawns a buildVehicle group (heli_wildfire / heli_rescue, scale 2) 600+ m out, flies in at 30 m/s, cruise alt 48. Water: hover at 28 m for 2.5 s, fire.dropWater, leave. Rescue: Commander.sendRescue picks the roof with the most survivors within 90 m of the tap (bRoof), heli descends to roofY + 4, people.evacuateFrom(k) every 0.5 s (uninfected ROOF survivors -> state EVAC, hidden, stats.rescued), leaves at 12 or after 6 s with nobody, event rescued. Helis fly out and are removed 300 m past the bounds.
- Points: +30 per fire put out, +3 per rescued. End screen: helicopters sent, rescued, fires put out.
- Headless check: at a 325-zombie outbreak at 163 s, truck + crew arrive but the crew dies within a minute (fine, sent late); water drop put out 3 buildings; rescue heli lifted 12. Calm test: crew hoses a building out in ~10 s with two on the line.

## Commander phase 3: army and airstrike (v0.7, 2026-10-25)
- Cards: Army APC 250 pts / 60 s at alert 3, Airstrike 300 pts / 45 s at alert 4.
- APC: traffic MISSION_KINDS.apc = VEHICLES.stryker, cap 6, cruise 12, tough: never crashes (the other car wrecks), ignores pedestrians, parks in the intersection it was sent to (stopAt = len - 1). On arrival people.spawnOfficers(x, z, 8, KIND.SOLDIER): state COP with kind SOLDIER, SOLDIER_LOOK (olive, helmet, vest, pack), post = the APC. Soldiers: search 70 m, leash 45 m from the post, engage from 18 m, fire every 0.35 s within 32 m with accuracy as if the target were at 0.6x the distance, fall back at 14 zombies within 6 m (police: 8). Their gunfire draws idle zombies within 45 m (police 25 m), so a squad is a horde magnet. Grenades: when 6+ zombies sit 10-30 m away, one soldier lobs at their centroid; squad-wide cooldown people.grenadeCd 4 s; 1.5 s fuse, people.blast(x, z, 5, civilians=false) kills zombies only, puff + mist.
- Airstrike: Commander.sendStrike -> helis.strike(x, z, {radius 25}): an A-10 (buildVehicle('a10'), scale 2.2) spawns 900 m out on a random heading through the target at 120 m/s, 60 m up; releases a bomb (cylinder mesh) at lead = 120*sqrt(2*60/9.8) m before the target, ballistic fall; on impact fire.blast (fireball + smoke + dust, char every building within 25 m, 35% ignite) and people.blast (everything within 25 m dies incl. rooftop people; civilians count stats.bombedCiv, -5 pts each, +1 per zombie). Camera shake if within 500 m. The strike is marked with an exact red ring (report(..., exact=true)) and 'Impact in N s' in the call strip.
- Headless: an APC squad against a 10-20 zombie clump kills everything approaching within 30 m (34 kills / 90 s with few arrivals, 8 alive). Airstrike on a 24-zombie clump in a busy street: 24 zombies and ~40 civilians dead, 2-8 fires. Expensive on purpose.
- Screenshot trick: g.speed = 0 freezes the sim while the render loop keeps drawing; to frame a jet at height H with pitch 35 shift the camera target toward the camera by H*cos(35)/... (about 86 m for H=60, azimuth 45: target = jet + (-0.707, -0.707) * 86).

## Commander phase 4: nuke and evacuation bus (v0.8, 2026-10-25)
- Cards: Evac bus 60 pts / 30 s from alert 0; Nuke 1000 pts, once per round (UNITS once: true), alert 4, red-bordered card.
- Bus: traffic MISSION_KINDS.bus = VEHICLES.bus, cap 8, cruise 10. Parks beside the tap; onArrive people.callToBus(x, z, 45, 30, busId): the 30 nearest uninfected street civilians (WALK/IDLE/FLEE/RETURN) go to state BOARD and run for the door (tx/tz), 25 s patience, detours; within 2.2 m they become EVAC (hidden), stats.bused++, event boarded (Commander counts bus.loaded). Commander.busTick: parked bus leaves after 25 s or 30 aboard, or when 4+ zombies are within 10 m after 3 s (toast 'Bus swarmed'). traffic.depart(bus): routeTo(bus) reversed = route back to the city edge, mode DRIVE with leaving=true; on the last segment traffic.remove() parks it at 1e5,1e5 (gone=true) and onGone fires. Wrecked bus -> people.releaseBus. +3 pts per person bused; bused and rescued count as survivors for the score and stars.
- Nuke: Commander.sendNuke -> helis.nuke(x, z, {radius 150}): a B-2 (scale 4) 1100 m out, 70 m/s at 140 m, bomb scaled 2.5, ballistic drop. On impact fire.nuke (fire column, smoke cap at 40 m, base smoke, dust; buildings within 0.8R charred to 0.18 and set bFire 2 + soaked forever; 0.8R-1.15R 60% ignite) and people.nuke (everyone within R dies incl. roofs and INSIDE, bodies darkened, piles cleared, bCap = 0 so nobody shelters there; scare everyone within 2.2R). Game.flash (post uniform 'flash' whites the frame, decays 0.6/s) + shake. Exact red ring 150 m and 'NUCLEAR STRIKE AUTHORISED' call strip. -5 pts per civilian (RP floored at 0 so the cards never lock; the loss still hits the score through this.earned), stars capped at 1 when the nuke is used. End screen: Evacuated by bus, Nuke: zombies / civilians.
- Headless: bus to a plaza crowd: 30 called, 20 aboard in 10 s, 10 stragglers, left at 25 s, gone at 35 s. Nuke at the centre: ~1190 civilians dead, 185 buildings scorched, ~108 fires in the ring.
- HUD: 8 unit cards wrap to two rows of four at 390 px, plus speed and restart on a third row (bar ~150 px tall). Sean may want this tighter.

## Commander phase 5: progression and menu (v0.9, 2026-10-25)
- src/profile.js: Profile saved in localStorage key zday_profile { xp, spent, upgrades, rounds, best, stars, lastLevel }. XP = max(200, score/10) per round. LEVELS cumulative [0, 6000, 14000, 26000, 44000, 70000, 105000, 150000, 210000, 290000, 390000, 520000] (12 levels). UNLOCK: police/bus L1, fire L2, water/rescue L3, apc L4, strike L5, nuke L6. UPGRADES bought with banked XP (xp - spent): squad4 3000 @L7 (cruisers carry 4), drone 4000 @L7 (amber rings on infected before they turn: people.markInfected, marks InstancedMesh), bigbus 3000 @L8 (45 seats, 55 m call radius, 35 s wait), doubledrop 4000 @L8 (helis.doubleDrop: second drop 45 m further along the run), fastcops 5000 @L9 (police and fire cooldowns halved). difficulty(): outbreaks 1/2/3 at L1/L2/L3+, incubation [20,30]/[15,24]/[10,18], seeds 4 from L8, runners from L10 (30% of turns become Runners), police cd 15 from L5.
- Menu: default page (no ?mode) shows #zd-menu over the living city (Idle mode, morning light): title, rank line, XP bar, next unlock text, Play Commander, Units and upgrades (unlock list + buy buttons), Sandbox, Roster link, footer with rounds/best/stars. ?mode=commander or ?mode=sandbox skips the menu (Restart and the Menu button use location.href so modes never stack). Commander shows only unlocked unit cards (L1 = two cards, so the bar is small early).
- End screen: +XP, level, XP to next, Level up line with what it unlocks, Play again (reloads into ?mode=commander after a level up so new cards appear) and Menu.
- Tested: fresh profile L1 -> 1 outbreak, cards Police + Evac bus, incubation 20-30; a holdout at 0:59 scored 102k = +10,247 XP -> level 2, saved. L8 profile: buy squad4 works (bank 157,000). Max profile: 8 cards, 3 outbreaks, 4 seeds, runners, drone marks, double drop, cooldowns 10/15.

## Disasters, weather and Storm mode (v1.0, 2026-10-26)
- Sean asked to port the Tornado Alley tornado (better, cleaner, matching this world), as a random event and a playable mode, plus weather, earthquakes, floods, tsunamis, riots and alien invasions.
- src/disasters.js Disasters(ctx): owns Debris, Decals, Weather, WaterSurface and the hazard list; start(type, x, z, opts) for tornado / quake / flood / tsunami / riot / aliens (x null = random); destroyBuilding(k, 'tear'|'collapse') (people.clearBuilding, fire.gone, city.extractBuilding + hideBuilding, shell into debris lift or sink, rubble heap, dust cloud, crush); strike(x, z) lightning (tube bolt + glow, flash, may ignite a roof, kills in the street); events with .text are toasted by Game (Commander turns 'warn' events with x,z into a 911 report + ring). lightK / sunK = scene light level for the unlit shaders (water, wave, funnel, cloud puffs) so they darken with rain and night.
- Game wiring: disasters.update(h) in the fixed substep, weather.apply(dt) right after daylight.set (multiplies sun/hemi, fog, sky, sepia, exposure, snow cover emissive), disasters.sync after people.sync, disasters.onEvent(e) for every event (saucers listen for blast/nuked), game.shake decays 1.2/s and main.js offsets the camera only for the render (Commander used to drift the camera). resetWorld: disasters.reset() then city.restoreAll().
- city.js: dead[], buildingsNear, hideBuilding, extractBuilding, addRubble (3 big slabs + chunks, mound higher in the middle, cap 6000), takeStreetNear (trees, lamps), restoreAll. traffic.js: CAR_MODE EXT 4 / AIR 5, grab/fling/airStep (lands as a wreck, may flip, crush, burn), parkedList + takeParked/burnParked/geometryOf, burn(car), cars stall in water > 0.55 m (not APCs), speedK/gripK. people.js: EXT 19, AIR 20, RIOT 21, ALIEN 22, KIND.ALIEN, grab/fling/airStep, clearBuilding, crushNear, alarm(secs) (civilians head indoors/up), drowning (> 1.3 m for 16 s), startRiot/riotStep/arrest (2.5 s cuffing, rioter held) /gas, spawnAliens/alienStep (zap). INSIDE people now count as humans (a tsunami alarm used to make the Humans count crash).
- Weather (src/weather.js): clear, rain, storm (bolts every 3-9 s near the view), fog, snow (cover, cars slide), heat (fires spread 3.5x, random roof fires), sand. GPU precipitation in a box around the focus point, faded outside the sharp band. Weather fog extra is scaled by 430/camera distance so the haze at the target is the same at any zoom (fog and sand used to white out the screen).
- Tornado (src/twister.js): two noise-banded funnel shells (dark core, feathered silhouette), wall cloud + dust ring sprites, 140 instanced junk boxes, top leans into the direction of travel (base stays on the damage), reach 8+6*size, tears buildings up to maxH = 10 + 22*(size-1), damages taller ones, takes people/cars/parked cars/trees, scour decals, mild bolts. TornadoEvent switches the weather to storm and back.
- Earthquake (src/quake.js): magnitude 6.6-7.8, R = 70 + 95*(mag-6), collapses by distance and height, crack decal chains, shake, panic, crashes, gas fires, 60% aftershock.
- Flood / tsunami (src/water.js): one shader water plane (murky brown flood, teal sea; level + front line). Flood rises to 1.75 m over 26 s, holds 75 s, drains 35 s, sets rain, alarm. Tsunami: 18 s warning (alarm, exact ring on the coast), then a 12 m wave (shader strip, dark body, foam crest and toe) at 15 m/s flings everyone and everything at street level in its band, each low building gets one 45% collapse roll, sea 4.2 m deep behind it, drains over 50 s.
- Riot (src/riot.js): mob of ~35 marches crossing to crossing, recruits 2 every 5-9 s (cap 50), throws petrol bombs (cars, parked cars, shopfronts: 30% ignite) every 3-6 s; police arrest within 25 m when nothing hostile is near; soldiers within 32 m fire tear gas (people.gas). Ends below 5 or at 170 s.
- Aliens (src/aliens.js): mothership (scaled saucer, 230 m up, casts a shadow) launches 5 saucers; each cruises to a crowd and abducts (beam, people/cars rise and vanish), zaps (laser: burns, ignites, sometimes collapses), or drops 4 armed aliens. Saucers below 50 m take fire from officers (0.25/s) and soldiers (0.6/s) within 45 m, hp 60, shoot back 8%/0.5 s; airstrike within 32 m or nuke downs them. Leaves after 150 s or when all are down.
- Commander: HAZARD_LEVEL quake/riot L2, flood L3, tornado L4, tsunami L5, aliens L6; WEATHER_LEVEL rain/fog L2, storm/snow L4, heat/sand L5. One disaster per round from L2 (65%, 85% from L4), two from L7, at 2:30-8:00, 90 s apart; 35% chance the weather turns at 3:40-6:00. Warnings raise an alert floor (riot 1, aliens 3, others 2) so the right units unlock. Points: arrest +3, alien +10, saucer +500, riot broken +100. Contained also needs no active disaster. End screen lists weather, disasters, disaster deaths, arrests, saucers.
- Sandbox: Disasters button opens a panel (6 hazards, 7 weathers; tornado/quake/riot are tap-placed).
- Storm mode (src/storm.js, ?mode=storm, menu 'Storm · be the tornado'): player Twister, drag or tap to steer (pan off, pinch zoom on), WASD/arrows; camera follows. Score = value eaten x EF rating; mass target 12000 (EF5 at ~1:45 on a busy route). 3 minutes, 4 outbreaks seeded (zombies worth 15 vs 10). Ranks F/D/C/B/A/S at 0/30k/80k/150k/230k/320k. XP = score/20, best saved (profile.stormBest).
- Tested headless (dev PC): all six hazards and seven weathers run without errors; 60 fps in q=low with tornado + storm + riot. Not yet tested on Sean's phone.

## City layout, ragged tsunami, six more disasters (v1.1, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: loved the sandbox; wanted a more dynamic tsunami (splashes on buildings, less even) and a city that feels real (superblocks, triangle blocks, roundabouts), then solar flares, volcanoes, sinkholes, dust storms that sweep like the tsunami, cold waves that freeze people, and meteor impacts.
- Layout (city.js, planned right after the street grid, layout = { removed, rounds, diags, superblocks } exported with nid): one diagonal avenue corner to corner through 3-4 blocks (triangle halves: slabs clipped by the avenue, lots refined by halving until they clear it, leftovers become paved plazas with trees), 2-3 roundabouts (the first a 15 m circus with a fountain and a ring of trees, the others 6-8 m with a big tree; corners of the four blocks cut by the ring, lots inside the ring+sidewalk dropped), 4-6 superblocks (two blocks with the street between removed; 30% become a two-block park with crossing paths and a fountain, 35% a civic plaza around a fountain, the rest dense). Non-rectangular sidewalks are ExtrudeGeometry slabs using the walk material (white top, curb sides). Street furniture, parked cars, lamps skip removed streets, rings and the avenue; the avenue has its own trees and lamps.
- Traffic: nodes carry inset (IN 5, diagonal junctions 10, roundabouts ro+1.5) and round; edges follow the layout. Roundabout turns are polylines around the ring (island on the driver's left), pose() supports them. Don't block the box: a car picks its exit at the stop line (car.plan) and waits until that road has room. In a junction, gapAhead only counts cars in the same junction or on the way out. After 4 s held, drivers edge through pedestrians. 60% of calm drivers go straight. Platooning was tried and dropped (followers rear-ended leaders on curves). Result: no gridlock; 6-way junctions carry ~30-40 queued cars that plateau. Closed roads (traffic.closed, closeNear) are avoided by drivers and dispatch.
- People: walk grid adds superblock interiors cleared, ring = road, island and curved sidewalk walkable, avenue road + sidewalks. Walkers and corner crowds are pushed out of the ring onto the curved sidewalk (offRing). INSIDE counts as alive. grid0 copy restored on reset; block(x, z, R) for pits and lava. New state FROZEN 23 (icy look, civilians die after 18 s, zombies thaw at thawAll), fallIn(j) for pits.
- Tsunami: SweepFront (water.js) splits the front into 96 sections, each with its own pace; sections inside standing buildings run at 0.45x and pile up 1.4x, open streets 1.08x; light smoothing plus a slope limit keeps it one body. Wave and sea shaders read the front from uFront/uPile vec4[24] arrays. Splash (water.js): ballistic GPU droplets; bursts climb each building's facade on first contact, spray off the crest, splashes for cars and debris landing in water. Sea surface churns white just behind the front.
- impact.js: Hole (flat disc whose shader ray-casts into a shaft: strata walls, mud or molten floor, torn lip), lives in D.leftovers and keeps swallowing anything that wanders in. Meteor: 18 s warning with exact ring, 3 s fireball streak, impact: flash, shake, crater R 20, collapses within 1.5R, fires to 4.5R, people killed to 2.2R, cars and parked cars hurled, ejecta, dust ring, 3 small fragments. Sinkhole: 3.5 s of rumbling and cracks, then grows to 11-24 m over 7 s swallowing people, cars and buildings (collapse without rubble), muddy water spray, roads closed.
- volcano.js: 14 s of tremors and steam, then a fissure (Hole with lava floor), additive lava fountain, ash column, 'ash' weather (grey flakes), lava bombs (ignite roofs, burn people and cars), LavaField: 80x80 grid of 3.5 m cells, viscous flow (stiffens as it cools), buildings hold it back then burn and collapse after 14 s in contact, crust plates on glowing lava, kills/burns what it covers, blocks the walk grid and closes roads. 60 s eruption + 25 s waning; the field stays.
- fronts.js: Haboob (240 m billowing dust curtain on a SweepFront at 12 m/s, lifts 40% of people and 35% of cars in its path, crashes others, takes trees, sandblasts or topples low buildings, sandstorm weather behind it). ColdWave (blizzard curtain at 9 m/s, frost plane creeping over the ground behind it, snow and icy roads, anyone outdoors behind the front freezes after 5-12 s; ends with thawAll).
- solar.js: aurora curtains high up plus drifting aurora light bands over the city, blackout (windows, shops and street lamps off after daylight via Disasters.afterLight), transformer sparks and electrical fires near the view, 22% of cars die in the road, helicopters grounded in Commander, 911 calls garbled (bigger rings).
- Commander levels: quake/riot 2, flood/sinkhole 3, tornado 4, tsunami/coldwave 5, aliens/haboob 6, solar 7, volcano 8, meteor 9. Alert floor 3 for aliens/volcano/meteor.
- Tested headless: every hazard alone and six stacked in one L12 round, no errors; 60 fps at 390x844 q=low on the dev PC.

## Onlookers, nukes, dust-cloud haboob, solar wave, outskirts (v1.2, 2026-10-26)
- Sean wanted realistic crowds (roof refugees coming out of roof doors, running to the side of the roof facing a disaster and across when it passes, people filming disasters on phones), nuclear bombs and a power plant meltdown, the meteor to look like a nuke, a dramatic solar flare (a visible wave, a black-light-poster moment), land around the city (desert, forest, mountains, beach), and the dust storm rebuilt to look like a huge billowing cloud (Mad Max reference shot).
- Roof huts: every refuge building gets a stair hut with a dark door on the side facing the open roof (city.js roofStuff(need)); rec.hut / rec.hatch. insideStep puts people at the hatch, roofSpot() keeps targets clear of the hut.
- Onlookers: Disasters.poiAt(x, z) asks every event/leftover for poi(x, z) -> { x, z, danger, range } (tornado, tsunami front point or the coast while warning, riot mob, nearest saucer, falling meteor, crater/pit for 90 s, sinkhole, volcano, haboob/cold front points, mushroom, missile, plant) and falls back to a burning building within 70 m. Roof people (every 0.6-1.2 s) walk or run to watchSpot(): the parapet facing it, spread out, three rows, then face it; 70% film (anim 6, phone held up). Fronts give each building the nearest point of the front, so watchers turn round once the wave is past (tested: 1415 east / 156 west where passed, 3163 west / 90 east ahead). Street people: watchScan (1/6 of people every 0.25 s), WALK/IDLE/RETURN civilians within min(range/2, 160) m and outside danger, 35% chance, max 600: state FILM 24 (faces it, films, refreshes every ~1 s, runs if it comes within danger, returns after 8-22 s). people.phones InstancedMesh (HDR screens) for everyone filming; occasional camera flashes via flashList.
- Haboob rebuilt: BillowWall (fronts.js) = 720 (380 low) instanced lit billboards riding the SweepFront: 42% cauliflower clumps rolling at the foot, 40% towering billows, 18% big overhang puffs that sit in their own shadow; each churns upward and respawns; sorted back to front, depthWrite with discard so the tilt-shift blur sees a solid wall; sun light plus a viewer-side fill so the face is never a silhouette. Two darker curtains behind as backdrop. 'dust' weather (fog 5.5, orange-brown sky) once the wall passes the view point, sand later, clear at the end. Mostly comes out of the desert (east).
- nuke.js: Mushroom (fireball shader, shock dome, ground ring, 280 puffs: rolling torus cap that glows then cools, stem, base surge; drifts with the wind; 150 s; poi). nuclearDamage(D, x, z, R) = fire.nuke(cloud:false) + people.nuke(byPlayer false -> disaster stats, not the player's penalty) + traffic.nuke + collapses within 0.4R + Mushroom. NuclearStrike (L10): sirens, alarm, 20 s, missile streak, detonation R 130. Meltdown (L11): the plant's lights flash faster, steam bursts, escalating messages over 32 s, then plant hidden, glowing crater, debris, nuclearDamage R 170 at the reactor, a green fallout cloud drifting to the city centre at 5 m/s for 75 s (outdoor civilians under it 1.2%/s death, cause 'rad'). The commander's nuke now gets the Mushroom (helis.mushroom), and big meteors spawn one (R x2.6).
- Solar flare: a wall of shimmering light sweeps in at 70 m/s from a random side; cars die (22%) and lamps spark as it passes; post.js reconstructs world position from depth and turns a 120 m band behind the wave into a black-light poster (posterised violet/magenta/cyan/acid green with glowing edges, dFdx edge detect); the blackout follows behind the wave.
- outskirts.js: 3800 m terrain mesh around the city (asphalt plane trimmed to city + 36 m): biome weights by angle from the centre (east desert dunes + far mesas, south beach sloping into the sea, north ridged mountains with snow above ~180 m, west forest hills), flat apron near the city, highways out of the middle of the west, east and north edges (terrain flattened under them), the plant site flattened on the coast. Sea shader plane (surf lines rolling in). Instanced pines (4200 / 2000 low, thicker near town), cacti, rocks, palms, no shadow casting. Nuclear power plant: two lathe cooling towers with steam (outskirts.steam(fire) after every reset), reactor dome, turbine hall, striped stack, warning lights. Tsunamis mostly come from the ocean (south).
- Tested: every new piece headless; six disasters stacked in an L12 round with no errors; 60 fps in q=low at 390x844 on the dev PC.

## Commander incidents and Be The Disaster (v1.3, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: rename the game My Tiny Disaster; the Commander game should be random disasters that the player reacts to with rescue teams; the tornado Storm mode becomes Be The Disaster, where you pick a disaster and move it with your finger (saucers, wave, tornado, lava, quake cracks, sinkhole).
- Commander (commander.js): a round is profile.difficulty().incidents incidents (L1 2, L2-3 3, L4-6 4, L7+ 5) drawn without repeats from HAZARD_LEVEL (zombies, quake, riot L1; flood, sinkhole L2; tornado, coldwave L3; tsunami, haboob L4; aliens L5; solar L6; volcano L7; meteor L8; nuclear L9; meltdown L10), zombies weighted x3. The first comes at 4 s; each later one is due by a spread-out time (4 + k x 420/(n-1) +-15%) or sooner once the city has been quiet (no disaster in the list, no zombies or bitten, no burning buildings) for 12 s and 40 s have passed since the last. A zombie incident breaks out at diff.sites sites (1/2/3) 0, 11 and 23 s apart, each with a jittered 911 ring. Contained ('City secured') = every incident in, 30 s since the last, quiet for 3 s. HUD has an Incidents n/N pill. The round plan (weather, incidents, outbreak sites) uses Math.random: the people RNG is reseeded on every reset, so p.r() gave the same round every time.
- Be The Disaster (be.js): controllers get down/move/up from one pointer (MapControls touches.ONE and mouse LEFT are null; two fingers or right mouse pan, pinch or wheel zoom). Tornado = player Twister, camera follows. Sinkhole = a Hole that grows 4.5 m/s while held (max 38 m) swallowing and collapsing what it reaches, then blocks the walk grid and closes roads. Quake = the crack tip tears toward the finger at up to 35 m/s, a jagged decal every 7 m; buildings within 9 m collapse by height odds or catch fire (gas mains), people fall in, cars crash. Meteor = tap to call one (5 s cooldown, quiet, every other one throws a mushroom cloud). Lava = a city-wide LavaField (n 150, cell = city span / n) poured at the finger at 150 m3/s with an additive lava fountain; lava now sets buildings alight (also in the volcano). Tsunami = waves always off the ocean, speed 16; SweepFront.pull boosts sections within 220 m of the finger up to 2.5x (0.5 elsewhere, 1.6 for stragglers once 60% of the city is under); the next wave lands 16 s after one recedes (the old one drains 4x faster). Saucer = player saucer flies to the finger; hold still to beam people (3 per 0.3 s) and cars up, fly to blast buildings, cars and people below every 0.35 s.
- Scoring: stat deltas every 0.5 s (150 per building, 12 per death, 30 per abduction, 40 per wrecked car, 60 per building fire) x a per-disaster k (tornado 0.9, sinkhole 0.6, quake 1, meteor 0.45, lava 1.6, tsunami 0.35, saucer 3.5) tuned so a busy simulated run of each lands near rank A (65k). Ranks D 8k, C 20k, B 40k, A 65k, S 95k. profile.addBe(kind, score): XP score/10, data.beBest[kind]. BE_LEVEL tornado/sinkhole 1, quake 2, meteor 3, lava 4, tsunami 5, saucer 6.
- Gotcha: the URL param `d` is the camera distance, so the disaster kind is `kind` (d=lava made the camera NaN).
- Tested headless at 390x844: all seven controllers for 3 simulated minutes without errors, real mouse and CDP multi-touch input (one finger drives, the second finger stops it and pans), 60 fps pouring lava in q=low, Commander rounds at L1 and L8, the City secured path, the menu, unlocks and Sandbox.

## Gaits, zombie roster, real pits, black hole, boats, T2 nuke (v1.5, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: more natural walking and running (bent knees and elbows); glowing green zombies become radioactive zombies (much wider infection radius, mostly after a nuke or the plant blowing) and regular zombies use his sheet's variants including partial skeletons; the boats from his sheet rescue people in floods and tsunamis; water puts fires out; windows blow out when water or dust hits a building hard; nukes like his T2 clip (much larger, the bomb seen coming in, bodies to dust); traffic stuck in junctions; sinkholes should swallow cars and people (cars drive off the edge), things floated over pits; a mini black hole disaster that leaves a void; the Sandbox disaster menu should close once a disaster is picked.
- Reference images are in this session's transcript (jsonl line 2939: four sheets: civilians/zombies, police/military/rescue vehicles, jets, cars) and the nuke clip was a .mov in ~/.claude/uploads (read with ffmpeg contact sheets; ffmpeg is installed via winget).
- Traffic: idle crowds were placed on block corners that the diagonal avenue cut away, i.e. in the roadway; the car in the junction yielded to them forever and the queue backed up. people.offRoad() keeps crowd points off road cells. Cars held up by people who will not move edge through at 1.6 m/s after 3 s (car.pedWait) and the people step aside (collisions, v 0.2-2). Cars brake for the stop line when they will have to wait (exit not clear), not stop dead (rear-end pileups). A car keeps its junction (car.clearing) until its tail is 1.5 m past the box (a car stopped half out of the box was T-boned). 15 simulated minutes at q=high: no wrecks, no lasting jams (was 100 stuck).
- Figures (figures.js): limbs split at knees and elbows (limb ids 6-9 shins and forearms), shoes and hands; gait per anim: hip swing, knee fold after toe-off (cos(phase + 45deg)^1.5), arms swing against the legs with elbow bend, bounce, forward lean when running (pumping bent arms). New instance attribute aZed (bone mask, blood, burn, fx): BONE bits skull, half face, left arm, right forearm, left shin, ribs, full skeleton, no nose, all skin; the fragment shader draws bone (thinner limbs), rib bars, skull sockets, noise blood stains, charring with glowing cracks, fire flicker. FLAG.GLOW now only on radioactive zombies (pulsing).
- ZTYPES (indices 0-6 unchanged): walker, runner, crawler, bloater, juggernaut, screamer, skeleton, half face, arm exposed, bone leg, noseless, ribs exposed, skull face, spitter (spits acid 4-9 m, 6-10 s cooldown, 60% infects; green arc via D.spitFx), burned, skeleton geared (hp 2), skeleton clothed, fire zombie, radioactive (RADIOACTIVE, weight 0). people.zrad marks the irradiated: they turn radioactive. Radioactive zombies infect civilians within 6.5 m (5% per 0.5 s each). people.irradiate(x, z, R, p). Sources: the nuke's outer ring (12%), meltdown fallout (2%/s dose: 45% die, the rest irradiated).
- Real pits (holes.js + impact.js Hole): every city/outskirts ground material, decals, blood, bodies piles and scorch go through cutGround(): fragments below cutY inside a registered hole are discarded (max 8 holes). The pit is a lathed wall (shaft or bowl profile) plus a floor disc and the old torn-lip shader. D.holeFloor(x, z) gives the pit surface; debris, cars (traffic.groundAt) and people (people.holeAt) land on it. swallow(): moving cars traffic.driveOff() (keep speed, nose drops), wrecks and parked cars tip in, people slip in and fall (die at the bottom), bodies drop. Hole.setR wakes resting debris above it. Each building's roof garden is now in its vertex ranges (grass), so it goes with the building (the floating green squares).
- Black hole (blackhole.js, HAZARDS blackhole, Commander L11): black sphere at 15 m, accretion disk shader (doppler side), photon ring, post.js lens (point lens on screen: image at r shows r - Re^2/r, shadow inside 0.45 Re). Grows 3 s, feeds 26 s (Rh up to 3.2-4.5), pull radius 18 + 15 Rh: people grabbed (and dragged back if they run), cars, parked cars, street items, chunks of street, nearest building torn apart ('tear', noRubble) with its occupants; all spiral in (debris.js sinkStep for sink vortices) and vanish at the horizon. A shaft Hole (black floor, depth 70) grows under it and stays as the void; then roads close and the grid is blocked.
- Boats (boats.js, vehicles.js police_speedboat / patrol_boat): route in from a wet edge node along traffic.routeTo, wait behind a tsunami front, idle at the target pulling people out of water and off roofs under 13 m (3 per 0.5 s, cap 12/24, stats.rescued), then head out. Sandbox: auto every 12 s while flooded (boats.auto); Commander: unit card 'Rescue boat' (UNLOCK L2, 80 pts, only when flooded); Be mode: off.
- Water and fire: fire.carFires are tracked; fire.quench(depthAt) every 0.5 s while water is up: car fires under 0.45 m+ of water and buildings with 1.1 m+ at their walls go out with steam. D.blowWindows(k, dx, dz, power): glass shards burst out of the far face and rain down the hit face, building charred; tsunami (every building it does not flatten), haboob (55%), nuke (outer rings).
- Nuke (nuke.js NukeBlast, nuclearDamage now spawns it): flash 1.3 then post.js uHeat orange wash fading over 9 s, Mushroom at 0.7 R, a shock wall ring (fire at the foot, dust above) expanding to 1.25 R in about 3.4 s; as it passes: buildings within 0.5 R (and weak ones to 0.72 R) blown apart with debris hurled outwards, out to R charred, windows blown and set alight, beyond that windows; people within 0.72 R turn into ash statues (people.ashen: FROZEN with an ash look) that crumble 0.3-2 s later into grey chunks and dust (people.crumble counts nuked/disaster deaths), out to R burned, beyond irradiated; cars hurled and burnt, parked cars and trees thrown. NuclearStrike R 280, the missile dives in from just above the near edge of the view over 5 s and airbursts at 60 m. The commander's nuke (aircraft.js -> helis.nukeBlast, radius 230) uses the same blast. Meltdown R 230.
- Sandbox: picking a tap-placed disaster closes the panel; the Disasters button reads 'Cancel <disaster>' until you tap the map.
- Tested headless (390x844 q=low): every disaster 40 s without errors, all Be modes, a Commander round with black hole + tsunami + nuke + outbreak, 10 min of traffic with no wrecks, 60 fps pouring lava; screenshots in shots/ (pit-car, bh-3, bh-void, gait-roster2/3, zombies-roster2, radioactive, boat, nuke-2/3).

## Hold to grow: sinkholes and black holes up to ten blocks (v1.6, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: sinkholes and black holes should grow while the click is held, up to 10 city blocks.
- D.maxHoleR = block pitch x 1.78 (about 112 m on the 63 m q=low grid: a 225 m pit covering about ten blocks). HAZARDS sinkhole/blackhole have hold: true.
- Sandbox: picking one disables the camera controls and says 'press and hold'; pointerdown on the map starts it with { grow: true }, pointerup calls ev.release(); the Disasters button shows 'Growing · N m'; tap() ignores the release (noTapUntil) so no Shambler drops.
- Sinkhole grow mode: 0.8 s rumble (short cracks), then R grows at 3 + 0.09 R m/s (about 16 s to the maximum), depth 90; it settles 5 s after release or at the maximum, then blocks the grid and closes roads.
- BlackHole grow mode: holdT drives the sphere (Rh 1 + 0.4/s, up to 9) and the void (3 + 5.5 m/s up to maxHoleR); pull radius max(18 + 15 Rh, void + 45), tear radius void + 10, up to 3 buildings inside the void torn per 0.25 s; feeds 10 s after release, then collapses. The void is bottomless (Hole { void: true }: floorAt -400 inside, so what falls in drops out of sight). post.js lens fades out where it would sample off screen.
- Be The Disaster: the sinkhole grows the same way to maxHoleR; new 8th disaster 'Black hole' (BE_LEVEL 7, k 0.5): press and hold to grow, release to let it feed and collapse, then press again. Simulated 3-minute runs: sinkhole 69k, black hole 63k (rank A at 65k).
- Bug fixed on the way: an inline comment had swallowed this.fb (the pit floor size) in Hole, so pit floors were missing (sky showed through).
- Measured on the dev PC at full size: about 3 ms of simulation per 0.1 s (worst 15 ms) and under 2 ms GPU per frame. The Playwright window was throttled to 1 fps by the OS (occluded), so real-time fps could not be read this time.

## City palette, landmarks, alien tripods, every disaster playable (v1.7, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: building colours should look like a real city, plus a few really large unique buildings; alien invasions get ground units (tripods with bendy octopus-arm legs, heat-ray lasers, tractor beams, grinding people up and spraying bones and blood); every disaster should be playable in Be The Disaster in its own way, the black hole movable like the tornado.
- city.js: MATERIALS (brick, brown, stone, sand, concrete, white, dark, pastel, glass) mixed per district (MIX: down = heightAt > 0.55, old brick town, painted residential, industrial edges, mixed) by wallColor(x, z, fl, style); ribbon-window (style 1) blocks are glass/concrete; tall ones lean to stone/concrete/dark; each colour jittered +-7%. ROOFS gained greys.
- Landmarks: four whole ordinary blocks in the busiest districts, 140 m apart: 'spire' (glass skyscraper, three setbacks, spire to ~150 m), 'deco' (stepped limestone Art Deco tower with a crown), 'dome' (city hall: white stone, portico columns and steps, drum and green copper dome with a lantern), 'stadium' (round stands, canopy, striped pitch, floodlights). Built by tiered() as normal building records (rec.landmark = name), so they burn, char and fall like the rest; a paved forecourt with a crowd.
- tripod.js Tripod: lathe head (hull, band, hood, eye arm with a glowing eye, rear vent) at 40 m; three legs drawn as 18 tapered instanced segments along a cubic curve hip -> bow -> foot with a travelling ripple, plus six dangling tentacles; tripod gait (one leg steps at a time, 0.7 s arcs); a landing foot crushes people, flings cars, burns parked cars, flattens buildings under 26 m. AI: walks to crowds, heat ray every 3-6 s (a 1.8 s sweep along a 14-28 m line on the street or up a building: burns people, cars, chars and ignites buildings), tractor beam every 7-12 s (8 s, up to 24 people hauled up at 10 m/s into the head). Each person eaten is spat out of the rear vent: a red gore spray, bone-white debris chunks, blood decals 8-22 m behind. hp 140; soldiers/police shoot (as at saucers); airstrikes 80 + falloff, nukes kill; down: legs go limp, head falls, crushes and burns, the wreck stays as a leftover. Invasion drops 2 (opts.tripods) from the mothership from 16 s, 10 s apart; they're recalled (winched up) when it leaves. Commander: +800 points per tripod, end-screen line.
- Be The Disaster now has 18 (BE_LEVEL): tornado, sinkhole, zombies (tap = bite, 3-5 bites regenerating every 5 s; hold and drag = people.lure draws the horde to the finger; zombies made count 12 each) L1; riot (drag leads the mob along the streets, Riot.lead; the mob smashes cars, lights shops, beats people) and quake L2; flood (hold: water rises 0.16 m/s to 6 m, release: drains) and meteor L3; lava and dust storm (haboob with SweepFront pull, new storm when one passes) L4; blizzard (a 26-48 m freezing cell that follows the finger: people.freeze, cars skid, fires out, snow and a mist whiteout core) and tsunami L5; saucer and solar flare (hold to charge 12-70 m, release: a light column, people zapped, engines die, lamps spark, a black-light poster patch; 2.5 s recharge) L6; tripod (drag to walk, hold near it to beam, tap to sweep the ray; it also fires on its own) and volcano (tap to place, hold to aim a lava-bomb barrage) L7; black hole L8 (roams after the finger, slower as it grows, grows with what it eats and tears; at the end it collapses and leaves a void sized to it); nuclear L9 (3 warheads, one more per minute; hold to set yield 120-300 m); meltdown L10 (hold to push the core to 100% (it cools when released), then drag to blow the fallout cloud around).
- Score scales (k) tuned so a busy simulated run lands near rank A (65k): see BE in be.js.
- Picker rows: buttons no longer shrink (flex: none).
- Tested: all 18 Be modes and all 15 Sandbox disasters without errors, a Commander alien round; screenshots city-colors, cityhall, tripod-1/2, be-blizzard, be-blackhole, be-picker.

## Alien collision and the tripod glass dome (v1.8, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: collision for the alien ships and walkers; a glass dome roof over the top of the walkers.
- tripod.js roofTop(city, x, z, R): the tallest roof within R. Tripod: the head rides at standY() = clamp(roof under it within 11 m + 8, 40, 80), so it rises on its legs over tall blocks; towers taller than 72 m are walked round (the part of its heading into the wall is removed and it slides along it, picking the shorter way round once per building, side/sideK) and the head is pushed out if it ever ends up within 9 m of one. Feet: footSpot() moves a landing point out of any tower above the foot's reach (head y - 12); footing() stands a foot on the roof of a building at least 26 m tall that it can reach (L.gy, L.bk), lower ones are crushed as before; steps arc up over the higher of the two heights; if the roof under a planted foot is destroyed the foot drops. A foot landing on a roof makes sparks instead of crushing the street.
- aliens.js: clear(s) keeps every live saucer 7 m over the roofs here and 2.5 s ahead (and at least 3 m over the one under it); a falling saucer that meets a roof smashes buildings under 45 m down and falls on, or comes to rest burning on a taller roof (dropping to the street if that building later goes). spread() pushes saucers and walking tripods apart (saucers give way to tripods). The player saucer in Be mode keeps over the roofs too.
- Glass dome: a fresnel glass hemisphere (r 5.3) on a dark collar on top of the head; inside, the grinder: a heaving, veined, wet meat mass (vertex lumps) that churns faster the more it has to grind (gut), with up to 12 bone chunks tumbling round it while it feeds.
- Tested: a tripod sent straight at the 148 m skyscraper walks round it (closest 11.8 m, never inside, no foot above its reach); a saucer flown over it stays 3+ m above the roof; five saucers shot down over 20-40 m buildings all crashed through them; tripod wrecks clear on reset; Be saucer and tripod rounds run. Screenshots tripod-dome, tripod-game.

## Twin towers removed (2026-10-26)
- Sean asked to remove the two side-by-side towers (insensitive to 9/11 survivors). Do not add twin towers or anything that echoes them again. The fourth landmark is now a stadium (city.js stadium()): a 40-segment outer wall with arches, four tiers of red stands with radial aisles, a white roof canopy, a striped pitch and four floodlight towers; one building record whose 'roof' is the pitch (roofY at pitch height, no hut), top 18 m; it burns, collapses and resets like any building.

## Cold front: fires out, frozen cars, ice until the thaw (2026-10-26)
- Sean: the cold front should put out fires and freeze cars where they stand and people, with frost on them until it thaws; people die but people in cars survive until the thaw.
- ColdWave (fronts.js): behind the front, every ~0.9 s fire.quench() puts out burning buildings and car fires and fire.soak(k, 30) stops anything catching; moving cars freeze 3-6.5 s after the cold reaches them (traffic.freezeCar: stopped, tinted 85% toward frost white; driveStep returns while car.frozen) and parked cars frost over (frostParked). At the end traffic.thawAll() restores colours and they drive on (their occupants survived).
- People: freeze() as before; after 18 s a frozen civilian dies but stays standing in the ice (FROZEN with fx 9: counted dead in the census and in disasterCiv once; kill() skips re-counting fx >= 8). thawAll(): fx 9 fall as bodies (kill cause 'thaw'), the living walk again, frozen zombies wake. Nuke ash statues are fx 8 and the thaw leaves them alone.
- Be blizzard: freezes and frosts cars in its cell (some still skid and crash), puts fires out and soaks buildings; frozen cars count as wrecked cars for the score.

## Hold-to-size gauge, planet killers, storm chasers and cloud seeders (v1.9, 2026-10-26)
- Sean: hold longer for a bigger meteor, up to a planet killer that hits like two nukes; a gauge showing the size while holding; it appears on release; same for tornadoes, meteors, black holes, volcanoes, sinkholes. Commander: cloud seeders and storm chasers to stop storms and tornadoes; chasers call in more planes for tornadoes and spot aliens so the army calls in jets and tanks.
- gauge.js: SIZED table (meteor 6-125 crater radius, tornado 0.8-4.5, blackhole 2-23, volcano 0.5-3, sinkhole 5-112, nuclear 80-420 blast) with ring reach, labels (Fireball ... Planet killer, EF0 ... EF5+ monster, Pinhole ... District eater, Vent ... Supervolcano, Pothole ... Abyss, Tactical ... Tsar) and D.start opts. Gauge: a pulsing ring + disc on the map and a top bar; value = min + (max - min) * (t/5)^1.7 (5 s to the maximum); drag moves it; release() returns { kind, x, z, opts }. The camera pulls back while holding so the ring stays in view.
- Sandbox: every SIZED disaster (meteor, tornado, black hole, volcano, sinkhole, nuclear) is press-hold-release; the old live-growing hold mode is gone. Be: meteor (cooldown 3 + size/10 s), sinkhole (opens on release and widens to the set size), volcano (first press sizes it), nuclear use the shared gauge; tornado and black hole still start small and grow by feeding.
- Meteor: rock radius max(6, 0.32 R) (an icosahedron core inside an additive fire shell, flames trailing behind), falls from the top edge of the view over 3 + (R-20)/50 s. R >= 40: crater min(maxHoleR, 0.9 R), buildings inside vaporised (destroyBuilding { vanish }), swallow, nuclearDamage at 3.2 R (bigFlash), a second shock 0.35 s later, 160 ejecta. Planet killer: ~620 buildings, ~5,700 dead at q=low.
- Sized others: tornado size up to 4.5 (life 50 + 10 s per size), black hole Rmax up to 23 reaching full size in 6 + 0.3 Rmax s, void up to maxHoleR, pull capped at void + 90; volcano k scales vent, lava field (bigger grid), pour k^2, fountain, bomb range and rate; sinkhole Rmax to 112 with a longer give-way.
- Commander: Storm chaser (UNLOCK L2, 60 pts, mission kind 'chaser', a white pickup with radar dish and weather mast) parks where sent; within 280 m of a grounded tornado it calls a free seeding run every 18 s and an exact report; within 300 m of a walking tripod or a saucer under 80 m it reports it, calls an A-10 strike every 12 s and dispatches a tank (mission kind 'tank', M1 Abrams, tough like the APC; at most 3) every 30 s while tripods are spotted; tanks within 160 m shell the nearest target every 3 s (tracer, burst; tripod -14 hp, saucer -20 hp). Cloud seeder (L3, 100 pts): helis.seed() flies a light twin-prop plane over the target trailing seeding mist; on the pass tornadoes within 220 m lose 0.55 size (rope out under 1.05) and life is capped, a thunderstorm turns to rain and rain to clear. End screen: chasers, seeding runs, jets and tanks called in.
- Bug fixes: two more inline comments had swallowed code (BlackHole Rh/tick so sized black holes never fed; the blizzard snow colour). A scan for '// ... ; code' found no others.
- Tested: gauge labels and ring, planet-killer fall and impact screenshots, sized tornado/black hole/volcano/sinkhole/warhead runs (worst step 19 ms), Be meteor/sinkhole/volcano/nuclear with the gauge, a Commander tornado (one seeding + chaser call-in roped it out) and alien round (8 jets, 2 tanks, both tripods down), all 18 Be modes and 15 Sandbox disasters without errors.

## Roof flicker fix (iPhone)
- Flickering squares on roofs on Sean's iPhone were depth fighting: the rust roof patches sat 5 cm above the roof, roof-garden grass 6 cm (1 cm above the rust), and style-3 and landmark cornices shared the roof's plane.
- Rust patches are now 14 cm raised slabs, garden lawns sit in 32 cm planters, cornices stop 8 cm under the roof. The camera near plane follows the zoom (max(10, 0.2 x distance), main.js), about 9-22x more depth precision. Same RNG order, so the city layout is unchanged.
- Rule: keep any flat detail at least ~10 cm off the surface under it, never coplanar.

## Sandbox zombies in the Disasters menu
- The Shambler button is gone from the bottom bar. "Zombies · N" is the first entry in the Disasters menu: pick it and every tap on a street drops a Shambler until the 3 run out or you tap Done. A tap with nothing picked only shows a hint.

## Super villains (villains.js)
- Sean asked for three villains, pale white skin, all black, black capes. A first version with graphic effects on people was cut off by a safety filter; Sean then asked for a tamer version. Keep it tame: villains wreck cars and buildings, people scatter, get out of buildings first (destroyBuilding opts.spare + noCrush; people.clearBuilding 'spare'), or stick in webs; cars they throw land soft (car.soft skips crushNear).
- Blur: 62 m/s straight dashes through buildings (bursts in and out, small ones collapse), flings cars aside, bowls people out of the way; red trail ribbon.
- Tyrant: drops in with a shockwave, flies, lifts cars and buildings (city.extractBuilding shell) and throws them at buildings or cars; red laser eyes torch cars, char and ignite buildings, scorch streets.
- Weaver: six arms, drops on a thread onto a roof, swings roof to roof on arcs kept clear of roofs; web balls make ground webs (shader disc + guy lines) that stick people (people.web: FROZEN fx 7, silk look) and cars (car.frozen + car.webbed) for 45 s; acid at stuck cars only (car.eaten, dark rust tint).
- Sandbox: Super villains in the Disasters menu (tap to place); they leave after 200 s. Be The Disaster: Super villains (k 1, level 3), Switch button cycles who you control (VillainsCtl in be-more.js). A random-play run scored about 20k.

## Rebuilding, camps, hazmat, siren lights, riot buses
- Commander units (rebuild.js): Construction (L1, 100 pts) drives a crane truck to the nearest ruin within 90 m of the tap, clears rubble 6 s (city.clearRubble), raises green netting floor by floor with a tower crane (16 + 1.6 s per floor), then city.restoreBuilding(k) puts the building back exactly (fire.bFire reset so it counts intact). Refused inside radiation zones or holes. +60 pts.
- Medical camp (L2, 120 pts): ambulance; tents on open ground (campSpot: walkable, no building within 10 m, avoid roads); people.callToCamp pulls up to 60 civilians within 70 m into new state HELD (fx 0), bitten early are treated (stats.treated). Closes after 90 s once no construction is going on within 320 m and no zombies within 70 m; people.releaseHeld sends them home.
- Hazmat (L3, 120 pts): hazmat truck, decon tent, yellow/black cordon R 70, 20 s: scrubs people with a radiation dose (zrad + inf), foams fires (fire.quench), then D.zones.clean. Radiation zones (Zones in rebuild.js, D.contaminate) come from NukeBlast (0.9R) and meltdown fallout; drawn as pale green dashed rings.
- build and hazmat trucks are tough (push through wrecks) so they reach nuked areas.
- Riots: arrest() puts rioters in custody (HELD fx 1) at a holding point; Commander custodyTick sends a police bus (kind jail) once 4 are held or 20 s pass; custodyToBus walks them aboard; stats.jailed. No bus in 90 s: released (people step).
- Sirens (sirens.js): flashing light bars on every mission vehicle, boats, heli beacons, plus beacons at sites, camps and cordons. Additive points (bigger halos at night) and coloured street light pools at night. The old whole-vehicle red/blue emissive flash is gone.
- Commander unit cards now sit in one horizontally scrolling row (.zd-cards).

## Super heroes (heroes.js)
- Hero signal: Sandbox Disasters menu (Heroes section, "Hero signal ☮") or Commander card (L4, 300 pts, cd 120). D.start('heroes', x, z): a searchlight on the tallest roof near the view angles up towards the camera and paints a glowing peace sign on clouds at the top of the view (sprite sized to ~0.085 x its camera distance). Calling again while they are around just re-lights it.
- Dragonfly (four buzzing insect wings): cuts every Weaver web (web.release), then hits Weaver 3 times (34 hp each, knocks him off his perch). Nova (female, ponytail, strongest): grabs any building Tyrant throws and carries it home (city.restoreBuilding), punches Tyrant 4 times (25 hp) and catches what he drops. Tempo (glowing hands): time bubbles (D.bubbles, D.slowAt = 0.1 inside) ahead of Blur until he has been trapped 3.5 s; bubbles over thrown cars/buildings and tornado debris (traffic.airStep, debris FLY, Tyrant shells all honour slowAt).
- All three: incoming meteors and nuclear missiles. They wait under the target during the warning, Tempo bubbles the falling object, the nearest (Nova preferred) seizes it (e.seize(): phase held) and flies it up to y 520, then e.vanish(). Meteors R >= 40 are smashed first (glowing chunks fly up). Tested: normal meteor, warhead and planet killer all caught, no damage.
- Villains.beat(v, hero): v.leave() with a toast; the Villains event ends when all are gone. Heroes leave after 40 s with nothing to do. Commander.quiet() ignores the heroes event.
- Hero look: peach skin, white suit, boots and cape, gold belt and chest emblem (rig() in villains.js now takes mats, slim, longHair).

## Unlocks page
- Units are listed by unlock level with a one-line description each (UNIT_DESC in game.js); the hero signal row is "Super heroes (hero signal)" (L4).
- Super villains are a Commander threat from level 5 (HAZARD_LEVEL.villains) with a hint that only the heroes can beat them; each villain beaten is +300 pts.

## Menus, crews, roster
- Menus fit the visible screen on iPhone: #zd-menu pads by the safe areas, the box is a flex column (max-height 100%) with a pinned header (‹ Back + title) on sub panels and a scrolling .mbody. Be The Disaster list sorted by unlock level.
- Crew figures (people.spawnCrew, HELD fx 0, kind FIREFIGHTER, dismissed with people.dismiss): construction workers (CREW_LOOK) round the truck at a site, hazmat crew in yellow suits (HAZMAT_LOOK), paramedics (MEDIC_LOOK) at camps. Crew ids: sites 20000+, hazmat 26000+, medics camp id + 10000.
- Roster (showcase.js) adds firefighter, soldier, the three crews, the heroes (heroModel) and villains (villainModel) with animated capes and wings, a construction site with its crane (siteModel/buildCrane), and both tents. New trucks show up from VEHICLES automatically.

## Roster page
- Framed like the game menus: pinned header (‹ Back, Roster) over a scrolling list; the canvas sits inside #wrap above the panel background and below header and labels (z 2 vs 3), and each tile is scissored to the list's visible rect. Tiles minmax(100px) so three across on a phone.
- Disasters section: examples.js builds small animated dioramas (tornado, quake, flood, tsunami, sinkhole, meteor, volcano, lava, dust storm, cold wave, solar flare, saucer, tripod via tripodModel(), black hole, nuke, meltdown).

## Sandbox auto-responders (response.js)
- Responders (on by default; Disasters menu > Help > Auto responders On/Off) answer everything for free: fire trucks to burning buildings (4 out) and a water drop on 3+ fires, rescue helis to roofs, police to zombie clusters (6 out) and riots, an APC past 60 zombies, cloud seeding + a storm chaser on tornadoes, airstrikes on tripods and low saucers, hazmat to radiation zones, the hero signal for villains and incoming meteors and missiles. Once D.list is empty: construction crews (4 sites, nearest the view first, 10 s after a building fell, skipping holes and radiation) and a medical camp by the work.
- Shared with Commander: seedEffect, campSpot, custodyBuses.
- Fixes found on the way: mission vehicles that leave free their mesh slot (they used to cap each kind per round, 24 dispatches total); a departing mission stuck 8 s counts as gone; Sandbox units drive off once their job is done; people.setupBuildings sets b.t for every building with a door (small burning buildings crashed firemanStep).

## Emergent behaviour (people.js)
- Each civilian has a temperament (people.trait, hashed from the index so looks are unchanged): fighters (builders and off-duty cops 45%, else 7%), drivers (business 18%, else 6%), faithful (homeless 30%, else 8%). panic() calls react() first: fighters stand their ground against zombies (FIGHT, hand item shown, overhead chop anim 11, 35% kill or knockdown per swing, run if 4+ zombies close); drivers run to the nearest unclaimed parked car (STEAL) and traffic.hotwire() swaps in a same-model moving car from 150 m+ away that pulls out and races off away from the danger; the faithful kneel (PRAY, anim 10) in a prayer circle within 40 m (golden-angle slots) or start one, and others join a circle of 3+ (15%). Prayers keep praying through most panics, get up when no zombies within 30 m and no disaster near. Candles glow in each circle (sirens beacon). Toasts: pray, fightback, stolen.
- Pathing: a fleeing person who hits a wall now steers to the nearest open heading (steer(), 9 headings tried nearest first, 2.5 m and 5 m clear) instead of a random one; stuck fleeing ~1.5%.

## Frame rate (no visual change)
- Measured busy scene at q=high: 15.6M triangles a frame, most of it 9.8k figures (236 tris) and 7.7k tree crowns (480 tris) drawn twice (main + shadow). Now: people cast shadow through a 12-tri box stand-in on layer 1 sharing the instance matrix (the depth pass never posed the figure anyway); tree crowns cast through an 80-tri ball; crowns, trunks, tanks, lamps and road stripes are split into 160 m tiles so the camera and shadow camera cull them (city.js instTiled, at(k) maps item -> [tile mesh, index]). Result 6.9M triangles a frame (main 5.4M + shadow 1.5M), draw calls about 190.
- Shadow map redraws only when the sim stepped or the camera moved (renderer.shadowMap.autoUpdate false, main.js); traffic.sync and people.sync run only on frames with a sim step (positions only change then; limb animation is shader time). CPU per frame 2.3 ms -> 1.3 ms.
- shadow camera layers enable(1) is required for the stand-ins.

## Follow, hero timing
- Sandbox Follow steps through villains, heroes, tripods and saucers (game.subjects), then zombies; the button shows the name; the camera keeps it centred (ground point under the view ray) and game.focusAt makes the depth of field focus on it (main.js), so it stays sharp and the background blurs.
- Heroes never arrive (or start on villains) within 20 s of villains arriving (Heroes.holds); an incoming meteor or missile overrides. Applies in every mode.
- Be The Disaster villains: heroes are signalled 8 s in (arrive at 20 s), opts.brief makes them leave 6 s after the last villain is beaten, and they return 30 s after leaving; Villains opts.persist respawns a beaten villain 15 s later (respawn(i), KINDS). Fixed: Nova could never set a returned building down; Blur standing still in a bubble was never trapped.

## Brawls, worm, kaiju
- brawl.js (D.brawl): cars thrown at a rival (throwCar: ballistic with lead, tracked shots); the target reacts within 8 m: catch and spin once (0.65 s) and hurl it back, break it in half (Nova), dodge (Blur, Dragonfly), or take it and get tossed (hurt 18). Tyrant can laser an incoming car in half from 38 m (zap). Car halves: the car geometry with every vertex clamped to one side of x = 0 (cached per type), flown as debris. grab(holder, victim): spin, then throwThrough: tossed at 36 m/s through the building most nearly ahead (25-90 m), hurt 30. tossStep: flight with heavy gravity, smash() on entering and leaving each building (blowWindows, debris, collapse if < 30 m or 50% < 50 m, spare people), crater + dust on landing. Grip flag is gripped (Tyrant.held is his carried object).
- Villains: hp 780 (was 260; the attack lasts 480 s), pre() handles tossed/gripped/busy, foe() picks the rival (Blur-Tempo, Tyrant-Nova, Weaver-Dragonfly); Tyrant plans brawl or a car at the foe; Weaver yanks cars with a web line at Dragonfly; Blur throws cars from rest. Heroes fetch street cars to spin and throw (>28 m), 45% of close hits are grab-and-throw; Tempo beats Blur after 24 s trapped in all; held 3 s straight, Blur breaks the bubble (b.pop) and bubbles cannot hold him for 4 s (freeT). Villains.update keeps every villain that has reached the city (inCity) inside its bounds until beaten or leaving. Typical fight ~40 s: ~11 cars thrown, 7 throws through buildings, a few split. Hotwired cars now drive normally (car.panic 0) - panicked ones were mowing down crowds.
- worm.js: tunnels at y -9 at 13 m/s leaving a caved-in furrow ribbon (cutGround material, stays as a leftover); breaches in a 7 s, 38 m arc: a Hole (R 9, shaft) where it comes out and where it goes back in (swallow), buildings it passes through come down. Body: 40 instanced ridged rings following the head path, banded sand/dark; maw of 66 teeth. MAX_HOLES now 24. Be: WormCtl drag steers, release breaches.
- kaiju.js: ~45 m hunched rig (legs, very long arms, neck, jaw, tail, back spines), walks in from the nearest edge at 5.5 m/s, foot plants at the leg cycle crush (buildings < 45 m, crushNear 4 m, cars flung), wades through < 70 m, swipes, picks up and throws cars, roars; hp 400, airstrike blasts 50 (auto responders strike every 9 s), retreats at 0 hp or after 200 s. Be: KaijuCtl drag walks, tap car/building/open ground = throw/swipe/roar.
- Sandbox Follow also covers riots, the worm and the kaiju. Commander threats: worm L6, kaiju L8. Roster dioramas for both (showcase camera far plane scales with the tile).

## Worm and kaiju redesign
- skins.js: scaleTexture (canvas of overlapping shingled scales, tiled) and scaly(color, repeat) = MeshStandardMaterial with it as map and bump map. Used by both creatures.
- Worm body is now one continuous tube rebuilt each frame along the spine (fillTube: a ring of 22 vertices every 1.2 m over 132 m, frame carried down the body so it never twists, gentle rings, taper, a ripple, soft dark bands and a pale belly in vertex colour; triangles wound outward). Head: scaly collar, throat with two rings of teeth, three scaly jaw flaps (red inside, toothed) that ease open as it breaches and shut before it dives.
- Kaiju: every limb is a tapered cylinder with a ball at its top joint, ellipsoid feet, hands, skull, jaw and belly, all scaly, so no seams. Joints ease towards each pose (sync(dt) keeps an eased copy; sync(0) gives the exact pose for footfalls without disturbing it); folds use a gentle ease curve; the body rolls and twists with the stride.
- v3 (third pass): worm is always full length (its path starts prefilled LEN+18 m back underground), the tail tapers to a closed round tip (radius eases to 0 over the last 14%), crisp raised rings every 2.6 m with shaded grooves, darker back, slight neck swell, and the head collar flares into a thick lip (lathe). Kaiju: limbs are lathe muscles (bulge mid-length) with joint balls; digitigrade legs (hip, knee, ankle, foot), barrel torso with pale belly and 12 back spines, two small chest arms, long thin arms with three clawed two-joint fingers, 3-segment neck whose head is counter-rotated to stay level, bigger head (scale 1.25) with horns, brow and toothed jaw, 8-segment tail with a travelling wave. Rib hoops were dropped (they read as belts). Roster worm diorama is centred (offset -3, pit at -28, fit 42).
