A wind-up is a grab instead with odds 0.12 + 0.015 a level (+0.12 after a second wind), never twice within three wind-ups (noGrab): phase 'grab', the villain pinned 'grab' (the reel pose), a mash circle (.hold.mash) counting taps to mashNeed = max(3, 7 - floor(hero level / 4)) within 2.2 s (x slow); freed in time: hit(10), the villain reels, the spots resume near their end; too slow: hurled(): villainHit(14) and flight.hurl() throws the hero away from the villain at 34 m/s, the fight ends with the villain let go unharmed (not tossed) and its AI back (hitT 3 s so it can't hit at once). Second wind (secondWind, once, v.second kept across rounds): under half of hpMax (villains.js Fighter.hpMax), spots x0.8 time, wind-ups faster, a burst, a roar, a title. end() removes a dodge circle.
- flight.js: hurl(vx, vy, vz, why): state 'tossed' (tossStep: gravity 22, tumbling, the dive pose, plough through buildings on the way, damage still counts); down in the street at ground + 2.4 (or after 6 s): a crack decal, dust, a burst, a thud, people scared, the inside dropped, state 'down' with downAt so after 1.4 s getUp() puts the hero back on their feet where they fell (not a respawn at the edge: half speed, nose up, turned round) with the tip 'Back up! Press Lock on'. The down camera sits nearer for a knocked-down hero. Shots: a villain the hero is locked on, 30 to 220 m off, throws something every max(1, 2.6 - 0.06 a level) s (shoot: Blur a glowing blue bolt with a fire trail and a zap, Tyrant a chunk of rubble, Weaver a white web ball; SHOT table, shared geometries, a material each), aimed half a second ahead of the hero at 72 m/s (shotStep); within 3.6 m it lands: villainHit(8), a burst, a roll and a dip; gone after 3.5 s or below ground; cleared on spawn, crash and stop. homeIn takes the stick's left and right (or A, D) as a weave of 22 m/s sideways while more than 12 m out, so the shots can be dodged; the first shot tips 'Drag left or right to weave'.
- Bench (lab-fights.cjs, 5th argument a reaction delay in seconds: spots tapped that late, dodges that late, mashing seven a second from then): sharp (0 s) at levels 1 and 20: 18 of 18 won, 0 damage, dodges all made, grabs all freed; 0.7 s at level 10: 13 to 57 damage a fight; 1.5 s at level 15: 410 to 945 damage, 1 to 3 knock-downs a fight, still won (the hero's health at level 15 is 240 and the villain never heals: fine for now, item 5's fear and a villain retreat will change the ending). Screenshots: fightback-shots.cjs (the dodge circle, the mash circle, the hero thrown, up again).
- Six more hand-to-hand moves (HERO-PLAN item 3c), heroduel.js, each from the dazed state like the old four, each ending in thrown() (FLY AT THEM): jab combo (go_jab: three tap circles on a 0.6 s beat (x Tempo's slow), each tapped a jab of 14 with a flinch and a burst, the third a knock-back toss and 30 more; a circle let run out and they shrug it off with a short toss); uppercut (go_upper: 20 and they go up 22 m, a tap circle to fly up after them, then a punch for 45 that tosses them away at 36 m/s; no tap in 2.2 s and they fall back); ground pound (go_pound: a hold circle winds up (k over 1.6 s) while the hero rises, let go and the hero drops to the street: pound(): a crack, dust, a burst, a ring of force 12 + 22k wide that flings cars, bowls people over (scared, counted), blows windows, and tosses the villain for 25 + 45k); throw into a building (go_fling: the villain held overhead, a drag anywhere on the screen aims (aimAt: the screen direction from the villain to the finger turned into a ground direction with the camera's right and forward; an .aim arrow from the villain), let go and they are tossed that way at 40 m/s for 40, the hero turned to watch; brawl.js's tossed villain smashes the building they land in, as before; no aim in 6 s and they go the way the hero faces); hammer throw (go_hammer: the spin's finger circling, 1.5 turns instead of 2.5, then tossed outward at 46 m/s for 60); elbow drop (go_elbow: the hero rises 16 m over them, a hold circle (k over 1.4 s), then dives elbow first: a crack, dust, a burst, a ring, the villain left down 1.5 + k s, 30 + 60k). Poses reuse kick, upper, rise, hammerUp, hammer, dive. The dazed row keeps SPIN & TOSS and RANDOM MOVE and adds a row of round chips (.chips, one icon a move that is on, ICON) so a move can be chosen outright (pick(m)); Random Move draws from all ten. LAB.moves has the six (on by default), the lab panel lists them. The duel's pointer handlers: the hammer uses the spin's circling; the fling's drag aims. end() removes the chips and the arrow.
- Tested (moves-test.cjs: every move forced once at villain level 20, driven by the script): all eleven run to thrown with damage (jab 101, upper 91, pound 81, fling 56, hammer 84, elbow 76, kick 63, laser 73, drag 35, slam 211, spin 112 at hero level 1), no errors; the bench at levels 1 and 20: 18 of 18, the new moves turning up in Random Move. Not yet felt by hand: the fling's aim on a phone, the jab's beat.
- The stuck zoom (Sean, 2026-11-01: mashing a fight's circles with two thumbs, the page stuck at double zoom, the picture small in a corner, and a refresh kept it: iPhone Safari remembers a page's zoom). nozoom.js now also cancels a pinch while the page is not zoomed (a two-finger touchstart and touchmove, and Safari's gesturestart/change/end), which leaves the game's own two-finger camera moves alone (they come through pointer events). A page found zoomed (visualViewport.scale over 1.05, on load or later) gets the viewport tag rewritten three times with a changed maximum-scale (Safari takes a changed tag as a new limit; the same string again does nothing, which is why the old minimum-scale flip failed); if it is still zoomed, pinching is allowed and a pink line at the top says to pinch out. main.js sizes the renderer and the camera by the layout viewport (documentElement.clientWidth/Height, LW/LH) instead of innerWidth/Height, which on iPhone shrink to the visible part when zoomed: a zoomed page now shows part of a whole picture, not a small one. Checked in WebKit and Chromium at phone size: no errors, the two-finger events and the gesture cancelled, a one-finger move not. Untested on a real iPhone: whether the tag rewrite zooms it back out (the hint line is the fallback).
- The iso flying view (Sean: keep the view in iso for flying as well, to feel how it plays). flight.js: a third view, iso, in the cycle chase, cockpit, iso (the View button and V): the town's lens (FOV_ISO 18), ISO_DIST x 1.35 (x the pinch zoom) behind the hero's heading and ISO_EL up, aimed 8 m ahead of them, eased at 3 a second, the blur on (setView sets post.opts.dof for iso). Heroes start in iso the first time (viewSet), then the view stays as it was left; planes still start in chase. The hero model is shown in chase and iso (obj.visible is now 'not cockpit' everywhere: spawn, hurl, getUp, duelOver). duelOver puts the iso lens and blur back when that is the view.
- The combat animation pass, part 1 (HERO-PLAN 3e.1; Sean: the moves looked clunky, the rigs too simple; the uppercut should bend down, punch up, pull back and dive after them). clips.js: a pose names joints and angles, a clip is poses at times each reached with an easing (linear, out, in, snap, soft); playClip(r, clip, t) blends the two keys round t into the rig (after relax); the joints: body, bodyY (the hips dropped or lifted), torso (the waist), chest, head, shL shR elL elR wrL wrR, hipL hipR kneeL kneeR ankL ankR. The rig (villains.js rig(), shared by heroes and villains) gained a chest pivot above the waist (the upper torso box, the shoulders, the emblem, the arms and the head hang off it; r.chest), a wrist on each arm (the hand on it; a.wr) and an ankle on each leg (the boot on it; L.ank); relax() resets them; the smooth close-up body sweeps through them. Heroes: flight.heroPose plays F.clip when one is set (the clip's lean and hip drop are its own) else the old act poses. Villains: f.react (a clip with its own clock, advanced by the duel) plays over the held pose in pinPose while it lasts. Hit stop: game.hitStop seconds during which main.js runs the whole game at 8 percent speed (heroduel stop(s)). Clips so far: jabL, jabR, guardL, guardR, cross, uppercut (coil at 0, contact at 0.28 s, follow-through at 0.55), windup, dash; reactions hitUp, hitHeadL, hitHeadR, hitBody.
- The uppercut now (heroduel go_upper, _upper): coil (the hero drops in front of them, the clip coils; at the clip's 0.28 s the contact: 20, hitUp on the villain, hit stop 0.08, a small flash), up (they rise 22 m, the tap circle), rise (the hero up past them), wind (0.32 s of the windup clip, leaning back, fists back, knees up), dash (the dash clip, head first; 45, hitBody, the villain tossed down and away at 30 m/s and 16 down; the hero follows right on their heels with a spark streak until they hit the street), then the landing (a crack, dust, 10 more) and FLY AT THEM. The jab combo: guard clips wind each punch as its circle appears, the tap plays jabL, jabR or the cross and the contact lands 0.08 s later (the clip's contact key): 14, 14, then 30 with hitBody and the knock-back; hitHeadL/R snap the villain's head round; hit stop 0.05 (0.09 on the cross); the hero steps in to 2.4 m.
- The fight camera is now side on (flight.js D.cam: perpendicular to the hero-villain line, from whichever side the camera was nearer) and 0.18 rad lower than the flying view, so the moves read in profile (over the shoulder, the cape hid the coil and the punch). Pictures: anim-shots.cjs (the coil, the contact, up, the wind-up, the dash, the landing, a jab wound and landed) at pinch zoom 0.45.
- The combat animation pass, part 2 (3e.2): the rest of the moves on clips. clips.js: kick (the knee up, the leg driven out at 0.22 s, back to the guard), hammerUp (fists together overhead, the back arched, breathing; held while a circle is held) and hammerDown (the blow down, contact at 0.12), liftOverhead (the villain held up) and throw (a step, both arms hurled forward at 0.14), elbowUp (the elbow cocked high, knees up) and elbowDrop (horizontal, elbow first), land (the landing crouch, up to the guard), settle (the guard to rest, 0.5 s: played after every move by thrown(), unless a landing is playing), coil (the uppercut's crouch, held and deepening: the sky slam's charge) and punchUp (the uppercut all at once: the slam's launch); reactions blownBack (the pound and the smash) and crushed (the elbow drop, folded flat). heroduel: go_kick plays kick and the contact is at 0.22 with hitBody and a hit stop; the pound holds hammerUp and drops with hammerDown, blownBack on the villain; the fling holds liftOverhead and throws with throw; the hammer throw plays throw as it lets go (flight.heroPose now gives the hammer phase the spin's arms); the elbow holds elbowUp, dives with elbowDrop, lands with land, crushed on the villain; the sky slam coils (coil), launches with punchUp and hitUp, aims with hammerUp, smashes with hammerDown and blownBack, dives with dash (this.diving), lands with land. Every move tested (moves-test: 11 of 11 to thrown, no errors), the bench at level 10 clean; pictures mvclip-*.png (anim2-shots.cjs). Seen: at the pinch zoom used for the pictures (0.45) a hero 16 m over the villain leaves the frame; at the normal zoom it fits. The camera punch-ins of 3e.3 will handle framing.
- The combat animation pass, part 3 (3e.3). A reaction on every hit: each weak spot tapped snaps the villain's head (hitHeadL and R by turns, only if no reaction is playing), the laser folds them (hitBody held 1.2 s), the pound, smash, kick and elbow had theirs from 3e.2. Speed streaks (streak.js Streak: a ribbon through the hero's last 14 positions, wide and bright at the hero and fading along its length, additive, facing the camera, in the hero's colour; it fades out where it is when let go): on while the duel's dash flag is set (the uppercut's dive, the sky slam's dive, the elbow drop, the kick's lunge), while the hero is thrown, and in free flight over 55 m/s; made and freed with the hero in flight.js. Camera punch-ins: heroduel stop() (every hit stop) sets punchT 0.35 s, a weak spot 0.2 s, the spin's throw 0.35; the fight camera's distance is x(1 - 0.16 x punchT/0.35), snapped in (lerp 12 a second while punchT > 0.25) and eased back out.
- Figure detail and texture, Nova first (3e.4; Sean: more detail, and texture so they stop looking shiny and balloon-like). cloth.js: weaveTexture() draws a woven bump map once on a 256 canvas (32 threads a tile, warp over weft in a checker, a little unevenness), clothMat(hex, o) a MeshStandardMaterial with roughness 0.92, no metal, that bump (bumpScale 0.22; 0.15 on the dark boots and gloves) and a dimmer glow (emissive 0.22; the plain heroes glow 0.45). heroes.js kit: HM.look.Nova = cloth('#ff3fcf'): suit, boot and glove (dark '#2a1a33'), cape (DoubleSide, the weave repeated 5 x 9 across it), face material, fancy: true. villains.js rig(): with mt.fancy, extra parts marked keep (shown over the smooth body): a cuff on each boot and glove, a shoulder pad on each top arm, a collar on the chest, a belt buckle, eyes, brows and a mouth on the head. smoothBody: the body material takes the suit's bumpMap and bumpScale; with fancy the tints draw seams (the suit's colour x0.74) down the sides (|s| > 0.965), down the front (c > 0.985 above u 0.32), across the chest (u 0.56), at the glove tops (u 0.74 to 0.8) and the boot tops (u 0.675 to 0.72). Pictures: nova-shot.cjs (the hero's frame swapped for a pose-only one, the camera put 17 m off; 'nova2-nova-front.png' against 'nova2-dragonfly-front.png'). Still plain: Dragonfly, Tempo, the villains. Not yet seen in daylight at the fight distance; Sean to judge how far to take it (hair shape, a mask, an emblem shape, folds in the cape, knee and elbow pads).
- The other figures in cloth (3e.5). heroes.js: HM.look is cloth() for all three (cloth(hex, dark): the gloves and boots in a dark of each hero's own: Dragonfly '#15321c', Tempo '#132e36', Nova '#2a1a33'). villains.js vmats(): the suit and cape clothMat in the villain's LOOK colours, gloves and boots the suit's colour x0.45, a face material, fancy: true, so the rig adds the cuffs, pads, collar and face to Blur, Tyrant and Weaver too (Weaver's pads on his top pair of arms only); a villain with glowing eyes (Tyrant, rig eyes: true) gets brows and a mouth but not the dark eyes. Pictures: six-shot.cjs (the three heroes with the pose-only frame; the villains spawned beside a hero kept flying so the camera stays the script's). Bench at level 10: 9 of 9, no errors.
- The ranged powers (HERO-PLAN item 3d), heroduel.js, chosen from the dazed state like the laser (chips, Random Move, LAB.moves, the lab panel), each ending in thrown() except the shield: freeze (an icy beam a second, then a block of ice round the villain (ICE, a translucent box) for 1.4 s with 30, then it shatters into 18 white bits for 25 and a toss, the villain down 2 s); fire (a jet of flame from the hands, bursts on the villain, 9 every 0.25 s for 1.6 s then 12 and a toss; hitBody); lightning (hands up 0.35 s, then a jagged line (BOLT, re-jittered every frame, flickering) from the hands to the villain: 50, hitUp, hit stop, a flash, zap and thunder, then they drop); sonic clap (hammerDown: a ring 22 m wide, boom, people scared within 60 m (counted), windows blown within 24 m, cars nudged within 18, the villain 30 and blownBack and tossed); gravity pulse (up to 4 cars within 34 m grabbed and circled over the hero's head 0.9 s (rubble flung up if there are none), then hurled at the villain: 15 + 12 a car, hitBody); wind blast (dust along the line, 0.4 s then 20, blownBack, tossed at 42 m/s, people scared and cars nudged down the line); shield (a bubble (BUBBLE sphere) for 3 s: the villain throws itself at it every 0.8 s and bounces off for 12, the hero mends 6 a bounce; then back to the weak spots); telekinesis (the villain lifted and turned, a hold circle for height (k), let go and they are slammed down: a crack, dust, 35 + 45k, crushed). end() drops the ice, the bolt, the bubble and lets held cars go. Tested: 19 of 19 moves run to the end (moves-test), the bench at level 10 9 of 9, no errors; pictures powers-shots.cjs.
- The street-fight beats, part 1 (HERO-PLAN 3f.1), heroduel.js. A second villain joins in (partnerStep, rushEnd): every 6 to 12 s of weak spots or dazed, the nearest other villain within 400 m (not tossed, gripped, leaving or overloaded) is held by the duel and comes at the hero from behind over 1.1 s, a title warns, a red warning circle appears after 0.25 s. Tapped: the hero sidesteps, they fly past (tossed at 30 m/s, 15 damage). Missed: villainHit(12), a shake; the second miss in a round also villainHit(10) and F.hurl across the street, ending the fight. end() lets a coming villain go. The breathing mask: villains.js adds a small dark mask (v.mask, a group on the head) to every villain. Under 85% health, once a round, one weak spot is a bigger gold spot on the head (.spot.mask, 1.2x time); tapped: breakMask(): the mask gone for good (v.maskBroken), bits, glass, hitUp, hit stop, a flash, 40 more, and v.overload 6 s: dazed pose, no wind-ups, no partner rushes, every blow x1.5. The getaway (getaway.js Getaway): when a hero beats a villain a dark ship drops down over them (1.2 s), a beam draws them up (1.5 s), it climbs away (1.5 s), then state 'gone'; flight.js steps them and finishes them on Land. Tested (f1-test.cjs): dodged, two missed and thrown, gold spot, mask broken, ship and gone; every move, the bench at level 10 9 of 9, no errors.
- The rogue army (3f.2), army.js Army, on with Rogue army in the hero lab (LAB.army, saved), arrives at a hero's take-off, gone on Land. Two A-10s circle town at 240 m, 300 m out (well clear of the roofs) and fire a missile every 3.5 to 6.5 s, at the hero or a villain; two olive helicopters hover about 60 m from the hero at 90 m or more and shoot; six soldiers (boxes) on the street shoot at the first villain. Tracers one LineSegments. Taps (.c circles in #zd-army over the action): CATCH a missile aimed at the hero and it is thrown at the nearest villain; CATCH a helicopter knocked down every 18 to 26 s and it lands safely (missed: a dust-and-burst wreck, hurt +1); SAVE a soldier cornered by a villain every 10 to 16 s (3.2 s to tap: the villain tossed, 20 damage; missed: knocked flat, hurt +1, they live). Three saves (LAB.stats.saved, each -30 fear) and the army turns friendly: no more fire at the hero, guns and missiles on the villains only. A badge top left shows it. Tested: army-test.cjs.
- Powers against disasters (HERO-PLAN item 4), heropower.js HeroPower: on the hero's bar a Power button (goes round the eight powers that are on in the lab; Q) and Use (E), 1.3 s cooldown. Use works on the nearest disaster within 320 m (town-wide ones from anywhere). WEAK says what beats what (3 a big bite, 2 fair, else 0.5, with a hint naming the best one); each bite takes k x 7 from e.heroHp (100), so about 5 uses of the right power, 28 of a wrong one. Empty: finish(e) ends it its own way (a tornado's life, the kaiju retreats, a black hole collapses, the plant withers, a rift closes, a flood drains, a tsunami recedes, meteor and missile vanish, others run out their clock) and F.onStopped(e). Effects: a short beam from the hero, rings, bursts. Tested: all 20 disasters end, no errors (dis-test.cjs). Zombies already turned stay after a spill ends: left for later.
- The hero game (HERO-PLAN items 5 to 8), herogame.js. The menu's Defend and Destroy now start modes 'hero' and 'villain' (HeroGame); Commander and Be The Disaster are only reachable by URL (?mode=commander, ?mode=be). Save: localStorage zday_hero (HS), apart from zday_profile; per side xp, rep, skills, idx, towns, flyer, trophies; outfits by name. Hub (#zd-hub, The Tower or The Lair): level and XP, reputation, next town, team, wardrobe with a turning preview (its own WebGL context, freed on leaving), skills, trophies, a drawn tower (a floor a level, 12 at most). Levels: xpFor(lv) = 50((2lv-1)^2-1): 400, 1200, 2400... to 20; one point a level. Start moves spin, kick, laser, drag, fire, wind; TREE (Strength, Speed, Energy, Control), each branch in order, opens the rest. A town: WAVES by the road index, LAB.hero = level, LAB.villain = idx + 1, LAB.moves from the skills, spotK and toughK from stats, the army sometimes; LAB is put back after (labWas). flight.heroGame stops the stock villains spawning. Defend: stop all (powers or Lock on). Destroy: bring down 8 + 4 idx buildings and beat the heroes who come (Tyrant's AI in hero looks: o.rivals, V.rivalName, no mask). Three knock-downs or Land ends it; the tally: XP, fear, reputation (rep falls fear/30, +6 on a win; under 15: Villain, hunted by the other heroes; lose that and rep +15). Towns remember fear above 200: a tip and a scared crowd. Outfits: heroRig(name, of) and villainRig(name, of), of.suit, cape, dark, noCape, hood, plates, coat. Tag team (heroduel go_tag): a teammate streaks in for 70. flight's iso view drops the blur above 30 m. Tested: hg-test.cjs, wk.cjs (WebKit). Not built: Watch betting; a mirrored fight; three-rank skills; outfit unlocks; towns locked behind a power; a real 3D tower; the Commander level line on the menu is stale. Armour is plain boxes.
- The fight test (Sean, after playing the hero game: rough, hard to track, not reactive; nail one fight with no circles), fighttest.js FightTest, a Sandbox card. Control option 1 of 4 (Sean: try 1 first). Nova vs Tyrant, free bodies (class Body: own state machine, not the villains' AI or the duel), a raised bare plaza (buildings within 135 m hidden, kept alive, street items taken; Leave reloads the town via travel), a fixed iso angle (fov 28 on a portrait screen, 18 otherwise), the distance eased to keep both in view. Input on #zd-fight: tap, swipe over 26 px, hold over 220 ms. Combo jab, jab, cross (0.6 s window, buffered); tap, tap, hold: uppercut, then a tap while they are up: slam; hold from rest: heavy, charged, breaks a block; a swipe dodges that way; a dodge within 0.28 s of a blow gives 0.95 s of counter window with slow motion. A blow lands only if the bodies are within reach at the clip's contact time, else it whiffs. Tyrant: walks round, closes; jab, grab (tap 5 times in 1.15 s to break free), charge; red marks on the ground warn (a disc, a strip); any hit in the wind-up stops it, except the armoured attack straight after getting up (only heavy or counter). Blocks 45% of the attacks begun while idle: the hero staggers, Tyrant jabs back fast. Poses are clips (clips.js) eased 0.08 s from the last pose, feet snapped to the ground. Bots (ft-bots.cjs): a masher wins with 36 to 52 of 100 health in about 24 s, a good player with 36 to 84 in 31 to 41 s. Hand feel untested; the numbers are at the top of the file (ATK).
- Fight test, more moves (Sean: not bad; add the spin toss; knock them down, dash in, take the feet, spin or smash them over the head from side to side). Tap, hold (after one jab): seize; beats a block; the villain swings out from the hero's hands (Body.bodyX, C.swung), speed rises by itself and with the finger circling; let go and they fly straight out from where they are in the swing, harder the more turns (3 turns at most counted: 15 to 55 damage). Knocked down: a tap dashes in (ATK.ankle) and holds the feet (states ankles, pinned), 1.7 s to choose or they kick free. Tap: smash (0.36 s swing from A0 in front through overhead to A1 behind; the body one straight line with the arms, Body.armsA and bodyX, swingPlace; 12, 14, 16 damage; the third and they kick free; a grab only every 10 s; armour after getting up now lasts through the swing, since the first version let a masher win untouched in 11 s). Hold: the same spin and toss. The instructions fade after 12 s. Tests: spin-test.cjs, ankle-test.cjs.
- Fight test, powers (Sean: try the powers). A blue energy bar under Nova's health (starts at 30, top 100): a hit +8, a knock +12, a dodge in time +25, a smash +6, a toss +8, a slam +10, a blow on armour +3, being hit +4. Two round buttons bottom right (keys 1, 2), lit when affordable, paid only if the power can work. Freeze (50): a beam, a block of ice for 3 s; Tyrant can't act; jabs land in full and keep filling the bar, a knock, launch or grab shatters it for 15 more. Lightning (35): a bolt from the sky, 30 damage, stops anything including the armoured attack and a block, shatters ice, frees you from a grab, then a 0.8 s stun. Bots: a masher (no powers) wins with 56 to 72 of 100; a good player using them with 100 of 100 in about 22 s: probably too strong, for Sean to judge. Test: pw-test.cjs. Fire, shield, gravity, wind and a per-hero choice of two: not yet.
- Fight test, the powers settled (Sean: pick two powers, one stun with no damage for a second or two, one knockback with minimal damage like the laser; everything else hand to hand or grabs). Freeze: 50 energy, no damage, 1.8 s (hits on the ice still count, a knock shatters it, no bonus). Laser replaces lightning: 35 energy, 6 damage, a shove of about 9 m on their feet (state pushed, not a knockdown), stops anything, frees you from a grab. Picking from several powers per slot: not yet, only these two exist. The neck drag: hold on a knocked-down Tyrant: ATK.neck, states drag and dragged; hauled along at 21 m/s, 16 damage a second, steered by the finger (3.4 rad/s), a trench of scars (70 at most, cleared on restart) and chips; let go, 2.8 s up or the plaza's edge: flung on for 10. Feet and neck share the 10 s cooldown. Six small buildings on the plaza's far side (buildBoxes, the camera's side kept clear): fighters are pushed round them; anyone driven through (drag, flung, pushed, Nova dragging) breaks one for 6 to 16. Test: drag-test.cjs.
- Fight test, the kit (Sean's shape for building a character later, dozens to choose from in each slot): 2 powers (stun, knockback), 2 melee attacks (tap combo, hold heavy), a block, 2 grabs (spin and toss; the feet and smash), 2 finishers (the neck drag; the uppercut to the sky, punched back down). Finishers work only under a quarter health (hurtV says FINISH THEM, Tyrant's bar turns gold, the hint changes), and only they can kill: other damage stops at 1 health (fin, killBlow). Uppercut needs the tap to slam; the neck drag needs Tyrant down, no cooldown. Block: a held button (blockOn, blockOff, state block): a fifth of a jab or charge; raised under 0.22 s before it lands, a parry: nothing taken, 18 energy, Tyrant stumbles; grabs go through it. Dodge is kept. Bots: a masher now loses (Tyrant left at 1 health, about 50 s); the good bot wins at 100 of 100 in about 23 s, so a real player's balance is still to find. Test: kit-test.cjs.
- Fight test, finishers made obvious (Sean: at no health they stand dazed and wobbly and you choose how to finish with two buttons picked before the fight; once chosen you must complete it). Health now stops at 0 (hurtV); as soon as Tyrant is on his feet with none (idle, a stumble, a getup...) daze(): state dazed, C.stumble with a sway added after the pose (wobble), the bar gold, FINISH THEM, two gold buttons (.fin, data-f). Taps then say PICK A FINISHER; finishable() is now dazed. The button pointer is put in ptrs as held, so the screen handlers steer and release it. Neck (finPick, finRun to them, ATK.neck on a dazed Tyrant): dragged while held, up to 14 s, turned in from the edge, let go (or a quick tap: 0.9 s) flings and kills. Sky: finRun, skyCharge while held (k1, 1.6 s: launch 18 to 40 m/s, 7 to 33 m), TAP to fly up past them (skyRise; untapped, it happens by itself at the top), both hang in slow motion (finSlow 0.35, 0.9 s or auto), HOLD the grab (k2, 1.5 s: dive speed), release to dive (finSlow 0.3, Tyrant held under), HOLD the shockwave (k3, 1.8 s: the fall crawls while held), let go or full: skyDrop then skyHit: rings, debris, a dust column over 0.5, every small building within the radius (5 + 5 k2 + 28 k3 m), a dark disc, MINI NUKE at over 0.85, YOU WIN 1.6 s later. The old quarter-health gating, the tap-tap-hold uppercut and the tap slam are gone. Block costs a third of the power you have on the press and 10 a second held, none left and you cannot block; a dash costs 12 (DASH_COST); the bar starts at 40. Camera: look rises 0.8 of the fighters' height and the distance 3% a metre, so the sky finisher stays in frame; a small building between the camera and either fighter (hidden(), 13 samples a segment) swings the azimuth round in 0.3 rad steps until clear (azTo, 2.2 a second), home (AZ0) when clear again. Bots: the masher wins with 56 to 72 health in 24 to 35 s now the neck drag only needs a press; the good bot 100 of 100 in 32 to 34 s. Tests: fin-test.cjs (real presses on both buttons, the quick tap on each, the costs), cam-test.cjs.
- Fight test, Tyrant's finishers on Nova (Sean: if the enemy wins, they do finishers on the player too). Her health stops at 0 (hurtH: hDazed, TYRANT FINISHES YOU, her bar red); input is ignored; once she is on the ground she stands dazed with the same wobble; Tyrant, as soon as he is on his feet (vfStart, from a grab, a block or a charge she is dropped first), walks up and does one at random: the neck (vdrag 3 s, turned in from the edge, through the small buildings, then flung: FINISHED, KNOCKED OUT 0.5 s after she lands) or the sky (vcharge 0.9 s, vpunch launches her 34 m/s, she hangs at the top in half time, he flies up (vrise), grabs (vgrab 0.7 s), dives at 60 m/s in slow motion (vdive) and vfHit: a 16 m wave, rings, debris, every small building in reach, a dark disc, KNOCKED OUT 1.6 s on). No input from the player during his finisher. Test: vfin-test.cjs.
- The horde (Sean: dabble in the disaster fighting by fighting zombies: easy, small, hundreds at a time, a bite does a little damage, punch and keep moving, they explode into dust, hard not to get bitten): horde.js Horde, a Sandbox card under Fight test (data-horde), a pilot like the fight test (main.js). Nova on foot in the town's own streets (snapWalkable from the centre; a step off the street is taken back: move), the fight test's Body, C poses and CSS (now exported), the same steady iso camera at 36 m with the swing round any town building between it and her (hidden over the buildings within 70 m, roofY). 300 zombies (N0) rained down round her 12 to 48 m out over 9 s (people.drop with a rolled type; a restart drops the same slots again: drop(x, z, type, slot), since slots are never recycled and only 320 spare exist). people.heroTg {x, z, R 90, bites}: in zombieStep every zombie within R goes for her over any civilian (bt and target cleared), bites when within biteR + 0.7 (t1 = biteT + recover, bites++, the Horde reads the count and takes 2.5 (BITE) a bite, a red ring, BITTEN!); blocked by a wall it wanders at a run 1.5 to 3.5 s (fx 3, wt) and never piles at a door meanwhile. Tap: the combo (jabL, jabR, cross; ATK with reach and arc), faced at the nearest zombie within 11 m and lunged at when out of reach; everyone in the arc is dust (people.vanish: EVAC, hidden; 4 grey chips and a ring each, n DUSTED). Swipe: a 27 m/s dash, anyone within 1.5 m of the path dusted. Hold: the slam, 5.5 m all round. Civilians are not hurt by her. Bars: her health and ZOMBIES left (counted every 0.4 s among the dropped, states turning, zombie, falling; the run-over are gone too). Ends: STREET CLEARED with dusted, seconds and bites, or OVERRUN; Again re-drops. Leave reloads the town. Standing still she is eaten in about 20 s (40 bites); the bot (punch, slam when 6 are near, dash off after bites) clears 300 in about 146 s with 78 health and 9 bites. Zombies arrive at walking pace from up to 48 m, so the first half minute is a trickle. No powers, block or energy in the horde yet. Tests: horde-test.cjs, horde-why.cjs.
- Heroes and villains the size of a person (Sean): villains.js S (the rig scale) 2.2 to 0.92, exported; HIP in flight.js and heroduel.js follows it. The fight test and the horde had every body height and body-to-body offset shrunk by the same ratio (size-patch.cjs: x.y + N, top + N, fx * N, the swing, the ice block), the separation to 0.9 m, reaches to about 0.6 of before (jabs 1.9 m, heavy 2.1, counter 2.3; Tyrant jabs under 2.1 m, grabs under 1.9, circles at 3.4, charges from 4.6), the warning marks and the charge strip smaller, the fight camera in to 20 m at least (was 38), the horde camera 26 m (was 36); the horde punches kept wider (2.9 and 3.3 m, the slam 5) and a bite needs biteR + 0.4 (was 0.7), since the small Nova was being eaten (the bot clears 280 of 300 in 150 s with 85 health; before the widening it was overrun in 37 s). The stock villains and the old circle fights (heroduel, lab) run on the same small rigs and were not retuned beyond HIP. Bots after the rescale: the masher wins with 64 to 80, the good bot 100 of 100.
- The game's own iso camera (flight.js, the view heroes start in) swings round a building (Sean): swing(dt, at, b0) and hidden(c) as in the fight test, over the town buildings within 70 m of the hero, their roofY; azOff eases to the first clear angle in 0.3 rad steps either side, and home when clear. Not in the duel camera or the chase view. Test: swing-dbg.cjs (the hero held on a street behind a 25 m building: the view comes round in about 2 s).
- The see-through eye button is gone and see-through stays off (see.js user false, no button). The sound, music and radio buttons are off the screen in play: they sit in the pause panel (#zd-paused, now a small panel with a row, above the canvas, taps on it do not unpause) and move back to the side bar when play goes on, where they show only on the main menu (ui.js). Test: ui-test.cjs.
- 10b, the zombie level and the whole kit on zombies (Sean: Defend's first level is a zombie level, about 20 people and a few cops, the rest zombies; laser them; finishers on them after one hit; a tossed zombie bursts the others; the sky finisher clears a wide circle). horde.js rewritten round the fight test's kit. Held or flying zombies are people.js EXT (st 19, wz 1, t1 999: the system leaves them alone; take(j)), put back to ZOMBIE before they are dusted (vanish). Tap: the combo; jabs dust everyone in their arc, the cross (ATK.cross.daze) leaves its nearest one standing dazed 4 s (dazedJ, t1 999, a gold ring, the hint hidden, the two gold finisher buttons). Tap, hold after one jab: seize the nearest within 3.1 m (spin, the zombie carried round at 1.1 m, swept through the others: dust), let go to toss it (flyAdd: a ballistic flight, fx 2 so it tumbles; 12 to 32 m/s by the turns), bursting itself and everyone within 3 to 5 m where it lands (burst). Hold: the slam (5 m). Swipe: the dash (1.6 m either side). Laser (35): a 16 m line, everyone within 1.3 m of it; freeze (50): everyone within 5.5 m stunned 3.2 s (t1); block as the fight test (a bite on a block takes a fifth). Finishers on the dazed one: finRun to it; the neck (ATK.neck): dragged at 14 m/s behind her, steered, a scar line, everyone within 1.6 m of it dusted, up to 6 s or let go: flung 18 m/s, bursting 4 m where it lands; the sky: the same four beats as the fight test (hold k1: launched 18 to 40 m/s; tap or the top: fly up, hang in slow motion; hold k2; the dive; hold k3; skyHit: everyone within 5 + 5 k2 + 25 k3 m, the rings, the column, a dark disc). Energy: 30 to start, 1.5 a dust, 2 a bite. The level (herogame.js zombieLevel; WAVES[0] is zombies): every civilian but 20 near the centre is hidden (people.hide, the slots pooled), 4 officers spawned, 400 zombies (plus 100 a town up to 700) dropped from the pool over 12 s; Horde started with level: true (no Restart or Leave, onEnd), the rep box shows the count; cleared: TOWN SAVED, 300 XP + a dust each (+100 under 10 bites), reputation +6; overrun: 20 + a quarter. The hero game's update and finish branch on round.zombie. Bots: the horde bot (punch, slam, dash off) dusts 212 of 300 in 150 s with 78 health; it never uses the grabs or finishers. Known: tree crowns are not in the camera's hidden() test, so a street under trees can hide her; the level starts whenever the town's clock says, night included. Tests: kit2-test.cjs (every move with real input, then the level started from ?mode=hero, 16 civilians and 4 cops left, cleared to TOWN SAVED), sky-dbg.cjs.
- 10c, the kit (Sean: more powers, melee, stuns and knockbacks; a knockback held costs more power and sends them further, the laser kept on them as they fly, a kick wound up before it lands). kit.js: STUNS (freeze; sonic clap, 40: a stagger of 1.5 s and a shove, no harm; gravity pin, 45: pinned flat 2.4 s, every hit lands, a purple disc), KNOCKS (laser 35, +12 a second held; flying kick 25, +14 a second wound up: 14 to 50 m/s, 6 to 13 up, WAY BACK; gust 30, +10 a second: carried off at 24 m/s, no harm), MELEES (jab, jab, cross; hammer fists: two slow heavy blows, 18 and 22, the second knocks them down, both break a block; flurry: four quick blows the last a cross). loadKit() and saveKit() keep one pick a slot in localStorage zday_kit (default freeze, laser, combo). The picker: three rows on the Sandbox's Fight test card (Sandbox.kitHtml, data-kit), the chosen one lit with its blurb. The fight test and the horde read the kit when their UI is built (COMBO becomes the melee slot's list, this.COST the two costs, the buttons carry the icons and costs; keys 1 and 2 with key up). Held knockbacks (fighttest holdStep, horde holdStep, castUp on pointer up, cancel or lost capture): holdP, power drained at the slot's rate, 3.5 s at most, OUT OF POWER stops it; the laser and the gust keep Tyrant in pushed (push(dmg, sp), refreshed every frame, the beam redrawn every 0.07 s, chips for the gust) until let go; the kick winds up in kickCharge (kickK, 1.6 s to full) and lands on release (ATK.kick, the fling by kickK). In the horde the laser and the gust are a line swept again every 0.12 s while held (line(id), 16 m by 1.3 or 12 m by 2.4), the kick a cone 3.5 to 6.5 m wide by the wind-up, sonic a 7 m ring (2 s, shoved out), gravity a 5 m ring (3 s). Bots after: the masher wins with 8 to 84 health, the good bot 100 of 100. Test: kit3-test.cjs (the laser held: Tyrant from 5 to 44 m, 64 to 44 energy; the kick wound up: 19 m; sonic, hammer, gravity, flurry, gust; the picker saving; the horde's held laser 13 dusted, the kick 10, sonic 9 staggered).
- 10d, the fighting moves against the disasters (Sean: figure out how to use fighting moves vs all disasters). In flight (the hero game) the bar's power button cycles the kit's two powers only (HeroPower.list when F.heroGame), each with its own bite on each disaster (heropower.js WEAK extended: gust as wind; laser 3 on aliens, 2 on the kaiju, worm, rift, plant, meteor; kick and ram as melee: 2 on the kaiju, worm, riot, aliens, plant, 1 on a meteor, tornado, rift; half a point otherwise). Ramming: flown through a disaster's heart (within 14 m, under 40 m over it) at over 22 m/s is a blow of the melee slot (HeroPower.ram, once a second, 6 x the bite x 1.4 for the hammer fists, 0.8 for the flurry; the kaiju takes damage too), with a ring, a flash and a tip. The riot is fought on foot (herogame.js riotLevel, WAVES[2]; horde.js mode riot): Horde.riot is the event, its members the targets (isT: S.RIOT or held), the mob goes for her and shoves (people.riotStep heroTg.riot: within 40 m, a shove within 1.3 m every 1.4 s, 0.6 of a bite), she knocks them flat and calms them (people.calm: FLEE, never dusted, the mob's calmed count), no finishers or dazing in a riot (the cross just knocks the nearest flat), tosses and powers work; the level ends when none are rioting or the riot ends by itself (RIOT OVER, TOWN SAVED). The other disasters stay with the flying hero and the Use button; a tornado, a kaiju or a worm fought on foot with the kit is not built.
- 10e, the team move (Sean: incorporate moves with the NPC hero teams, built up by building the team). A TEAM meter (fight test gain(): 0.7 of every energy gain; horde: 3 a dust) fills a green TEAM button (data-t, .team) naming the teammate (Dragonfly or Tempo at random); full, a press calls them: the fight test (teamGo, mateStep): they streak in from the side, a cross flings Tyrant down at your feet (25 damage, DOUBLE TEAMED, GRAB THE FEET) and climb away; the horde: a pass along the line to the nearest of the crowd and 36 m through it at 0.9 m up, everyone within 3.5 m of the pass dusted (or knocked flat in a riot), then away. In the hero game the button exists only with the tower's Tag team skill (TREE Speed: tag; HS.load().hero.skills.tag), in the Sandbox always. Test: team-test.cjs (the fight test's call, the horde's pass, the riot level from ?mode=hero with idx 2 to TOWN SAVED, the flight bar's two powers and two rams on a tornado: 100 to 88).
- Known small leak (older, not from the inside): each collapse leaves one empty Mesh in the scene (11 after 11 falls on the live site before interiors); for the speed list.

- The on-foot view, the GRAB button, the glide (Sean, 2026-11-02: in the first level you cannot see what is happening, the camera too close and the buildings and trees in the way; hold to block and the block button made a GRAB button, to try and maybe revert; no power for a dash, she should float just off the ground and move that way). The horde camera: pitch 0.58 to 0.8 rad, distance 26 to 32 m with a zombie within 16 m and 46 m with none (eased 1.2 a second; the crowd counted every 0.3 s over the horde's own ids, never people.around: that, called right after the level's people.hide, hung the renderer in the headless shell, 'Target crashed'); the building swing kept, and a wall still on the line (hitT, 24 samples from the look point out to the camera) pulls the camera in along it just short of the wall (pull: in 8 a second, out 1.5), so downtown it comes down to the street instead of sitting inside a tower. Tree crowns in the camera's way shrink out of it: city.js keeps every crown (street and lot) in a 24 m grid with its built matrix (instTiled now returns mats; crownsNear(x, z, R); crownShow(it, k) scales the instance about its centre and leaves one already zeroed by a disaster or with its building alone); horde.trees refreshes the list within 48 m every 0.5 s, shrinks any crown within 1.15 s + 1.2 m of the camera line to 5% (5 a second), grows them back once clear, restores all on stop. A ghost of the hero (fighttest.js ghost(r, col)): the smooth body mesh and the kept parts cloned under the same joints with a MeshBasicMaterial, depthFunc GreaterDepth, front faces only, additive at 0.7 of the colour, drawn in the opaque pass at renderOrder 1 with every mesh of the rig raised to 2: a flat glowing outline drawn only where the town, a crown or the crowd (all drawn before it) hides the body, never over the body itself and never through her own cape (a transparent-pass ghost showed through the cape trailing between the camera and her back, up close a cyan patchwork); Nova cyan in both fights, Tyrant red in the test. Controls (both fights): the screen held is the block (hold(): the finisher holds first, else blockOn; the pointer tagged blk, so its release only drops the guard); the GRAB button (data-b, grabOn; its pointer goes into ptrs as held, so the screen handlers spin, steer and release it; keys g and b): a dazed one's neck (finPick neck), by the feet already: the spin, a downed Tyrant: his feet (the ankle grab; TAP smash; GRAB again spins), in reach (horde: the nearest within 4.5 m; test: under 5 m with him on his feet): the seize (spin while held, let go to toss), otherwise the heavy or slam wound up while held. The tap-then-hold seize and the hold-for-heavy are gone; a tap at a downed Tyrant says GRAB: BY THE FEET. A swipe with under 12 power glides instead of refusing (state glide: 9 m/s decaying 1.6 a second, 0.45 m up, 0.8 s, no cost, no counter). Tests: cam2-test.cjs (the level: distance, the pull behind a wall from 24 m above her down to 8, a crown shrunk, 15 ghost meshes, no errors), grab-test.cjs (every grab, the block, the glide, in both fights). The app's browser pane: javascript_tool errors on g.mode.start() (the mode travels), so schedule it with setTimeout; the pane runs at about 30 fps.

- The neck drag after Sean's reference (2026-11-02, a phone video of the Smallville drag: the villain ploughed through the ground at speed, the camera low and riding along, punches on the way, dust everywhere). Both fights: cine(dt) blends the camera (cineK, in 5 a second, out 2.5) to 8 m behind whoever drags, 3 m up (3.5 in the town) and 1.5 m to the side, looking at a point 1.5 m past them, the depth of field focused there (frame: g.focusAt is look2 while cineK > 0.5); in the town the shot is pulled in short of a wall (hitT(c, L) now takes the look point; never under 0.6 of the way). plow(x, z, fx, fz) each frame at the one dragged: three chunks of ground flung up and back (4 to 12 m/s, a 7 m/s spread), a dust ring 0.6 of frames, and in the town city.takeStreetNear 1.6 m (lamps and trees torn out and flung ahead). The drag is faster (test 21 to 24 m/s, town 14 to 18). A tap while dragging (tap() first, before the finisher taps; keys: space) is dragPunch: in the test a spark, hitStop 0.04, shake 0.35, N HITS ON THE WAY, 0.22 s apart; in the town a ring and a sweep of 2.4 m round the one dragged (the zombies next to it burst). Each hit adds to the fling: the test's fling2 24 + 4 a hit (up to 5) and 6 + hits up, with 14 chips of ground at her feet; the town's 18 + 3 a hit and a burst 4 + hits wide. Tyrant's own drag of her gets the same shot. Tests: drag-test.cjs (the shot at 3 m up and 8 m back in 1.2 s, 3 hits, the fling, both fights), ghost-test.cjs (her behind a tower with the swing and pull off: the cyan ghost through the wall).

- 11a, the zombie level from the air (Sean, 2026-11-02: take off and fly round, lock on a zombie within a city block and land to fight; shoot straight down for a super hero landing with a shockwave; the spells from the air; the grab button's place in the air a Smash land, with a plain land too). The horde's HUD has TAKE OFF (data-a fly, in the level and the Sandbox): takeOff() hides her body and HUD, keeps every zombie (people.heroTg off, so they wander; toDrop pauses with the frame), sets horde.active false and flying true, and starts the flight (F.heroGame, F.fromHorde = the horde, the saved flyer) at her spot, 1.5 m up, her way (flight yaw = atan2(-cos, sin) of hers), speed 14. The flight bar gets SMASH LAND after Land when fromHorde; Land calls landHorde(false): the flight stops quietly and horde.land(x, z, smash) puts her back on the nearest walkable spot with the HUD, camera (fov, look, azimuth, distance and pull reset), heroTg and the dof restored; smash: smashLand(), a 9 m sweep, four rings, 40 chunks, lamps and trees within 5 m flung, a cracked disc, a flash, hitStop 0.12, shake 1.2, SUPER HERO LANDING: N DUSTED. SMASH LAND arms smashing: the frame drops her straight down at 70 m/s, nose down, to 1.2 m over the ground, then landHorde(true, true). Lock on with fromHorde picks the nearest zombie within 90 m (a proxy with x, z, y and gone getters over the people arrays); homeIn lands her (plain) within 7 m of it instead of starting a duel. The flight's stop clears fromHorde and smashing. The kit's two powers ride the bar as in the other levels (heroGame). herogame.update tolerates the horde off while flying (H.flying); finish stops the flight too. Test: fly-test.cjs (take off, the bar's buttons, lock and come down, up again to 45 m, the smash: 3 dusted, no errors).

- 11c, the Home Base (Sean, 2026-11-02: the Sandbox more of a home base: inside, a costume area, a computer for skills and powers, a practice ring against a personal robot whose level and powers you set at a computer by the ring, and Go outside to the sandbox town; the base's look from outside, its style and its trim, window, door and wall colours, picked by you; his sheet of ten bases, five hero and five villain). base.js, a pilot like the horde (main.js game.base, in the pilot list; the fight test's and horde's stop lists; the Sandbox's constructor places it on the town's centre, the city's own buildings within 26 m taken away, and starts inside it unless ?out=1; a Home Base side button goes back in). Saved in localStorage zday_base: style, wall, trim, win, door, robot {level 1 to 5, jab, grab, charge, laser}. STYLES: tower, lab, mountain, fortress, hideout (hero), castle, factory, lair, temple, chaos (villain), each a build over helpers (box, cyl, cone, dome, rock: a cone with its lower ring jittered, wins: a grid of lit panes, strip, hangar, dish, jet, tree, smoke, horn, door) in the four picked colours (baseBuilding(cfg, y)); the room inside takes the style's w and d. Inside (start): the exterior hidden, a room on a 3 m podium (the town's people walk underneath, out of sight; four steps down to the door), the two far walls full height and the near two a rail so the iso camera (pitch 0.95, 24 m, azimuth as the fights) sees in, the walls in the wall colour lightened; five stations with a gold disc each: the wardrobe, the computer, the ring console (a red ring 5 m wide in the middle), the blueprints table, the door. Nova in her outfit (heroRig('Nova', HS.outfit('Nova'))) walks to where you tap the floor (a ray against the floor plane, clamped to the room, 5 m/s, walkK and phase for the legs); within 2.2 m of a station the action button shows (Change costume, Skills and powers, The robot: set it up, fight, Change the base, Go outside). Panels: the wardrobe (suit, cape, boots and gloves swatches into HS outfits.Nova, as the tower's hub; redress() rebuilds her rig), the computer (who flies the levels, the kit's three slots, level and XP), the robot (level, its moves: jabs, grabs, charges, eye laser; FIGHT THE ROBOT), the blueprints (hero and villain styles, four colour rows; rebuild() and the room walls retinted). The fight: fightTest.start({ ring: { x, z, y: the floor, r: 7 }, robot }): no district cleared, no plaza raised (plaza hidden, no boxes, its own scar materials), the arena radius this.R from the ring, the robot Tyrant's rig in grey cloth (suit #8f97a3, cape #3a3f47, dark #20242a) with 300 × (0.5 + 0.25 level) health, its blows × (0.6 + 0.2 level) (hitHero), vilAtk limited to the moves picked (none picked: it waits), the eye laser half the time it would charge from over 4 m (kind laser: at the end of its wind-up vLaser(): a red beam mesh 160 ms, LASER!, 10 × dmgK and a 12 m/s shove within 18 m unless she is out of the way or blocking); the label ROBOT LV n. Leave reloads into the Sandbox, which starts inside the base again. Go outside: stop(): the room removed, the exterior back, the orbit camera over the front door, Open Disasters as before. Known: the pane's javascript_tool errors on anything that navigates; the base's exterior is hemmed by the town's towers (26 m cleared); the sandbox's old centre-plaza fight test hides the base (hideAll) and the horde too. Tests: base-test.cjs (start inside, a real tap walks her, each station's panel, a suit, a kit pick, a style and wall colour, the robot's level and laser saved, the fight in the ring with the label and health, outside with ?out=1), laser-probe.cjs (the laser forced: LASER!, 100 to 90 health).

- The base's hall (Sean, 2026-11-02: the inside can be much larger, a basement with no limit; practice flying in there; later, random attacks inside and outside by zombies or hordes of robots, plan 11d). The room is now a 120 by 90 m hall (base.js HALL) 30 m up over the outskirts 320 m beyond the town's east edge (base.hx, hz; nothing of the town pokes through, the exterior stays on the town's centre), on its podium with the two far walls full height, the near two a rail, twelve pillars, the five stations in a cluster near the door (the wardrobe and computer 16 m either side, 30 m in; the ring 12 m wide 4 m past the middle with its console at 18 m; the blueprints at -18, 12; the door at the south wall), the camera 28 m out. TAKE OFF (data-fly, bottom left): takeOff() hides her and the HUD and starts the flight from where she stands (F.fromBase; no lock-on, and the far-from-town swing back off while fromBase); the bar's Land is landBase(): the flight stops quietly and base.land(x, z) puts her on the floor there, clamped to the hall. The robot fight's ring radius is 14 now. A joystick bottom right (Sean: in case the player wants it; .stick, a 118 px pad, the knob up to 40 px): its offset is her walking direction in the camera's frame (right and forward from the camera matrix), 5 m/s at full tilt, clamped to the hall; a tap-to-walk goal is dropped when the pad is touched. The exterior sits just outside the town's east edge (base.x = bounds.x1 + 48; Sean: not the centre), so no town building is cleared for it; Go outside orbits over it there. The headless walk runs slow (the frame clock capped), so base-test hops her to each station.

- 11b, the town thrown about (Sean, 2026-11-02: ragdoll cars and trees, moved by the shockwaves and the fighting; a special move to pick up cars, trees and buildings and toss them). horde.js, in the level and the Sandbox horde. flingAround(x, z, R, sp): every moving car within R (traffic.near, modes under EXT) flung outward (traffic.fling, soft so no one is crushed; up 0.55 sp + 3), parked cars hotwired into moving ones first (traffic.hotwire) and flung, lamps and trees taken (city.takeStreetNear) and flung as debris; called by the smash landing (9 m, 14 m/s), the sky finisher (its radius, 10 + 20 k3) and the slam (5 m, 9). flingLine (the laser and the gust, every 0.5 s of the hold, the line 1.6 m wider than the sweep) and flingCone (the kick's cone) throw them the way the power points. GRAB with no zombie in reach (grabOn, before the slam): lift(): the nearest car within 3.5 m (parked ones hotwired) grabbed from the traffic (EXT) and carried over her head (carryStep: 2.3 m up, across her, bobbing), else the nearest lamp or tree within 2.4 m ahead taken from the street and carried as a mesh of its own (a trunk and crown, or a pole), else the smallest building within 3.5 m ahead with a half-width under 9 m and under 18 m tall (people.clearBuilding spare, extractBuilding, hideBuilding, a collapse event and a dust cloud on its plot) eased up over her head in 0.6 s. State carry (C.spinH), the GRAB button lit, no block while carrying. Let go (release: throwHeld): a car flung 24 m/s and 9 up by the traffic (it lands as a wreck); a tree, lamp or building flies in this.thrown (thrownStep: gravity 24, a building turning, a hit on any building taller than it or the ground), and land2 where it comes down: the zombies within R burst (a car 4.5 m, a tree 2.5 + its size, a building its half-width + 4), a ring, chunks in its colour, a crash, N CRUSHED; a building also flattens the small buildings under it (destroyBuilding collapse, spare, noCrush) and blows the windows of the tall, a dust cloud its height, a flash. Known: a building thrown among others hits them at once (the test's crashed within 0.3 s); the fight test's plaza has none of this. Test: toss-test.cjs (a shockwave: 3 cars in the air; a parked car lifted, thrown, landed; a small building lifted and thrown; no errors).

- 11d, the base attacks (Sean, 2026-11-02: a meter filled as you win fights and beat disasters; full, a base attack can happen; a few robot variations, the leader looking like the ring's robot, the rest his army; the new bases and robots on the roster). The meter: hero.threat in the profile (herogame.js fresh), +25 a level won (+20 a zombie or riot level) in herogame.finish, +20 a fight-test win that is not an attack (fighttest.finish), shown in the base's HUD under the hint (BASE THREAT n%, red and pulsing at 100: ATTACK COMING; showThreat each second). The army: figures.js ZTYPES gains three robot types after the zombies (ROBOT_TYPES, w 0 so no town rolls them, robot: true): the drone (the runner's numbers, 0.85 scale, light steel), the trooper (the walker's, hp 2, a little faster), the brute (the bloater's frame, hp 4, 1.2 scale, no pop), all in steel skin and gunmetal clothes with no blood; the horde drops them when o.types is given (horde.start: o.types, o.label for the HUD, o.at for where it starts; SCRAPPED in place of DUSTED). The attacks (base.js): coming into the base with the meter full, alarm(): a toast and a crash, then 3.5 s later one of the two at random; Go outside with it full is always the gate. attackIn(): THE ROBOT LEADER IS IN THE HALL, the ring fight at level 5 with every move, o.attack: won, BASE DEFENDED: +400 XP and the threat 0 (fighttest.finish). attackOut(): the base stopped quietly, ROBOTS AT THE GATES!, the horde at the town's east edge (40 m in from the bounds, the walkable spot nearest) with 80 robots and the level rules; won, the threat 0 and +300 XP, lost, they will be back; the base starts again 1.8 s after either. The roster (roster.js): Robots (the leader: Tyrant's rig in the ring's grey outfit, and the three figures, filed there instead of under Zombies) and Home bases (the ten styles, hero ones in stone and sky blue, villain ones in gunmetal and red). Also (Sean): no bites from the ground while she is in the air: horde sets people.heroTg.air (over 0.9 m up, or the sky finisher's and the glide's states) and zombieStep and riotStep wait 0.4 s under her instead of biting or shoving. Tests: attack-test.cjs (the meter full on load: the alarm and whichever attack came, then the other forced: 71 robots of the three types at the gate, ROBOTS, won: threat 0 and the base back; the leader at level 5 with the laser, BASE DEFENDED: +400 XP, threat 0), roster-probe.cjs (Robots 4, Home bases 10, no errors). Known: in the headless test she stands still and the army bites her down in about ten seconds.

- Seamless take-off and landings (Sean, 2026-11-02: taking off, landing and smash landing portalled her to random spots). The cause was horde.land snapping her to the nearest walkable spot within 30 m (over a roof or a wall, the nearest street), and the take-off starting at 14 m/s so she shot off before the camera caught up. Now: land snaps only within 5 m, and falls back to 40 m just for a roof or a wall, saying LANDED ON THE STREET BELOW (or SMASHED DOWN ON THE STREET BELOW) when it had to; the take-off (the level and the base) starts at 3 m/s nose up, so she rises on the spot. Test: seam-test.cjs (the flight begins 0.4 m from where she stood, 2.8 m up; a plain landing 0.0 m and a smash landing 0.5 m from where the flight ended).

- Take-off and landing animated and eased; the on-foot UI the same everywhere (Sean, 2026-11-02: an animation, seamless, the same camera angle; a symmetrical UI that feels the same across all play; the joystick anywhere you hold is good but clashes with block and dash, and tap-to-walk with punching; maybe four buttons along the bottom). The take-off (horde.takeOff, base.takeOff): a crouch (C.chargeWind) for 0.22 s then a leap (state liftoff: 16 t² up), and at 0.55 s liftNow(): the flight from where she is, 0.6 m up, 4 m/s nose up, in the town's iso view (F.setView('iso')), with the camera handed over by game.camBlend (main.js, right after the pilot's frame: the old position, orientation and fov eased into the new pilot's over 1.1 s, smoothstep; the new fov read on the first blended frame). The landing (flight.landHorde, landBase): the blend set from the flight camera before the flight stops, then horde.land or base.land with the touchdown clip (CLIPS.land) and a puff. Input, both fights (fighttest.js CSS and markup shared by the horde): the left 45% of the screen is a joystick that appears where the finger lands (.stk, the knob up to 40 px; stickDir(): the camera's right and forward) and walks her at up to 5 m/s while idle (the legs going, facing her way; walkK decays otherwise); the right half is the fight as before (tap, swipe, hold, the finisher steering); the base's pad floats the same way, its right half still tap-to-walk. The bottom is one row of four (.row4, 64 px, equal): GRAB, the stun, the knockback, then TAKE OFF in the level and the Sandbox horde or TEAM in the fight test; the horde's TEAM is a slim bar above the row (.teambar); Restart and Leave sit top right under the bars; the hints say LEFT HALF and RIGHT HALF. Tests: stick-test.cjs (a hold-and-drag on the left walks her, the pad shown and hidden, a right tap still punches, the row's four), seam-test.cjs (the flight begins 0.4 m off, the landings 0.0 m from the flight's end), and the grab, fly and base tests unchanged.

- The office, the basement, the base's controls, the mini map (Sean, 2026-11-02: the same controls in the base with the joystick; a mini map, coming out he could not tell where the town was; taking off inside and landing in the town brought him back to the base, so take off only outside; the inside too big: an office with the stations and an elevator in the middle down to the basement for the robot fights; the basement its own instance so it can be bigger than the building). base.js: two rooms (buildRoom(key, w, d, cx, cz, stations)): the office, 30 by 22 m at base.hx, hz, with the wardrobe (-10, -6), the computer (10, -6), the blueprints (-10, 5), the elevator (a steel box with a lit sign, 0, 0) and the door; the basement, 120 by 90 m, 400 m further out (its own level, as big as we like), with the ring (12 m, at -2), its console (16, -2), the elevator up (0, 16) and the pillars; both on their podiums 30 m up, the far walls full height and the near two a rail, the walls tagged (userData.wall) for retint. goFloor(key, instant, at): a fade to black (.fade, 0.3 s), her set down by the other room's elevator (or its door coming in), the camera placed, the row redrawn; this.cur is the room (its bounds clamp the tap-to-walk and the joystick), this.stations its list, each station with ax, az and floor. The controls as everywhere: the left 45% a joystick where the finger lands, the right half tap the floor to walk, and a bottom row of four (.row4): the office's Wardrobe, Computer, Blueprints, Outside; the basement's Robot, Office, Blueprints, Outside; a tap walks her there (goTo: the other floor by the elevator first) and opens the station on arrival (autoOpen); the station button sits above the row. The ring fight and the leader's attack go down to the basement first (fight(), attackIn()); the office camera 22 m out, the basement 28. No take-off inside any more (the button gone, liftoff unreachable); outside, a Take off side button (game.js, beside Home Base) starts the hero flight by the base's door in the town's iso view, and Land is the flight's own (back to the street's orbit camera). minimap.js (main.js game.minimap, updated 8 times a second): a 96 px canvas top left under the bars with the town's bounds (a grey block, a cross through its middle), the base (an orange house) and a magenta dot for where the view is (the flight, the hero on foot, or the orbit camera's target), N at the top; hidden inside the base, in the menus and the hub. Tests: base-test.cjs (the office's stations, the ring in the basement at z + 399, the fight, outside), map-probe.cjs (the map outside and in flight, the street take-off in the iso view, Land back to the street), attack-test.cjs.

- 12a, who you play and the first four levels (Sean, 2026-11-02: at the start of Defend and Destroy choose 1 of the 3 you are levelling, not an outfit; a hero who turns villain leaves the Defend list and is chosen under Destroy, a villain who does too well the other way; level 1 aliens, 2 zombies, 3 the worm, 4 the kaiju, the main action fights, weather levels after, with a different play style to flesh out; 4 Defend and 4 Destroy levels so agents can test the whole game). herogame.js: HS now holds six characters (chars: nova, dragonfly, tempo, tyrant, blur, weaver), each their own xp, rep, skills, idx, towns, trophies, threat and side; last.hero and last.villain the last picked; HS.load().hero and .villain are getters for the current one, so old callers keep working; HS.team(side), cur(side), name(id), curName(side); an old save's one profile goes to whoever was flying it. rigFor(name, outfit): their body whichever side they stand on. pickScreen(side, onGo, onBack): the first screen from the menu (game.js playMode, before the globe): three cards (level, reputation, next level), one turning figure, PLAY, then the globe and straight into the level (g.heroGo). The hub (after a level) has the same cards in place of the team row; the wardrobe is gone from it (costumes at the Home Base, for the current hero). Crossing (cross(s), called in finish): a hero under 15 reputation becomes a villain (rep 14), a villain at 60 a hero (rep 60), with a line in the tally. Heroes lose it as before (fear / 30, now on foot too, a thrown building counting); villains gain 35 for each disaster stopped and 2 for each person saved, lose 3 a win. A side with nobody left says so and sends you to the other. WAVES starts aliens, zombies, worm, kaiju, then the old road (tornado, riot, flood and Blur...); villains named in a wave only if still villains; rivals only heroes still heroes. Defend level 1 (alienLevel): the invasion overhead (life 99999, sent off at the end) and 200 alien soldiers (figures.js ALIEN_TYPES: grunt, soldier; w 0) dropped into the streets, fought as the horde (o.types, o.label, o.verb), 200 far civilians' slots pooled. Level 2: the zombie level as it was (idx 1: 500). Levels 3 and 4 (bossLevel): horde.start({ boss: e }): no zombies, left is the boss's heroHp; it hunts her (the kaiju steered every 1.5 s, started 80 m off; the worm's goal her spot while it tunnels, so it breaks out under her, 12 if she is within 9 m on the ground); bossPt: the kaiju's body within 14 m, the worm only where it is out of the ground and low (y -3 to 14); bossHit: a jab 0.35, a cross 0.7, the slam 2, the kick 1.5 + 3 k, the stun 2 (and 3 s without a blow from it), the laser 0.15 a tick, a thrown car 6, a tree 2.5, a building 15, the smash landing 5, the team move 8, each feeding energy and the team meter; in the air the powers at 0.35 of usual and the ram, Lock on flies at it and lands. Its blows: every 2.4 to 4 s within 34 m (the worm 26 m while arcing) a red mark under her, 1.1 s, then 10 (the worm 8) unless she has moved, is in the air or dashing; a block takes a fifth. The camera at 62 m, kept so the giant is seen past her. Win 300 + 300 XP (+100 under 10 hits). Destroy's four levels: the flown level as it was (wreck 8 + 4 a level buildings, beat the heroes who come, Lock on, the old circle duel), with the level's disaster on your side (R.allies, not counted among what must end; zombies: 120 dropped). The on-foot levels' tally box is hidden (the bars say it). Tests: levels-test.cjs (pick from the real menu; a bot walks by the stick, punches and dashes off the marks through all four Defend levels; the four Destroy levels' flow; crossing, nobody left, an old save), destroy-real.cjs (86 buildings in 49 s by a flying bot; the rival at 780 health beat a tapping bot three times). Known: Destroy's rival fight is still the old duel, not the fight-test engine, and its building goal is easy; levels 5 on are the old road, to be redone as weather levels.

- 12b, the old duel gone (Sean, 2026-11-03: only the new, tested fighting, like with the robot). heroduel.js is deleted, and flight.js's fight camera, hover and duelOver with it. Lock on reaching them calls flight.fightFoe(v): v held (gripped) and hidden, the flight stopped quietly (F.fighting, which herogame.update waits on), fightTest.start({ ring at the nearest street to them, r 26, street: true, who: the flyer, hp: from the hull, foe: { name, hpMax 180 + 70 a villain level, hp: what they had left, dmgK 0.75 + 0.1 a level }, onEnd }). The engine takes any two bodies (rigFor), names them on the bars and in FINISHES YOU, hides Restart and Leave, and hands back at the end (stop(true) tidies without a reload). streetStep: no walking through walls; flung over 9 m/s a building under 24 m comes down and counts, anything else stops you. Its camera swings round the town's buildings as the horde's does. onEnd: the flight starts again from where she stood, 1.5 m up and slow, with the stats, speed and hull kept and the camera eased across; won: LAB.stats.won, the villain beaten (heroWin set, so it sticks) and fleeing; lost: what they had left stays on them (v.fightHp, at least 40), and it is a knock out of the sky (the level's three strikes). The ship chase after a beaten villain (F.getaway from the duel) no longer starts from here.
- The hub has a Testing row (Sean: test the fighting against the aliens, worm and kaiju): jump to any of the first four levels; take it out before release.

- Levels start in the air (Sean, 2026-11-03: level 1 started on the ground among what looked like zombies, then the aliens came in; start floating over the city, then choose to fly, land, auto lock or flee back to base, where the action goes on outside while you change outfit and powers, then go back out). The bug: the grunts were dressed as green zombies and walked like them, and the fleet's real alien squads (S.ALIEN, which she cannot hit) came 9 s later. Now the soldiers wear ALIEN_LOOK with the alien walk (anim 7), the fleet launches at once with 6 saucers, no squads and no tripods (opts.noSquads, tripods 0), and the soldiers drop only under the saucers (horde o.fleet, dropFrom). Every level starts floating: flight.hovering at 70 m, speed 0, in the iso view; a drag or the arrows or FLY sets off (unhover), HOVER stops. On-foot levels: horde.start with o.level calls hoverNow(x, z); the bar is a row of four, FLY, LAND, LOCK ON, BASE, with the powers and SMASH LAND above (view, speed and invert hidden). LAND comes down at 32 m/s and SMASH at 70 (flight.smashing { sp, smash }), not a jump to the ground. Lock on takes the nearest from anywhere. horde.bg(dt), from main.js while flying: the drops, the count, the clock go on, so the zombies keep on the town and a level can end while she is away. BASE: horde.toBase: the flight stopped, base.start({ level }): the ring says not now, no attack alarm, the Door calls horde.back(): her kit and body rebuilt (outfit and powers as changed), 30 health back, floating over the base. A level ending while she is in the base closes it. Destroy's levels start floating too; a drag sets off; they have no Land or Base yet. Test: hover-test.cjs.

- Level 1 just aliens (Sean, 2026-11-03: zombies still spawning). The soldiers are zombie-state figures, and a zombie's bite turned the people they caught into real zombies. people.bite now only scatters people for alien and robot types; the radio's zombie line is off for them (people.noZombieNews). A test agent also found a level could win itself (police and others shot 90 of 200 aliens in 75 s while she floated): in a level, kill() leaves zombie-state figures alone (people.heroOnly), so they fall only to the player. Test: noz-test.cjs.

- Tuned from four Sonnet test agents' play (2026-11-03) and Sean: no stop at a wall (flight.punch no longer halts flying: it was a deliberate 0.4 s, and the entry itself costs under 2 ms; speed stays up; a Use no longer stops her either). Enemies no longer pile on buildings in a level (they dropped out of the count, which is why it rose and fell); under 40 left, any farther than 30 m drop in near her, six every 3 s (pullIn); red dots for what is left on the mini map, a big one for a giant or a rival. Giants: Use from the air only within 90 m and at 0.12; a giant comes for her while she flies; the kick 4 + 8 k, the stun 5, the laser 0.5 a tick, a thrown car 10, a tree 4, a building 20, the smash 10, the team move 12, so the rest of the kit now beats punching (0.2, the cross 0.4); the camera 82 m (the worm 72) framing her and it together. Destroy: 25 + 15 a level buildings (at most 60% of the town), the bar FLY, LOCK ON, QUIT with a second tap to confirm; the tally box under the mini map, hidden in fights; a foe walled off for 0.8 s comes round to her. All levels by day (HeroGame.zombieNight). HUD: short hints, above the radio line; pause and compass up at the top on foot and in flight, out of the punching thumb's way; 44 px swatches, chips and fight buttons. The robots' attack at the gate starts on the ground (o.ground).

- Pause and the base (Sean, 2026-11-03: paused, chose Base, stuck). The base screen sat over the pause button (z-index 30 against 28 for the fights), so a game paused in the air could not be unpaused inside, and pause never worked there. It now sits under it like the fights, and going into the base carries on from a pause. Test: pause-test.cjs.

- Boost and the fleet (Sean, 2026-11-03, built after he asked how to speed up or fight saucers and tripods in story mode). Story mode is Sean's name for Defend and Destroy. Bar: each kit power its own button (no Use), BOOST held (flight.boosting: toward 1.2 of top speed, easing back on release; breaks a hover or a lock), Destroy too. Level 1 (horde o.fleet, the Invasion): tripods back (2, first at 8 s); level left = soldiers + undropped + machines up, launches and drops still to come included, so it is won when all are down. kill-style hits from people on saucers and tripods are off in a level (heroOnly). Lock on in the air takes the nearest machine first, soldiers only when none is up (LAND is for the ground); reaching one is a 34 hit and a hover beside it. Ram at over 22 m/s within 12 m: 25, every 0.8 s. Powers from the air on a machine within 110 m: 6 x the aliens table. Saucers 60 health, tripods 320 in the level (m.damage); soldiers 150. Tripods walk at her, and being under one (within 12 m of its middle) counts as in reach, since their feet can stand on roofs. The SMASH LAND button says GRAB IT within 18 m of a saucer, then THROW IT: she carries it ahead of her (state held, heroBy faked so orphans() lets it be), thrown 60 m/s, lands as a wreck with a burst of 8 m; landing or Base while holding shoots it down. On foot: fleetStep gives each walking tripod within 45 m a red mark under her, a beam from its head, 7 unless she has moved (a stun holds them off 3 s); punches hit a tripod if a foot is within reach (3, cross 5, slam 10, kick 10 + 20 k), the laser 2 a tick, a stun 12, the smash 30, a thrown car 25, a tree 10, a building or machine 50. A downed tripod: GRAB within 14 m lifts the wreck overhead, let go to throw it (burst 12 m); it can be thrown again. Mini map: blue dots for machines. Test: fleet-test.cjs.

- In close with the machines (Sean, 2026-11-02: Lock on, then punch and grab as against villains; spin toss or smash, floating). Lock on reaching a saucer or a tripod's leg is a 10 hit and flight.engaged: she stays beside it (a real drag, FLY or BOOST leaves), a tap on the screen is a punch (8, every third 16, 0.22 s apart). GRAB IT: held (state held, heroBy faked for orphans); the first bar row gives way to SPIN TOSS (hold: round her, faster, she faces it; let go: away along the swing at 25 to 70 m/s) and SMASH DOWN (tap: overhead for 0.55 s, then down with her at 65 m/s; the wreck where they hit, and her super hero landing). A saucer so thrown or smashed is finished. A tripod takes 160 (of 320) and gets up again unless that ends it (tripLands); then its wreck can be lifted on foot. Landing or Base while holding lets it go. Her place on a tripod is halfway up a leg, 26 m, above most roofs. Lock on no longer rams its own target on the way in (a saucer could die before she got there). Camera: flight.closeK takes it back and lower in close (1.5 saucer, 1.9 tripod) and holding (1.9, 2.8), and a spin does not turn the view with it. Tripods get their level health (320) while she is still flying, it was only set on foot. Tests: grabair-test.cjs, grabair-leave.cjs (Boost, a drag and Land all let go).

- Radio station (Sean, 2026-11-02, as in Tornado Alley; radiobox.js, the choices in music.js, remembered in localStorage zday_radio): the five songs, a play button for each and a tap on the name to take it off the air (one always stays on), Skip, the volume, Repeat the song (off: a shuffle), Cinematic music (on at the start). Cinematic on: action music (Desert Pursuit) in the disasters and in any fight of the story (on foot, flying in a level, the ring), the clean-up track through outbreaks and for a while after; the chosen songs at all other times, which is new: quiet moments used to be Calm Before the Storm only. Cinematic off: only the chosen songs, fights too. A song played with its button plays over everything until it ends. Song endings moved from the game loop to the player's timeupdate, since nothing else runs on menus or when paused. The menus still follow the side of the world in view when that song is on the air. Repeat holds on the menus too, over the song for the side of the world. Opens from the two-notes button on the main menu, the pause menu, the Tower and the Lair. Test: radio-test.cjs.

- Sharp globe (Sean, 2026-11-02: pixelated when zoomed in; asked for vector, this does the same job): paint() no longer paints colours. It stores height, moisture and the Far Isles in a half-float texture (768x384 or 1024x512, no mipmaps), and the planet's MeshStandardMaterial works the colours out per pixel in onBeforeCompile (GLSL_HEAD, GLSL_BODY: the same palette and rules as the old paint). Finer noise (a softened 256 x 256 tiling texture sampled on two planes turned off every axis, or its grid shows as a weave; the map itself read through a cubic curve, or a coast shows steps; at 14, 45, 140 and 500 cycles per planet radius) is added to height and moisture, so coasts and borders keep detail; its mipmaps even out far off, so the planet from the menu looks as before. Edges are smoothed over one pixel with fwidth. bio and hgt for placing towns are unchanged; a coast moves at most a hair from them. Sphere 192 x 128 so the horizon does not show corners. Clouds are still a soft 512 x 256 picture.
- Main Menu in the pause menu (Sean): every mode's pause; a second tap within 4 s to leave. game.leaveToMenu: from a level, flight, fight or the base straight to the menu page, otherwise toMenu's trip up through the clouds.

- The giants up close (Sean, 2026-11-02). Camera, worm and kaiju: hovering, riding or thrown, flight.focK blends the iso camera to sit behind her on the line from the monster, lower (0.36 rad) and with a wider lens (fov 36, not the town's 18, or a monster near her will not fit), looking at a point up to 40 m towards it; she turns to face it. Riding the worm: Lock on goes to it (flight.bossClose: hovering 9 m off and 8 m over it), a tap punches when it is up, GRAB IT mounts (horde.rideStart). rideStep: 14 m behind the head on its back; while the body is under ground she skims the ground over it, and the roof of any building in the way, with dirt flying and the camera looking nearly straight down, since anything under the ground cannot be seen; as it breaks out she goes up with the part already out; it breaks out every 6 to 9 s. Tap or PUNCH 0.7, every third 1.4, of its 100; LASER held 2.5 a second, 3 s then a 2 s rest. FLY reads JUMP OFF. Every 5 to 8.5 s it warns for 0.8 s and bucks (buck, buckStep): half low and fast into buildings (under 34 m they collapse and she goes on, taller she bounces back, 6 damage), half high and spinning; 1.1 to 2.2 s later she hovers with control back. Kaiju riding still to come (Sean: something similar). Test: ride-test.cjs.

- Heroes over the planet (Sean, 2026-11-02): globe.js watchers/watchStep: on the main menu the first three on the hero side, in their last outfits (HS.outfit), float in space at the planet's edge looking at it, with a slow bob; each has its own pose.
