Placed against the camera, so a drag does not move them and a zoom sends them off the screen. Only in the menu state; rebuilt when the team or an outfit changes.

- Alien options page (Sean, 2026-11-02, to choose from; not in the game): creatures.html, src/creatures.js, a third entry in vite.config. The soldiers as they are (the game's own figures), three other ground units (Greys, Mantis drones, Dome walkers) and four running dogs (Silver hound, Maw, Stalker, Plated runner), in plain shapes with a gallop cycle. One canvas with a scissored view per tile, as the roster does. Waiting on his choice.

- The secret button (Sean, 2026-11-02): held, light rises up the saucer's dome over the 7 s (BIG_SHOT, was 15) and it turns pulsing green when the planet-buster is ready, as the orb does (game.js orbFire, .dglow clipped by .dfill). Under 1 s a laser, then a nuke, as before. The first three shots each go at the next hero over the planet (globe knockNext) and knock them spinning off the screen; after that shots hit the planet. The planet-buster takes any left. They are back the next time the menu opens.

- Level 1's crowd (Sean, 2026-11-02: loved every one on the options page; add them all, a few silver soldiers as commanders, a sphere helmet that a punch knocks off). creaturekit.js holds the shapes and CreatureLayer: when the level starts each kind's stride is recorded as 10 whole merged shapes, one instanced mesh each, so a crowd costs one matrix per creature. figures.js: seven new ZTYPES with creature set (their own chase and lunge speeds) and ALIEN_MIX (3 each of Grey, Mantis, Dome walker, 2 each of the dogs, 1 commander: about 8 of 150). people.sync gives the layer each one's matrix and blanks the figure, so moving, biting, falling, throwing and counting need no change. Commanders keep their figure and wear the sphere: in sweep a punch or kick knocks it off and leaves them standing, a bigger hit takes helmet and commander together. Also fixed: RADIOACTIVE was the last ZTYPE, which the added alien and robot kinds had changed.

- Riding the kaiju (Sean, 2026-11-02; as the worm): Lock on goes to it and stops 24 m off at shoulder height (flight.bossClose), tap punches, GRAB IT climbs on: horde.rideKaiju, on the shoulders between the necks (10 m ahead of its middle, 44.5 m up), the camera behind her looking the way it walks. Punch and laser as on the worm (rideFight is shared; the beam goes at one head, then the other), it warns and throws her the same way, the low throw going down into the town. In story mode a tentacle no longer ends the flight when she flies too close: it swats her (10 damage, thrown, 6 s before it can again), and while she is close, riding or thrown, its fire and thrown cars leave her alone. LAND still starts the fight on foot. Riding camera: on the kaiju close behind her (closeK 0.55); on the worm off to one side (0.8), since its own body is what lies behind her. Check a ride with seq-test.cjs, a picture every 2 s.

- Freeze holds what you are fighting (Sean, 2026-11-02): the kit's stun power, from the air: horde.freezeIt takes the machine she is in close with or locked on, the worm or kaiju she is locked on, close to or riding, or the nearest in reach, and sets frozen = 2 s: worm, kaiju, tripod and saucer updates stop, hits do 1.6 times; the giants turn icy blue (emissive, not a shell, which would fog the screen with the camera inside it), the machines get a shell. 5 s before it can freeze again. Riding, a frozen monster cannot throw her: it cancels the warning and the next one is at least 3 s off; the ride bar has its own button. On foot the power near a giant or a tripod does the same.

- Level 5, the tornado mini-game (Sean, 2026-11-02: going round it the other way, but flying is too hard for that): windgame.js. Lock on (flight.storyTornado, the defending side only) and she is flown round the funnel against its spin (flight.ring; fly() hands over to ring.step), the camera outside the ring and a little behind her with a wide lens (fov 58), the funnel filling the middle; going round any faster (0.75 rad/s now) makes the world behind it spin uncomfortably. The three heights are 26, 42 and 58 m. Swipe up or down, the arrows or the keys change height; the three dots on the right show her height and what is coming at each. Cars, trees and roofs come round the other way: 1.9 s apart, 1.1 s by the end, and over half way some come at two heights at once. GUST: green on the gauge unwinds it by 3, plus 0.25 per hit in a row up to 4.5, and shrinks the funnel; a miss winds it back 1.5 and ends the run. Freeze: 2 s, no junk moving, the whole gauge green, gusts 1.6 times; 6 s to recharge. Hit: 10 damage, back up 6, thrown clear, hovering after 1.7 s, Lock on to return. Out of the ring in this level the tornado has no pull and never crashes her: too close it throws her clear. Powers from outside and rams do nothing, so the level cannot be won another way. No life limit in this level, so it cannot end itself, and the army's helicopters stay out of it (LAB.army sends them after heroes on levels 5, 8, 11 and so on). Testing row reaches level 5. Test: wind-test.cjs.

- Close combat round 2 (Sean, 2026-11-02). GRAB IT: when it can be done the button goes gold, bigger and pulsing, and 'GRAB IT!' shows across the top (flight.grabnow, bigText). horde.say now also shows while flying: it only went to the on-foot display, so words like FROZEN! and IT IS BUCKING! were unseen in the air. Spin toss, as against villains: hold SPIN TOSS and it winds up by itself (3.5 to 11 rad/s); a finger circling, on the button or anywhere on the screen, adds to it (spinDrag); at least 2 turns however short the press; let go to throw, the harder the more turns up to 4 (28 to 75 m/s; 100 to 220 to a tripod). Smash Down slams three times by itself, up overhead and down into the street, gathering speed all the way down (Sean: it looked as if it slowed: a fast swing then a steady fall, and arcade's held height was pulling her back up against the fall; slam-test.cjs measures it) (slamStep, slamHit): 70 to a tripod each, a saucer is wrecked by the third; she stays up and in close afterwards rather than landing. Tripods now 640 health (was 320), on-foot blows on them 1.6 times so a leg fight stays about as long. Pause menu: Flying view (Above, Behind, First person) and Invert up and down, remembered in zday_flyview and the old invert key. Tests: grab2-test.cjs.

- Arcade flying (Sean, 2026-11-02: as seamless, clean and easy as possible, arcade, fun, easy to see; first person only to get around). feel-test.cjs plays level 1 with real presses and measures every frame by stretch of play: camera jump, turn, hero off the screen, hero behind a building, slow frames. It found up to 28% off screen and 53% hidden in free flight, because the camera chased her with a lag through a narrow lens, and she flew low. Now in a story level (flight.arcade): a finger pushes her that way on the screen, as on foot, up to about 37 m/s; let go and she stops and hovers; BOOST for 90. No FLY or HOVER button: arrows on the bar go up and down (addUpDown, the cell turns back into LEAVE or JUMP OFF in the mini-game or on a ride). Her height is held and rises by itself over any roof near or ahead. The camera has a fixed direction, kept between air and ground (azS, azF, horde.az), and no lag at all. Edge of town pushes back at 40 m past it. Behind and First person: left and right turn, up and down climb and dive (Invert); camView gives iso in any fight (fightCam: in close, locked on a machine or giant, holding, riding, thrown, the ring, landing), with a blend, and back after. Off the ground the climb builds up over a second or she leaves the frame before the camera follows. LAND over a roof glides down to the street beside it rather than teleporting up to 40 m at the end. land-trace.cjs: takeoffs and landings frame by frame.

- Menus (Sean, 2026-11-02: a station's close button was blocked by the pause button; every menu symmetrical, intuitive, easy). ui-audit.cjs opens every menu and bar on three phone sizes and asks of each button what is really on top of it, its size, whether it is on the screen, whether its row is balanced, with contact sheets; it found the X on Computer and Blueprints really could not be tapped, and on a small phone Main Menu in pause lay under the bar. Base panels: body.zd-panel hides the pause and compass while one is open, X 44 px and sticks to the top as it scrolls, tap outside closes, titles and rows centred, buttons fill even rows. Pause: full screen and centred over everything, one column, RESUME first. 44 px or more for Back, Play, Units, Roster.

- Play test round (three test agents on three phone sizes). On foot: bite at most once per 0.45 s, none in the spin, drag or sky moves, 1.8 s of safety on landing, a red edge flash when hit, and going down takes 1.3 s with DOWN! before the end screen. The camera goes round tripod legs as it does buildings (hidden). The red marks have a hard rim and a fill that shows when they land. No depth blur in her fights. Help fades after 6 s. Energy bar thicker, words that make sense. In the air: in close she takes the camera's side of a machine, never behind it; a gold ring on her shows through everything; the hint sits above whichever bar is up; toast clear of the mini map; pause and compass in one place in every story level. Behind and First person: up climbs by default (Invert for the pilot's way), a gentler turn.

- First-time player's round: a toss homes on the nearest other machine within 170 m (DIRECT HIT, 70, or 120 from a tripod) and the camera goes with it (followF, coX, coZ), then back. Saucers fire bright slow bolts at her in the air, one every 1.3 to 2.9 s from the nearest within 110 m, 6 damage, never the last of her health, and not from one that is frozen or she is in close with: FREEZE and moving matter now. A bar under the HUD shows what she is fighting (targetBar), the HUD says how many are left instead of speed and height, LOCK ON pulses until first used, hands on what she holds, tip cleared after a hold, no percentages. The radio's words sit at the top in a thin line in her levels; the concert's news is silent in them. Kaiju's ride: further back and higher.

- Smaller, furnished bases (Sean, 2026-11-02). Office 18 x 14 (was 30 x 22), basement 40 x 34 (was 120 x 90; the ring still fits). baseroom.js furnish: office: fireplace with moving flames, rugs, a round table with the other heroes of the team seated in their own outfits, wall monitors (pictures drawn once on canvases), two stone statues of the team, plants, lamps, banners, bookcase. Basement: a super car and a super bike on slowly turning lit platforms in the back corners, monitors and a desk, a workbench and tool wall, tyres, a punch bag. Walking goes round them (cur.solids, solidOut); stations stand in front of their furniture. The camera is a little closer. Joystick fixes (Sean: very buggy): a touch the phone took back (lostpointercapture), a panel opening or a change of floor mid-drag left her walking and the stick dead until the next visit; now stickOff everywhere and a reset on entry. The pad follows the thumb past its edge; left half of the screen as on foot; a dead zone, and she turns smoothly instead of snapping to each tiny movement. base-test.cjs.

- Blueprints' little screen (Sean, 2026-11-02): bpView, a small renderer of its own in the panel showing baseBuilding of the current choices, slowly turning, sticking to the top as the panel scrolls, rebuilt on every tap; stopped and let go when the panel closes (bpStop).

- BASE flies her there (Sean, 2026-11-02; it used to cut straight in). horde.toBase lets go of what she holds or rides and calls flight.baseGo; baseRun, inside arcade: up over the roofs, at 1.6 times her top speed to a point 40 m in front of the base, then down to the door, the camera turned to look the way she is going; baseArrive: a quick fade and base.start. The button reads STOP meanwhile; BASE again, a real drag, Lock on or Land stop it. A knock out of the sky ends it. The fight goes on while she flies and she can be shot. Test: run-test.cjs.

- Base walking round 2 (Sean: stuck behind the tree in the corner; the joystick dead on the right of the screen): a drag anywhere walks, a tap anywhere goes there (pressP, 12 px decides). solidOut: head on into a round thing she goes round it, walls are applied after the furniture, and pushing without getting anywhere for 0.6 s next to something lets her squeeze past for 0.5 s, so she cannot be held. Plants out of the corners nearest the camera, the bookcase solid, the table a little further from the wall. walk-test.cjs maps where she can walk.

- Inside the base (Sean, 2026-11-02: more options, black for everything; floor, walls, carpet, round table, desk, fireplace and fire; looks for each; skulls, spikes or crosses): basedecor.js. Blueprints has Outside and Inside tabs. Inside: pick the item and the camera goes to it above a shorter panel, then Look, Design (None, Skulls, Spikes, Crosses) and Colour, and for the fireplace the fire's colour; saved in cfg.inside (insideOf fills in the defaults). Every choice makes the room's decor group again (decorate). Patterns are drawn on canvases in the chosen colour, so black works; floors and carpets take the design as a pattern, walls as posts and shelves, the rest as pieces on them. Black (#050506) added to every colour list, the outside's and the wardrobe's too. deco-test.cjs.

- Names (Sean, 2026-11-02: The Skull Runner, Mr Death Face, The Black Kay): names.js, four scroll wheels (title, colour, name, ending, each may be left out) and Randomize, tens of millions of names, Back to the character's own. In the Wardrobe, and Change the name on the Defend and Destroy pick screens. Saved as chars[id].nick. HS.shown(id) and HS.disp(rigName) are what is displayed (cards, Play, Computer, HUD, fight bars, team-up, crossing over); HS.name stays the character's own, as the rigs and outfits go by it. name-test.cjs.

- Headgear and belt (Sean, 2026-11-02): headgear.js, 31 choices (skull, hood, cowl, cowboy hat, head band, eye band, eye mask, visor, goggles, ninja, helmet, knight, viking, crowns, hats, gas mask, space helmet, robot, pumpkin and more), built on the rig's head for heroes and villains alike; outfit fields hg, hgc (its colour, else its own) and belt. Wardrobe now has tabs: Headgear (a strip with arrows, then its colours), Colours (suit, cape, belt, boots and gloves), Name; the camera comes down close on her (closeUp), facing her, so each change shows. Black in every list. The base is the hero side's: villains still have no wardrobe.

- Keeping their look (Sean, 2026-11-02): the table was made once on walking in and nothing in the wardrobe could reach anyone but the one being played. The wardrobe has a row of the team: picking one makes them the one at the base (setWho) and the others go to the table in their looks (seatTeam). look-test.cjs checks the table and the menu's three against what was chosen.

- The town camera reaches the home base (Sean, the Sandbox: it stopped at the edge of town, so the base stayed in the blurred foreground): its limit on the east runs to 30 m past the base. cam-test.cjs.

- The duel AI, and generals (Sean, 2026-11-02). End duel beside Fight again, and Leave too now, back beside the ring (base.duelBack, no page reload). fighttest.kAI from the foe's level, 0 at 1 and 1 at 5: blocks 18 to 78% of what is thrown at it and answers with a shove, attacks more often (0.8 to 2.3 times), shorter wind-ups (1.25 to 0.7 times); two new moves with their own tells: shove (13, knocks back) and slam (15, all round it, from level 2). Powers but the kick reach a foe on the ground or getting up; knock powers also in the air (canTarget). Generals: horde.gens, 2 a level from 10 s and 36 s, the alien's silver commander and the zombie's green juggernaut, rig Tyrant's with a jetpack, gold diamonds on the map, count towards winning. They fly at her; 16 m away, or Lock on (they come first within 240 m), starts the real fight, o.air: this.top raised over the roofs (airLevel), walked on as if ground. A knockdown, flinging, or a finish drops top to the street: she and it fall, buildings under 45 m collapse on the way (THROUGH THE ROOF!). Both steady for 1.6 s: back up at 20 m/s. A loss knocks her out, 45 health, and the general keeps its damage and comes back in 12 s; the level goes on. ai-test.cjs, gen-test.cjs. Tests that clear a level must also clear genQ and gens.

- Round on the AI: a stream of jabs could stun-lock it at any level; now the better it is the shorter its stun and the more it punches back through it, its big blows can be unstoppable (armor), and close range always chooses through closePick. Ground camera wider: on foot 46 in a crowd and 54 otherwise (was 32 and 46), the duel 26 and the street fight 30 at least (was 20). Pumpkin head in three (Sean loved it): orange, purple and green eyes, stems of 2, 4 and 7 pieces; ribs, sunk sockets with a glow and a bright core, a toothed grin, leaf and vine.

- The mini map goes under nothing (Sean): hidden with a panel open (zd-panel, the Sandbox's Disasters too), paused, on the radio station and on an end screen. No depth blur in level fights.

- A level no longer wins itself (found testing generals): saucer and tripod beams were abducting their own walkers, level 1 fell from 158 to about 80 in a minute with nobody playing. people.heroFoe(j) keeps those beams off a level's enemies, so the count stays until the hero acts. Level 1 is harder for it. She can no longer be left in grabbed by a foe that let go.

- The band game grows up (Sean): the songs drift, so the one steady tempo was on the beat only by luck (onset strength on against off the count: 4.55 / 4.44). beatmap.js holds every beat of each song and what the drums play on it (k, s, b, -), measured from the files by a tracker (scratchpad beats4.cjs: 7.37 / 5.05 and 6.27 / 2.98). Balls fall on real hits, kick to bass or drums, snare to drums or singer. Live PERFECT, GOOD, MISS counts and a CROWD meter; S+ and ALL PERFECT at the end, otherwise N OF M PERFECT. A miss or a tap too soon: lamps spark and flicker, the song crunches through a waveshaper with a squeal (music.bandHurt). Perfects in a row raise hype: fans press in, up to 70 more walk in from the back, flames and fireworks come more often. CROWD 45 or lower: the song skips (music.bandSkip) and no show; at 0 (25 misses from full) the song stops, the band walks off either side, grade F, Try again or Done.

- The villains' lair (Sean: a throne, one of them wants to rule the world; creepier all round, spikes on the walls, chains, cages, skulls; more evil headgear, bigger horns). HomeBase has a side now (useSide: the mode's, villain in Destroy): its own save zday_lair (cfg.lair), its own room 22 by 16 (lairroom.js furnishLair, lairDecor), hanging in a black void. In it: the throne on steps (Sit on the throne: a close view; looks Giant skull, Iron blades, Bone; grows at levels 3, 5, 8), the other two standing either side as henchmen (standTeam), a doom globe with a red mark per town, a hero caught in a cage, skeletons in hanging and floor cages, skull columns and piles, banners, iron spikes and looped chains on the far walls, spikes on the rails, doom screens, braziers with green fire. The basement is The pit: the same dressing, spikes round the ring, the car and bike in black. Small repeated things are instanced (batch). Wardrobe and Plans work per side; 12 evil headgear pieces for villains only, first in their list (headgear.js, fifth field). Reached from the Lair page (Walk into the lair; the Tower page has Walk into the tower), the door goes back to the page. No BASE button in a Destroy level yet: its flight is not the horde's. Fixed: B.door was both a helper and a material, so a base's door box had a function for a material and any world change after placing a base threw; base.placed now resets on leaving a world. Never a burning skyline on the doom screens (no towers, the twin towers rule).

- Band game: a miss now makes it quieter (Sean: it got louder; the distortion path saturated at full scale): the song dips to 0.3, the crunch goes through a 0.07 gain, and the zap and thud are lower. Record your timing (Sean): a button in the set and Timing on the end screen; the song from the top, each tap a ball at that moment over the member nearest the tap (rhythm.js recStart, recTap), Undo, Start over, Done; localStorage zday_band_mine_<song>; played instead of the measured beats until switched back (useMine, not saved). Get the code gives text to send on, so a chart can be built in for everyone: song:ms in base 36.lane, comma separated. A false alarm worth knowing: walk-test takes about 234 s, and a shell timeout shorter than that ends it with "Target crashed" (playwright closing the browser), which is not a game crash.

## Next
- The hero game (Sean, 2026-11-01): see HERO-PLAN.md for the whole plan and build order. Next up: floors inside smashed buildings, then the Sandbox hero lab.

## Earlier plan note
- Sean decided on 2026-10-25 to make the player the hero (emergency commander) instead of dropping zombies. The full plan is DESIGN.md (round, alert levels, units, points, levelling, 5 implementation phases). Sandbox stays at ?mode=sandbox.

## Destroy's LAIR button (2026-11-03)
- The flying levels' bar (flight.js, `inLevel && !fromHorde`; `inLair()` picks the word) has LAIR or BASE between Lock on and QUIT. `toLair()` uses the heroes' `baseGo`/`baseRun`; it reads STOP while going, and STOP or a drag cancels. The lair's tower is placed on the first tap.
- `herogame.js`: `flyUp(x, z)` is the level's flight setup (also sets `F.owner`, because `g.mode` is not set yet on a level started from a new mode), `baseArrive()` fades and goes in with `base.start({ level: this })`, `back()` flies up 70 m over the door with 30 health back.
- While `base.level === this`, `update()` returns: the level cannot be won or lost inside, and it is checked again on coming out. The disasters and rivals go on outside. `base.duelBack` now keeps `level`, so a duel in the pit does not lose the level (this was also wrong for heroes).
- Regression fixed: c665928 (2026-11-02, BASE flies her there) added `if (!this.fromHorde) return` in `arcade()`, so drag and BOOST did nothing in Destroy and in Defend's flying levels (tornado and the mixed ones); only Lock on moved you. Now it returns only if the flight stopped or crashed.
- Defend's flying levels (tornado and the mixed ones) got the same button as BASE (Sean, 2026-11-03): every level now has its way home.

## Test levels, aimed powers, weather jobs, zoom (2026-11-03)
- Test levels (levelmenu.js): in the Sandbox (button in the base and on the bar), all 21 Defend (D1 to D21) and 21 Destroy (X1 to X21) levels with map, enemies and how they play. A run keeps a copy of the fighter in `testWas` and restores it after (HeroGame.hub) or on the next story start, so the story is untouched; the list comes back with how it went.
- Story cap fixed: `s.idx` stopped at PATH.length - 1 (17 towns), so levels 18 to 21 of WAVES (black hole, spill, cold wave, the last) could never be reached. Now WAVES.length - 1, towns wrap.
- A freeze fixed: `people.head` was all zeros before the first frame, so `around()` looped for ever on person 0 when a disaster raised the alarm in the same tick as a world move (D7, the flood in Sunvale, in the story too). Now filled with -1.
- Aimed powers (heropower.js `closest`, `shoot`, `aimStep`): in flight in a level, a power finds the closest walker, machine, general, boss, villain or rival within 130 m (a red ring marks it). A villain's helicopters and, with nobody near, buildings within 90 m too (2 or more hits by height). Generals, villains and rivals only wear down to 30% (Lock on finishes them). The whole-town disasters still go through the old path. Laser cooldown 0.6 s.
- Weather jobs (stormgame.js, `F.stormFor()`): Defend only, flood, dust storm, tsunami, cold wave. Lock on goes to the nearest spot first (before any villain); gauge and action button appear on arrival; the last job calls `finish`. Powers do nothing to the weather itself. The storm is slowed; the flood holds until done. Villains still knock you about while you work (test bot lost 3 knockdowns by not fighting them): watch this.
- Zoom (nozoom.js): the second finger down within 450 ms is cancelled in the band and fight screens as well as the second lift; touchend is also listened for on the touched element, in case it left the page. While zoomed, `html.zd-zoomed *{touch-action:pinch-zoom}` lifts the game's touch-action:none, which had stopped the pinch out, and a line at the top of the visible area says so. Not testable off a real iPhone.
- Pause: top right, 52 px. Compass: above the mini map, hidden with it (minimap.js). Both now children of body, because #zd-side's transform carried position:fixed. The radio line in flight sits right of the compass.

## Destroy for points, weather on your side (2026-11-03; stormgame.js StormWar)
- All Destroy levels: `F.stormFor()` makes a StormWar. Score: your buildings 100 x chain (x3 at most, 4 per step, 4 s to keep it), the storm's buildings 50, wrecked cars 25, a hero stopped +500, a squad +300 (not multiplied). Win: score >= need x 100 and the heroes beaten.
- Jobs every 16 s, at most 2: a rival hero (hidden and gripped, a copy of their figure at the spot; 16 s) or a rescue squad vehicle (22 s). Blue beam. Lock on and the gauge, or 3 hits of any power (`closest` kind 'job'). Finished: storm -25%, or -200 points with no weather. At 0 the storm ends; Unleash brings it back.
- Storm meter: buildings +6, cars +1.5, a job stopped +20. Unleash adds 25% storm. Tornado: size up and it follows you 12 s (your own tornado does not hurt you). Flood: water up (no dam model: a button), over 2.5 m it takes buildings. Dust: 15 s the army does not fire, 3 buildings go. Tsunami: alive, faster; else pick a coast. Cold: 15 s buildings break in one hit, 5 go.
- Balance untested by a person: goals may be too high (X20 is 31,000).

## The kit grows (2026-11-03; kit.js)
- Data in kit.js: a stun's `fx` (col, secs, cage, flash, say, snd, r), a knockback's `shot` (wave, rock, car: one tap) or `beam`, `as`, `wide`, `dps`, `sp` (held, on the laser's or the gust's sweep in the horde). Used by fighttest.js `cast`, `freezeV`, `shot`, `holdStep`; horde.js `cast`, `line`/`line0`, `beam`; heropower.js POWER and WEAK (each borrows an old power's bites). Melee: new ATK names in both fighttest.js and horde.js; `spin` turns the figure round.
- A combo may not use a name twice (afterAtk finds the next by name): `jabL2`, `elbowU2`, `spinKick2`. This was also why Flurry never reached its cross.
- Mini map and compass hide in a street fight (they covered its big words).
- The other 45 of Sean's 60 are in the session's list, not built: see the conversation of 2026-11-03 if asked; Sean: most likely all of them eventually.

## The team in a level (2026-11-03; squad.js)
- `HeroGame.squad`, made on the first `update()` of a round, ended first thing in `finish`. Holds `cur`, `hp[id]` (0 to 1), `downs[id]` (where they lie: a rig on its back, a red beam, an X on the mini map). `step` runs from `update`, also while in the base.
- A knockout: F.state 'down' for 1.3 s (`airKo`), or horde.bite at 0 (`footKo`, after its fall), or a street fight lost (via crash; a general's now too). `ko` picks the next not down and sets `last[side]` for the level; they come in across town from the fall (`far`), so the downed one has to be found; `end()` puts the picked one back. None left: the level is lost ('The whole team is down', or the horde's 'Overrun'). The old 'Knocked out of the sky three times' rule is gone.
- Revive: within 18 m (on foot too) the heartbeat panel opens; 3 taps with the ring on the green; a miss loses one; 28 m away it closes, and does not reopen until you have been 26 m away. Back at 50% health. Tap a downed icon in flight: `F.lock` with `storm: this`, so homeIn's arrival calls `arrive`.
- Health: Destroy and Defend's flying levels through `flyUp` and `back`; the horde now shares one health on foot and in the air in a level (`land`, `liftNow`, `swapTo`). Base: `healPanel`, 9% a second while the pressure is in the green. The free +30 on leaving the base is gone in levels.
- XP and the story's progress go to the fighter who started the level, whoever finishes it.

## Street fights: pace (2026-11-03; fighttest.js, kit.js)
- `fightPace()` scales the fight's dt (Slower 0.8 by default; zday_fight_speed; pause menu). The horde has none of this: Sean has not said if zombie fights are too fast.
- `this.gap`: the foe's idle will not start an attack until 0. Set by vilAtk (wind time + 1.4 - 0.6 kAI) and by your hits (1.1 - 0.4 kAI) unless `sinceAtk` >= 2.6 (fight seconds): then its turn comes whatever it is doing (idle, the next jab, getting up) and that attack is armoured (jabs do not stop it). Your turn: it cannot block (gap > 0). Wind-ups x 1.25, 'WATCH OUT!'. Punched-back chances cut (block 0.4 kAI, stun 0.25 kAI); getting up it charges only by chance of kAI.
- Taps: next blow at 85% of the last; a tap in the first half of the wind-up is dropped; jabs end at 0.3 not 0.24. KB 0.65 on knock and push, kick 10 + 22k, hero flung 13 not 18, a held beam lets go at 13 m. Far away it mostly walks up (charge 30%, not 65%).
- To revert: git revert the commit, or KB, gap and fightPace one at a time.

## Legend, the menu's bar (2026-11-03; herogame.js legend(), game.js showMenu)
- Replaces Commander level under the saucer. That bar read profile.js XP, which only the old Commander and Be The Disaster modes feed (no main menu button), so Defend and Destroy never moved it.
- `legend()` in herogame.js sums each fighter's XP (`zday_hero`) by the side they stand on now, so one who crosses over swings it. lean = (villain - hero) / (villain + hero + 2000), the 2000 a cushion so a first win does not throw it to one end. Titles: Champion of the City (lean up to -0.6), Trusted Hero (up to -0.25), Wild Card, Troublemaker (from 0.25), Public Enemy (from 0.6). A Sandbox test still running (`testWas`) counts as what it saved over.
- Look: marker on a blue to purple to red track, filled from the middle; color hsl(285 + 70 * lean). The line under it is levels won per side (sum of `idx`), in place of rounds, best score and stars.
- Units came off the deck (its upgrades were only for old Commander; the panel, profile.js and commander.js are still there, reachable by ?mode=commander). Roster stays where it was, so the rim has one button on the right. Hidden with the rest of the XP panel on a phone on its side.

## Fight feel from Sean's video (2026-11-03; fighttest.js, audio.js)
Sean sent a 17 second solo dev clip: seven things that make a sword hit feel good, on two knights. What each became in the street fights (fighttest.js, so villains, generals and hero fights; the crowd fights in horde.js are untouched):
- Flash: `Body.flash` swaps every mesh on the rig to one white material for 130 ms, real time (`flashStep`, `unflash`), for blows over 1 damage on either fighter, at most 4 a second (`took`). A block does not flash.
- Sound: new `smack` (sharp, on the impact, over a quieter thud or crash) and `clang` (a block, a parry, armor) in audio.js. Rendered in code, never heard by Claude: judge by ear.
- Sparks: `spark` takes a direction, 11 small quick sparks back off the blow, and a small four-pointed star on the spot (`star`). The ring only over R 2.5, or it hides the white body.
- Health bar: `.ob` over the foe's head from the first hit (`obStep`), what a blow took stays white a moment, green over 50%, amber, red under 25%, a small pop on a hit. The two top bars stay.
- Knockback: small blows already pushed (about 0.5 m). Added: `rk` a lean back on every hit over any state, `stag` the feet stepping on a head hit (`REACT`), armor gives 0.18 m.
- Blood became soot and scuffs, the rule being tame violence: `scuff` darkens the smooth body's vertex colors, a new mark per ninth of health gone, on both fighters; `cleanBody` restores. Custom rigs with no smooth body get none.
- Death: zero health holds the blow 0.18 s; `C.dazed` a slump; a finished fighter lands in `C.ko` with a bounce (`koT`) and lies still 1.5 s (2 s after the sky) instead of 0.5 before the result. Finishers still end the fight, as Sean designed.
Poses learned from its frames: `punch()` makes jabs and crosses three clear poses: a coil away (COIL_T 0.09 more before the blow), a deep lunge (lead knee bent, back leg long, `lungeOf`), held HOLD_T 0.07. `FOE_JAB` the same with no coil. CLIPS itself is unchanged, so flying blows keep theirs. Camera nearer for bigger figures: 23 m street, 22 plaza (was 30, 26), side margin 3.6 (was 5).

## The neck drag rebuilt, and a hand that can hold (2026-11-03; fighttest.js)
Sean: one hand on the enemy's neck, the other punching, while they fly through the air; the arms only pointed out, and tapping moved no arm at all.
- `reachArm(arm, T)`: two bone IK from the joints' own lengths, sends a hand to a place in the world. `Body.ik` is a list of { arm, at(out) }, solved in `pose` after the clip and before the smooth body is swept. `go` clears it. This is the grip the Pose Lab will use.
- `gripStep(d, f, ...)`, the same for the player (`drag`) and the foe (`vdrag`): d flies level (`C.drag`), f hangs off the hand at `DRAG_LEAN` so they make a V (flat and stacked looked wrong; do not put them back on top of each other). One hand on the neck, f's own hand clawing at it, legs going (`fl`). They rise and dip together on `dragBase`; under 0.2 the heels tear up the ground. Whoever grips is posed after whoever is gripped, so the hand goes to this frame's neck.
- The free fist: drawn back over the shoulder, into the face at 0.08 s (`pT`, `pk`, chest turning with `tw`), on its own every 0.6 s or on a tap; the hit lands on that frame (`gripHit`). Auto punches count toward the fling too, which only ever makes the last throw bigger.
- Plough by the clock (`plowDue`, every 0.07 s): it was per frame, so a faster screen threw more rock, and it buried them. Chunks smaller and lower. d.vx, d.vz set for the cape, which lay flat over the body at no speed. Camera in the drag off to the side (was behind the one dragged, who then filled the screen).

## The Pose Lab (2026-11-03; poses.html, poselab.js, poses.js, posedata.js)
Sean: see the moves frame by frame and move limbs to where they should be; all animations feel stiff. Its own page like the Roster, no town to load; the Poses button on the saucer's rim, or poses.html?move=C.drag.
- The real thing: two fighttest.js Bodies posed by the game's own code. `MOVES` (fighttest.js, built after THROWN, FOE_JAB and REACT) lists the 47 moves it can open, with id, group, and who they are done to.
- Editing: drag a hand or foot and the limb follows (reachArm, the lab's reachLeg, angles read back into the pose); drag an elbow, knee, head, chest, waist or hips to turn it round the line of sight; sliders for the fine work. A change on a frame with no key makes a key there. Undo, per clip. Camera: one finger turns, two zoom and slide; Front, Side, Top; Cape and Dots hide those.
- Timing tab: add, delete, move a key by a frame, copy and paste a pose, mirror, how it gets to the key (the easings in plain words). Stage tab: on the neck drag the real numbers (height, along, hang, punch every; `C.drag.pair`); on others the other fighter is only there to look at.
- Pins: `key.pin = { L: 'neck' }`; `pinAt` (clips.js) gives how far on and to what, eased between keys; `Body.pins` (fighttest.js) lerps from the pose's hand to `PINS`. Needs `body.foe`, set at the start of a fight, unless it is an own place.
- The neck drag is now data: `C.drag` keys (fist back, on the face with the `hit` key, held, back) run on the punch's clock (gripStep sets d.ct from pT); C.dragged pins its hand to its own neck. Same measurements as before the change: hand to neck worst 0.18 m for the player, 0.10 m for the foe.
- Saving: every change into localStorage zday_poses (poses.js); `applyPoses` lays BAKED (posedata.js) then those over the moves when fighttest.js loads, so the game on that device plays them. A key keeps `src`, its place in the move as written, and `warp` moves each ATK's `at` and `end` with the keys, so the blow lands on its key. **To make Sean's changes permanent:** he sends the text from Save, Export ("POSES {...}"); that object goes in posedata.js BAKED.
- Lab copies every move's keys on open, because many share a pose object as written (GUARD).
- Limits: the foe's own moves are timed in the fight code, so moving their keys changes the look, not when the blow lands. Spin by the ankles and Swung have arms or body set by code (`armsA`, `bodyX`), and the lab says so. Walking legs, recoil and the like go on top in a fight. Partners other than the neck drag's are placed by eye, not as the game places them.

## The story's globe (Sean, 2026-11-03; threats.js, storymap.js, herogame.js, base.js, globe.js)
Sean: pick Defend or Destroy, the globe comes up, spin it until you see a disaster you want to play; the higher your level, the higher its level. After a city, back to base; dead, you wake there by a regeneration pod; the Map station flashes for new trouble.
- `threats.js`: one board per side (zday_threats). SLOTS: always 1 villains, 1 aliens, 2 worm, 2 weather, and 1 or 2 rotating (randomN). One threat per city; tsunami only on a coast (NEEDS). Win: the city clears and the same slot hits elsewhere. Loss: it stays. After any level the oldest rotating one moves on. Change the mix in SLOTS, WEATHER, RANDOM.
- Danger = your level + a little for the bigger towns (`danger`). `this.ix` is danger - 1 in a story level, s.idx in a test level, and takes s.idx's old place in LAB.villain, the army, rivals, buildings to bring down, XP, zombie and alien numbers. New: hitK on the on-foot blows (horde `bite`), heroTough on the giants' health.
- `storymap.js openMap`: the card, the fighter button (the old pick screen), the screen frame in the base; G.threats gives globe.js its markers (the place labels turned into icons, drawn as SVG in threats.js so no phone lacks an emoji; animation in CSS). The globe's own spin is controls.autoRotate, stopped while a card is open.
- The tower screen is gone. Its skills are on the base's computer (`skillsHtml`). `hub(msg, at)` now means the base (test runs still go back to the Test levels list). Won: `card` with Back to base lit. Lost: straight to the base at the pod. The base's door between levels opens the map.
- Base: `storyStations` adds the pod (always in a story, heals by squad.js's pressure game in a level, replacing the floating Heal button; between levels it just says so, since you are whole) and the Map station (not during a level): wall screen flashing red, row button flashing, a toast, while `freshOnes` is not empty. Opening a level clears it. Not in the Sandbox's base.
- A town fought in is rebuilt for the next level there (g.usedTown), else the riot won in seconds with no people left to riot.
- Sandbox: the numbered test levels are untouched.
- A page load in ?mode=hero or ?mode=villain: the base is made after the mode, so the HeroGame constructor waits for it.

## Room to move in a fight (Sean, 2026-11-03; fighttest.js)
Sean, against an alien general: dashing or floating away he was slowly pulled back, forced to mash or block. Not planned: from before the joystick, the idle hero walked to the foe by herself over 6 m at 5.5 m/s, against a stick of 5. Gone; a tap from far off still arrives by the lunge (30 m/s). The stick is now RUN, 7.5 m/s, over the foe's 6 and 6.5, and at the ring's edge you slide round it rather than stopping dead. Getting away earns the foe's charge, which can be dashed for a counter.

## Stuck on sidewalks in a fight (Sean, 2026-11-03; fighttest.js streetStep, open)
Sean got a general to zero and could not reach him, stuck on the far side of rubble; also stuck on random sidewalks. Street fights used the people's walking grid (P.walkable), which blocks rubble and the edges of things, and a blocked step was undone whole, rush at the foe included, so a beaten foe could be out of reach for good. Now `open`: anywhere but inside a standing building. Sean's recording showed the soft lock: picking a finisher turns the stick off and rushes the foe; the rush was undone, so she looped for ever, only the swipe's glide still working. `watchdog`: a beaten foe not finished in 25 s, or 5 s once a finisher is picked and nothing is happening, is a win; a downed hero left 25 s is a loss. Both fighters are kept inside the ring. Pause, End this fight (`endNow`): a win if the foe is beaten, else a loss. A blocked step slides along the wall. The lunge and the finisher's run ignore walls and always arrive (up to 1.8 s, the whole ring); ending inside a wall puts you out at the nearest street.

## Bug pass (2026-11-03, asked for by Sean)
A bot plays whole fights with the player's inputs in several towns watching for stuck states, a walk through the awkward flows, and a read through of the code. Scripts were throwaway, in the session's scratchpad. Fixed: the lair's Map button walked her to the Plans table and never opened the map (station moved); a stale saved pose could stop the game loading (poses.js checks every saved move); disasters ending by themselves gave a free TOWN SAVED (now "It passed without you", the city stays; the ones you cannot stop come with a villain to beat, as the old road had them, `partnerOf`); the last fighter crossing over lost the result card; two maps on Escape; a wrong-shaped or out-of-date zday_threats (dropped or filtered).

## The neck drag's camera pulled back (Sean, 2026-11-03; fighttest.js DRAG_CAM)
Far too close at 5 m back, 6 m aside, 3.2 m up, and pulling that same low camera further back only put it inside buildings. So the drag now has the fight's own camera, which rides high and swings round what is in the way, zoomed out to DRAG_CAM (36 m, against 23). Change that one number to taste. horde.js had its own copy of the low camera; gone too, with its own wide camera coming in to 32 m for a drag.

## Through the buildings, points, and picking the move (Sean, 2026-11-04)
Sean: the game is not as fun as it should be; flying through buildings was the fun part; Destroy should be about wrecking; the spin was more fun when you controlled it.
- Flying (flight.js arcade): the rule that lifted her over any roof near or ahead is gone, except on the way to the base (goBase). Moving, she settles to CRUISE (13 m), unless the up and down arrows were used in the last 6 s (ownHeight). plough does the rest, as it always did. In Defend too: smashing a building costs fear and reputation there, as it did.
- Destroy (stormgame.js StormWar.smashed, from burstOut): 100 + 20 a metre of height, rounded to 50, times a chain up to x10 (one up every 2, 5 s to the next), big on the screen. The goal is R.need * 400 to match.
- The neck drag (fighttest.js dragSmash, horde.js drag): through everything however tall, in every street fight. The ring moves with them (cx, cz) and only the town's edge turns them; streetStep leaves them alone. Up in the air they come down to the street for it. Player's drag: 10 damage and a harder final throw per building.
- GRAB: a grab lands (state clutch, foe pinned), time slows, and three buttons (.grabs, grabPick): SPIN (spinP: tap winds it up, it runs down by itself, GRAB lets go, the toss goes where they are facing then, as far as the turns earned), THROW, DRAG (2.6 s, 15 damage, not a finisher: dragShort). No choice in about 2 s: thrown. Holding GRAB does nothing now. On-foot levels' own grab (horde.js) not changed yet.

## The fights in the air (Sean, 2026-11-04; fighttest.js)
Sean: hand to hand felt clunky; limit the area, always in the air, the powers and finishers using the town. Every story fight (generals, villains) is o.air. Practice robot and the plaza stay on the ground.
- A fixed level, AIR_H (14 m) over the street, so buildings stand in the fight. The space is AIR_R (22 m) with a glowing ring you can see (airRing). Only a finisher or a drag takes them to the street (airStep), and they rise again after a short drag.
- Any fighter meeting a building brings it down, however tall (airStep). Thrown through one, the foe takes 12 and you gain power; thrown yourself, 6.
- Thrown, they catch themselves on the level (stun, hurt) rather than lying on nothing; so GRAB by the feet of a downed foe no longer comes up in the air. The grab's three moves are the way in.
- Flying: Body.fly, leanK: legs trailing, leaning into speed, a bob; no feet looked for (grounded). The stick is AIR_RUN 11 m/s and she faces the way she goes; the foe's walking is 1.35 times faster.
- HURL (hurl, hurlStep, HURL_COST 30): the nearest building within 60 m torn up and thrown (32), or a car (20). It lands where the foe was when it left, so a moving foe can be missed.

## The tornado as a villain (Sean, 2026-11-04; livestorm.js)
Sean: turn the weather disasters more into villains. First one. Defend's tornado has a cartoon face on the funnel (27 m up, always towards you), a health bar (#zd-boss), and steers after you tearing up the town. It throws cars and rubble (hurl) at where you are heading, so stay moving; 8 damage, faster and in pairs as it weakens. Lock on (flight.js, was WindGame) is a flying punch from up to 330 m away: 5 (less with danger), 2.6 times that straight after a throw while its mouth hangs open, half as much again frozen; you bounce off, 0.8 s to breathe, it shrinks. The camera keeps it in view as with the giants (F.live stands in for the boss). Powers still hurt it. Touching the funnel still throws you off (WindGame.thrownFrom); the old ring mini game is no longer reached by Lock on. Next, as Sean described: the tsunami (a face; freeze sections, punch the frozen parts, then fight what is left hand to hand), the dust storm the same, the flood a villain with a hose while you punch sinkholes to drain it.

## Punchier melee (Sean, 2026-11-04; fighttest.js)
Why it felt clunky, by the rules: a foe blocked up to 78% of opening blows and a block knocked you back into its counter; every 2.6 s it powered through your combo.
- The block: only on a combo's first blow, 8 to 28%, no stagger or counter: 30% goes through and two blows break it. FOE_TURN is 4.5 s.
- The counter (.cmb, addHit, hitK): hits in a row on the screen, 1.8 s to the next, each worth 4% more up to +50%. Being hit clears it.
- Enders launch: the last blow of a combo sends them up (state air). Tap goes after them (juggle, 40 m/s, jugWait): up to three blows, the third spikes them (v.spiked), 14 more on the level. A hard hit in any fight with a combo of more than one blow. Heavy, dash blow and counter still send them flying, into buildings.
- Dash blow: tap straight out of a dash (ATK.dashBlow, 20, flung far).
- The six (STYLES): speed of blows, damage, flying speed, default combo if the kit is left on its default ('combo'), a colour. Their signatures, from SIG_AT (8) hits in a row, tap when READY (15 s between; sigGo, sigStep, invulnerable while it lasts): Nova SUPERNOVA, Dragonfly RAZOR WINGS, Tempo TIME STOP, Tyrant WORLD BREAKER, Blur AFTERIMAGE, Weaver WEB SLAM.
- Not done: the on-foot levels' own melee (horde.js) has none of this; the foe's own moves are unchanged.

## The storms as villains (Sean, 2026-11-04; livestorm.js LiveFront, GhostFace, herogame.js wallLevel)
Sean, with a picture of a sand storm with a skull in it: they should look as they did before, large and towering, with a ghostly face in them; the cartoon look does not fit. So no meshes of its own (my first go, cartoon eyes on blue and brown lumps: do not go back). Defend only; replaces their tap in the green jobs (stormFor returns null while F.wallE lives). The real Tsunami (height 44), Haboob (light), ColdWave, slowed to cross in CROSS_T (120 s), wrecking what it reaches; it reaching the far side loses the level. GhostFace: painted on a canvas per storm in its own colours (GHOST), soft at the edges, two additive eyes; the tornado has it too. FRONT_S sections across the town: sw.cap (water.js SweepFront, kept even with no boss on the screen) holds the wall's height down where a section is hurt, so a blow tears a real gap. Lock on: flying punch at the nearest, 3 to break. Freeze: nearest and neighbours for COLD_T, one punch shatters, 25 to the sides; on the cold front any power cracks it. Last piece: fightFoe with a tinted rig; e.saved, e.lastHp, e.make bring it back after the base or a knockout. e is the level's own (e.own), e.real the disaster, e.cleanup ends it. Next: Sean may want the flood's water and Deluge looked at the same way.

## The flood as a villain (Sean, 2026-11-04; livestorm.js LiveFlood)
Defend only. Captain Deluge (Body with a tinted Blur rig at 2.6 times size, a hand held to the nozzle by Body.ik) flies between spots over the town with a hose and a jet, blasting at you every 3.4 to 2.2 s (6). The water (e.waterM, a plane; FLOOD_T 75 s to fill, FLOOD_H 10 m) rises while the hose runs and at full loses the level (M.finish). PUNCH A SINK HOLE (#zd-drain, drain, diveStep, hole): she dives from wherever she is and bounces back up; -20% at once, more while the hole lasts (7 s), and it takes the nearest building; 1.1 s between. Lock on: a flying punch, 11, only 5 with the water over 60% (the bar says so, the button glows); Freeze stops him and the hose 4 s and punches do 1.6 times. At zero he drops the hose and a last Lock on is the fight by hand (fightFoe with v.fightRig). It keeps itself on the event (e.saved, e.lastHp, e.make), as LiveWall does; the water stays through the hand to hand. Tune FLOOD_T, HOLE_DRAIN.

## Private preview link
- https://claude.ai/artifact/Jemm5Sxje8y9XvJxcFNPwN (private, Sean only). Page source: artifact/zday-city.html.
- To update: `npx vite build --base ./`, then publish artifact/zday-city.html with files {"assets/<bundle>.js": "dist/assets/<bundle>.js"} and change the script src in the page to the new bundle name (the hash changes each build).

- The tornado fight, played with what it throws (Sean, 2026-11-04: the Lock on punch can go; parry, laser, and a grab that spins it and throws it back harder). livestorm.js LiveTornado: no flying punch. A throw slows to half inside SLOW_R 32 and wears a ring sprite: yellow inside PARRY_R 26, green inside GRAB_R 16. PUNCH (#zd-parry; space too, flight.js lockOn calls live.parry) sends it back for PARRY_HIT 14 and POW_PARRY 34. GRAB (#zd-catch) is F.ring: SPINS 3 in SPIN_T 1.3 s, spinCam close by, then THROW_HIT 30 and POW_THROW 50. A throw passing within NEAR_R 13 unhit is CLOSE!, POW_DODGE 20. A press with nothing in reach is MISSED, 0.35 s lockout. LASER (#zd-laser) is held, armed at 100, LASER_DPS 13, LASER_DRAIN 40 (2.5 s, about 32), no throws or chasing under it, pauses when let go, kept in e.pow. Damage reduces with villain level. The Lock on button and the kit's power buttons are hidden for this fight and restored in dispose: those powers never hurt the story tornado (heropower ringF, tw.frozen is set only by windgame). The other storms keep Lock on (charge, ringStep shared from this class). Bot, dev: all buttons, whiff, early grab, win, no errors. Not played by a person on a phone; the windows and damage are guesses.

- The detailed look, a test on Tyrant (Sean, 2026-11-04: is there any reason they cannot look closer to a painted hero; yes, build a test). src/hdbody.js, page fighter.html (src/lookpage.js: old and new side by side, moves, night and day, frame rate). rig({hd}) on the same joints, so every move and Pose Lab edit plays on it: villainRig gives hd to Tyrant with no outfit (`of`), so generals, the robot and a dressed Tyrant keep the old body. hdSmooth sweeps tubes with muscle in shape() through the joints, up is the back so the paint's seam is hidden; their UVs are rewritten onto a 1024 painted atlas (REG torso, leg, arm; numbers baked to colour and normal). hdParts: sculpted head by vertex displacement and vertex colour (those colours are linear), hair cap and clumps, fists, bracers, greaves, boots, belt, collar, cloth on the shoulders, all solid on the joints and counted as keep. The Cape takes hd: 11 x 15, wider, folds, torn alpha. About 25k triangles against about 3k, 28 draw calls. Weakest part: the face. Not done: the other five fighters, shadow of the torn holes, close-up cinematics. Not measured on a real phone.

- Two bodies and a wardrobe (Sean, 2026-11-04: not six people, two bodies plus outfits; wings, jet packs or capes on the back; that detail in helmets and outfits; a female body with flowing hair). hdbody.js: hdLook(name, of) resolves body, colours and pieces from the outfit over DEF per fighter (defaults: Nova and Weaver female, the rest male; six characters stay, with their names and fighting styles). The painted sheet holds shade (red), trim (green), boots (blue), skin (alpha 0), recoloured in suitMat's shader; one sheet per body, painted the first time it is needed. New outfit keys, all optional so old saves load: body, hair, hairc, skin, beard, back, sh, chest, arm, leg (and the old suit, cape, belt, dark, hg, hgc). WARD lists the choices; base.js wardrobe tabs Head, Body, Armor, Colours, Name. Who has it: rigFor(name, of, hd = true), flight's flyer, the fight test's Tyrant, the Pose Lab. Who stays plain: statues (baseroom), a downed teammate (squad), generals, the ring's robot, storm foes, town villains and rival heroes (heroes.js, villains.js own rigs), the roster page. Solid geometry is cached by piece, never rebuilt on a redress. Cape.hooks: what moves with the cape (hair, wings, jet flame), since cape.update is called everywhere a fighter moves. FlowHair: chains of points in world space (verlet, gravity, wind from the cape's lift, the head and chest as spheres) drawn as ribbons in the head's space, each with a fixed width direction. HAIR_ALL and HAIR_NONE say what each headgear leaves of the hair. Headgear: all get a surface grain; helmet, knight, spiked, viking, crown, cowl remade with rims, rivets and crests; overlord and ironcrown given trim. The other 38 only have the grain. Weaver's six arms use the bracers on all six, not looked at closely. Not measured on a real phone; the face is still the weakest part.

- Faces and fit (Sean, 2026-11-04: improve the faces, with a female reference; headgear floats over the head; a full face mask, paint job or robot look; more headgear detail). src/hdface.js: the head is lofted from profiles (TW width, TF front, TB back, down n from crown to chin), forms() pushes the features out of the front, UVs unroll round the head (u from the angle, v from the height). faceMat paints a 1024 x 512 canvas per look (cached, 14 kept): shading, nose, lips, eyes, brows hair by hair, stubble and goatee, the hair's edge. look.face: natural, paint, skull, mask, android, robot, with facea, faceb, eyec (android and robot have bump and emissive canvases, and no hair). The hair too: hairEnd says how far down it grows at each angle, capGeo follows the head with that edge, locksGeo lays strands over it, FlowHair's roots start under it. Hair and head geometry are shared per body. headgear.js: on a detailed head addHeadgear scales each piece to the head it sits on (0.82, 0.985, 0.904 male, 0.745, 0.908, 0.852 female) with FIT per piece, because all were made for the old, rounder head; that was the floating. HIDE_HEAD: what covers the whole head replaces it, so no nose comes through. All 45 now have detail except that the pumpkins, overlord, plague, reaper, demon mask, iron maw and spike halo were already detailed and only gained the grain or trim. Wardrobe Body tab: Face row, its two colours, Eyes. Not measured on a real phone: a fighter is about 30k triangles and the head alone about 9k.

- Phone result for the detailed fighters (Sean, 2026-11-04, older iPhone): fighter.html Old 60 fps 1.9 ms, New 60 fps 3.0 ms; a real fight felt smooth. The Samsung has not been measured, nor Both on the page.

- Everyone in the detailed bodies, and any skin (Sean, 2026-11-04: yes to the rollout; skin from super pale, nearly white, to tan, and all colours, blue, green, red, black). hdface.js tones(skin) works out shadow, light, lips and lids from the colour: a warm human colour shades warm, anything else shades towards its own dark, near black gets its features from light. base.js SKINS (15, nearly white to deep) and SKINF (18 colours), plus a colour input data-skinp ('Choose your own'). Generals (horde.genRig), the ring's robot and the roster's robot leader (face robot, breastplate, no back), storm foes (FRONTS rig, FOE_RIG: skin and hair of the storm), downed teammates, the bus-stop villain in stormgame, statues (stone over the detailed body), the Roster page (heroModel, villainModel), town villains (Villain) and rival heroes (Hero). villains.js detail(): an hd rig not asked for always is the detailed body only within HD_NEAR 60 of the camera; beyond that the plain figure in the same colours (hdFar), its hair not simulated. What still uses the old body: band.js's figures. What stands still needs cape.update twice after updateMatrixWorld, or its hair and cape are never laid out. The switch at HD_NEAR was checked by numbers (hdOn), not seen in the town: the sandbox camera would not frame them. Six detailed fighters near the camera at once has not been tried on a phone.

- The wardrobe redesigned (Sean, 2026-11-04: the character is blocked by the options; his iPhone screenshot: Safari leaves about 375 x 636, much less than the 390 x 760 the bots had used, so test at that from now on). src/wardrobe.js, opened by base.js open('costume'), held as B.ward and B.panel, closed by B.close. Its own screen over a hidden #zd-base: the fighter on a stage of its own, the room's meshes hidden and its lights made neutral (restored on close), a backdrop round everything else, fov 30, near 0.25, far 60. Tabs Head, Face, Hair, Body, Armor, Back, Colours, Name in one row; each row of choices scrolls sideways; the sheet is 42% of the height at most, beside the figure when held sideways on a phone. W.cam frames the head or the whole figure from what is left above the sheet, with setViewOffset to centre it there and a distance that fits it: head for Head, Face, Hair; the back for Back; Close up or Whole figure by a button; drag to turn. stand() poses the fighter, fists down, in place of the base's idle. All the old outfit keys kept; the tests: wardui-test measures the projected head and feet against the sheet at four sizes.

## Each fighter's own powers (Sean, 2026-11-04; kit.js, kitmenu.js, guard.js)
- zday_kits: a kit per fighter, keyed by id (stun, knock, melee, fin1, fin2, keep). START in kit.js gives the six different picks and those stay open to them. An older save's zday_kit goes to whoever was last played on each side (zday_hero's last) and those picks stay open (keep). loadKit(who), saveKit(who, k): always say who. The melee's own style is now just the start pick ('combo' no longer means "their own").
- opensAt(slot, id): the first three of a list (two finishers) open at level 1, the rest spread over levels 2 to 20, by the list's order. isOpen also counts START and keep. The Sandbox passes level 99. Picks made in the Sandbox stay equipped in the story until changed.
- The menu: kitPanel (fighter chips with Main or Alternate and level, Make X your main, wheels, and the skills under them on the base's computer). kitWheels: 5 wheels in a two column grid. A wheel left on a locked pick keeps the last open one.
- TEAM: matesOf(who) gives whoever else stands on their side (up to 2), a button each, one meter. An alternate uses their stun: Guardian Shield (STUNS[id].self) flies to you and puts it on you, anything else hits the foe for 25 and lays that stun on them (stunFrom). In a crowd: comes through and stuns those round you (mateRing), or the shield. A downed alternate cannot come (mateDown). mateRig keeps one rig per fighter and outfit so a call builds nothing.
- Guardian Shield: GuardFx soak(n), 7 s, soaks 35, heals 15 (in the air scaled to the hull, 8 s cool down, on HeroPower). F.damage asks power.soak.
- Skills: the ones that opened moves did nothing since the kit took over, so TREE is now only stats that work (Heavy hands LAB.powerK, Afterburn, Deep well LAB.energyK, Tough, Team spirit LAB.teamK); HS.load drops unknown skill ids, which refunds their points. Half the XP of a level goes to the two not played, not in test runs (shareXp).

## All the powers and finishers (Sean, 2026-11-04; powerfx.js, fightclips.js, finishers.js)
- The other 45 of the list are in: 24 stuns, 23 knockbacks, 23 melee styles, 20 finishers in kit.js, in the order they open. Sean's older five (Fire blast, Lightning, Wind blast, Shield bubble, Telekinesis) stay out of the wheels: Fire jet, Lightning bolt and Guardian Shield cover the first ones; Telekinesis is only in Mind lift.
- Stuns: fx.look (stunLook in powerfx.js: what shows round the foe while it lasts) and fx.clip (STUN_POSE: the pose, which now really plays: a frozen foe used to hold its old pose because pose(0) never blended; stunLive). this.stLook is the look (this.look is the camera's). fx.soft: no hold, on its own clock (Slow field: v.slowT scales the foe's time); fx.slip: they fall (Ice floor). Mirror maze draws the foe's own meshes again through a reflection matrix (no second rig, it moves as they do).
- Knockbacks with ar (the crowd's shape: ['throw', R], ['ring', R], ['cone', R], ['line', L, w]) go through runShot(E, id, K, to, hit): shot says what flies; to is the live foe or a fixed spot; hit is the fight's push or the crowd's burst. ShotList (E.fxa): things that run a moment and clean up. h state 'shotMove' while a shot carries the fighter (Meteor fist, Sonic boom). E.top is the ground: the crowd sets it only for the call.
- In the air everything is a coloured bolt with a ring; its bite is that of what it is most like (like in kit.js). The looks are in the street and crowd fights only.
- Melee: MELEE_ATK in fightclips.js, one table for both fights (street: dmg, react, knock or kb, reach; crowd: r, arc, daze). Extra fields: fx (meleeFx: shock, flame with a burn, ice, rock, sand, slash, lamp, pound), dash, hop, pop (launch, then the juggle: MELEES[x].jug blows), freezeIt, blind, breaks (through a guard). MELEES[x].prop: addProp puts it on the fighter's rig (blade, staff, claws, rock, shock, flame, ice, tail, lamp). Sword and claws are light, with sparks: violence stays tame. New clips are in the Pose Lab (Weapons and styles; FP. and SP. poses too).
- Finishers: FIN_SCRIPTS, a list of phases { T, on, step, end } over E.d and E.t. FinRun steps them on the real clock (rdt), a tap adds to E.pow (0 to 1), which the last blow reads. The same scripts run for the fighter on the foe (finGo, finEnd), the foe on the fighter (vfStart picks from the foe's own kit: set a villain's finishers on the computer and they use them on you) and on one taken from a crowd (t is a proxy onto the people arrays; the blow on the ground bursts those round it). States fdo and fget. Team finisher uses mateRig for whoever else is on the doer's side.
- Tests in the scratchpad: pow-test (stun, knock, crowd), mel-test, fin-test (hero, foe, crowd), air-test, kit-test. Dev only where they import /src/kit.js. On the dev server an import in the page can be a second copy of kit.js after a hot edit, so set F.power.list directly there.
- Not verified by a person: any of it on a phone, whether the balance is fair (damage and costs are first guesses), whether the unlock order is the right one.

## TEAM in the air (Sean, 2026-11-04; heropower.js, teambtn.js, squad.js)
- HeroPower.team: the horde's meter when up from a crowd level, otherwise F.teamM (emptied at a level's start). Fills 2.5 a second in flight, 8 a power hit, 10 a ram, 12 a blow in a storm fight, all times LAB.teamK.
- A story level's team icons (squad.js) are the buttons: badge with the stun, a ring for the meter, a glow when ready; tap calls them, in the air (power.teamGo) or on foot (H.teamGo). Down, a tap still flies you to them. The Sandbox has no icons, so #zd-flyteam, the same look (teamUiStep).
- teamGo: Guardian Shield flies to you and shieldUp. Others go for mateTarget: the storm being fought (live.mateHit: tornado 16 by blow, a wall a punch's worth on the nearest section, Deluge 11), or closest() (shoot from where they are, more on crowds, machines and bosses, freezeIt), or the nearest disaster that powers can hurt. Nothing near: the meter is kept and a tip says so. fxFrom: where effect's beam starts.

## The storm fights and the villains behind the disasters (Sean, 2026-11-04; livestorm.js, foepower.js)
- Sean took option A: the tornado's controls on all three, a twist each. LiveFront (tsunami, haboob, coldwave) borrows LiveTornado's armUp, armDown, itemStep, cue, parry, grab, spin, gain, laser. Lock on is gone in these fights (charge just parries). e.heroHp is the storm's health; the wall's height follows it (sw.cap); the face follows you along the wall (fk). Crossing the town in CROSS_T (120 s) still loses it; the bar under its health shows how far. Blows shove it back: pushBack takes from sw.F, no further than e.f0, where it started (tsunami 2.4 m a point, others 0.8).
- Twists: STAGE says when. Tsunami: swellGo, a tap screen (Mash), the hero flies to the face, hold it to 100% and blow 20 with 60 m back, or it surges 24 m and hurts. Dust: a fog div (z 2, thinner with each lightning), what it throws has a bright outline, a flare on the face 0.7 s before a throw; devil(): an item with bonus 14 and onMiss, drifting past: let by it heals 5, GRABbed it hits for 44. Cold: inhale (inhT), then duelGo, beams meeting at Q.u, a tap +0.05, its drift 0.17 a second up with level; 1 is a blow of 26 and meter, 0 is damage and an ice block to tap out of (4 s at most).
- Villains: STORM_FOES (name, power, rig, says). stormFoe(F, key, done) pushes an event { type: 'stormfoe', alive } into round.evs, so the level goes on until it is beaten, and sets F.wallE to it, whose make gives a LiveFoe: the rig hovering in front of you, a word, 1.8 s, then F.fightFoe(v). A knockout comes back to a LiveFoe with Lock on to fight again. Tornado TWISTER, tsunami RIPTIDE, dust THE DUST DEVIL, cold JACK FROST. storyTornado ignores a tornado with heroHp <= 0.
- Worm and kaiju: horde.countStep no longer ends a story boss level at 0; HeroGame.update makes the controller with H.ctrlMake (a general in H.gens, with q.power), ends the disaster, and after 2 s H.genFight if genFree, else genStep brings them. Beaten (q.gone), H.finish(true). THE BURROWER and THE BEAST KEEPER. Not in the Sandbox's own boss fights, nor on the Destroy side.
- foepower.js foeShot(T, kind), from the foe's wind-up (vilAtk 'shot', state vcast) when o.foe.power is set, every 3 to 5 s: tornado runners that turn after you (two below half health), water lobbed on marks, an ice fan that freezes a moment (chill), dust runners that blind (blind), tremor spikes along the ground, roar a cone shown first. Captain Deluge has water too.
- Tests: storm-test (a bot plays each through to the card), ctrl2-test (a controller from the air, a loss, each power forced). parry-test's tornado ending is out of date: the level now goes on to Twister.
- Not verified by a person: on a phone, how hard the tap screens are, damage and timing (first guesses). Tap screens cover everything but the top bar.

## The check mark test (Sean, 2026-11-05; fighttest.js chkUi, chkDown, chkMove, chkUp, chkGo, chkRead)
- Sean: melee and powers are button mashing; try drawn strokes in the combos, avoiding a plain swipe down (it takes you out of full screen; Sean: mainly on a tablet). Robot fight only (o.robot), so nothing else changes.
- A dashed box bottom right, a faint check mark in it, the stroke drawn in gold as you go. Down-and-right, then further up-and-right, read at the lift by chkRead (the lowest point is the corner; angles and lengths are forgiving). It starts ATK.checkUp: an uppercut that launches (pop), then taps are the juggle.
- A stroke heading down and right waits for the lift, so that it does not dash: straight, it dashes then; bent wrong, it says so and does nothing. Any other swipe dashes at once, as everywhere. Moving by the time a hold would start: not a block. A stroke started on a hold (the block came up first) still counts, and drops the block.
- The box shows how many were read of how many tried, for judging it on a phone. On this fight only, touchstart and touchmove are cancelled on the overlay (not on its buttons) and overscroll is off, so the browser should not get the stroke's downward part and leave full screen; the first leg can also be very shallow (7 degrees down is enough). Whether that holds can only be seen on the phone. If it does not, the same idea on a shape with no downward part.

## Strokes, second test (Sean, 2026-11-05: loved the check mark; how for all moves, and dashes?)
- The rule: one straight stroke is a dash, a stroke with a corner is a move, a lift ends a stroke. So dash down, lift, dash up is two dashes; down and up without lifting is the there-and-back shape. Robot fight only still.
- No box or border: the right of the screen from under the bars to over the buttons (45% across, as the joystick's edge). A key above the buttons, the hint hidden there. chkLegs cuts the path to its corners; chkRead: check (uppercut), caret up-down (stun slot), mirror of the check (knockback slot), corner up-right (grab), there-and-back any way (heavy). Shapes are for slots, not one per move: 5 or 6 shapes cover all 60 powers, whichever the fighter holds. Buttons still work.
- chkMode, a button by Restart, saved in zday_dash_mode: at once (the dash starts at 26 px as everywhere; a corner at the lift cancels it, refunds the power and does the move) or on lift (nothing until the lift). To find which feels right.

- Stroke test, joystick (Sean, 2026-11-05): the left half popped a joystick when a stroke began there, so a move became a walk. In the robot fight the stick is fixed in a corner (chkStick, 85 px round it), bottom right as Sean asked, a Stick button flips it (zday_stick_side); a stroke can start anywhere else.
- The shape key: a green dot where a stroke starts, a red one where it ends (Sean, 2026-11-05: a line does not say which way). Heavy is a thin hairpin, out and back.

## Strokes in every fight on foot (Sean, 2026-11-05; strokes.js)
- The stroke code left fighttest.js for strokes.js, mixed into FightTest and Horde. Each says what is going on (chkCtx: go, fin, clutch, feet, let, off), what the shapes mean there (chkEntries, which is also the key) and does it (chkDo). Same five shapes everywhere: similar moves share a shape. The GRAB, stun, knockback and gold finisher buttons are hidden by CSS (class strokes), still in the page because the code touches them; TEAM, HURL and TAKE OFF stay. The grab choice buttons too, in effect: clutch reads check throw, caret spin, mirror drag.
- A dazed foe: check is Finisher 1, caret Finisher 2, the key large and gold. Neck drag and spin have no finger held now: the stick steers the drag, a tap punches, any stroke flings or lets go; the drag ends by itself at 8 s. Sky runs on its own with holds optional. A power from a stroke is one shot (no holding to charge); the kick is fixed at 0.6.
- Foes' fixed joystick and the settings: stick and dash timing saved in zday_stick_side, zday_dash_mode; their buttons only where the fight's top buttons show (robot, Sandbox).
- Not strokes: flying (a drag is the stick there) and the storm fights' PUNCH, GRAB, LASER.

## The flight home after a win (Sean, 2026-11-05; herogame.js flyHome)
- BACK TO BASE on the win card no longer cuts straight inside. finish() keeps where the hero was (homeAt: the flight's position, or the hero on foot in a crowd level); flyHome starts the flight there and runs the BASE button's autopilot (flight.baseGo, baseRun): up to cruising height, round to the front of the base at full boost, down to the door, the fade, then the office at the door as before. About 5 s from the middle of town.
- On the way: the bar, the HUD and the TEAM buttons hidden (F.homing, heropower teamUiStep), no army; the owner has no side so no storm game starts. A tap anywhere goes straight in; a 20 s watchdog gets her home whatever happens. A loss still wakes you by the pod, a crossed-over or empty team goes straight to the hub.
- Test: home-test.cjs (crowd level win, flying level win, a tap to skip).

## Stuck on sidewalks in the crowd fights (Sean, 2026-11-05, in the riot; horde.js openAt)
- The 2026-11-03 fix (fighttest.js open) only covered the one-on-one fights. The crowd fights (riot, zombies, aliens, worm, kaiju, the base's robot army) still stepped on the people's walking grid, which blocks rubble, trees and the edges of things. horde.move now uses openAt: anywhere in the town's grid but inside a standing building, sliding along walls; caught somewhere that is not open, any step gets her out. Measured: 60 of 60 spots the grid blocked walked through, no building centre open.

## The dust storm's throws without wire (Sean, 2026-11-05: "a mesh around them, it looks unfinished")
- Its rocks had bright wire edges so they read against the dust. Now a darker rock (7a5530) with a dark rim, an inside-out shell 1.14 times its size (livestorm outline). Its dust devils are a lathed funnel with pale slanting streaks wrapped round it (swirlTex in foepower.js), turning with it; the villains' twisters and devils on foot got the same streaks in place of their wire layer. The flash on the face before a throw was a sprite with no texture, a square; now the soft round glow (glowTex).

## The riot's enemies and instigators (Sean, 2026-11-05: "rioters are the enemies, they can be punched, with riot leaders or instigators to fight"; horde.js)
- A rioter hit (sweep, the cross's nearest) is no longer calmed on the spot: knock() takes them (EXT) and throws them back from the blow, 7 m/s for a punch, 9 for a burst, 12 for a kick or the signature's wave, with a hop; landing (flyStep, soft) they are calmed and go home, a little dust where they land. A thrown or tossed rioter still lands with a burst that knocks back those round (soft, so no chains).
- Three instigators in every riot (instMake, INSTS): The Ringleader, The Agitator, The Firebrand, each their own look, a pulsing red ring at their feet, 150 + 25 a story level of health. They walk with the mob (its centre plus their own offset), fists up every few seconds; within 16 m of her, or anywhere once the mob is gone, they come for her (3.6 m/s), and in reach it is a street fight on the ground (genFight with q.ground: air off, softer blows 0.75). instEnd: won, +5, 30 power, the rioters within 12 m calmed, "N TO GO"; lost, they back off 14 m with what health they had and come again in 6 s, she keeps hers (at least 35). footBack: back on her feet where the fight ended.
- The riot is over only when they are down: the count is rioters plus instigators (the top bar says "RIOTERS n + m LEADERS"), the mob gone says NOW THE INSTIGATORS, and the riot event ending by itself no longer wins (herogame update checks H.instLeft). The last one down: riotBreak, everyone still rioting goes home, the level is won.
- Test: riot-test.cjs (taps knock rioters back, the Firebrand came by himself, three fights on the street, TOWN SAVED).

## Punchier melee in the crowds (Sean's brief, 2026-11-05; horde.js, STYLES now exported from fighttest.js)
- Each fighter's pace (STYLES): the stick's walk times run, blows played spd times as fast (Tempo 1.3, Tyrant 0.85).
- Signature: a run of SIG_C (12) hits, the count says "TAP: SUPERNOVA" in the fighter's colour and holds 3 s; a tap starts it (sigGo, sigStep, 15 s between, no bites while it lasts). Each blow takes everything within 3.4 m and more, the last 7 m (11 m and a fling of the cars for the bursting ones, SUPERNOVA, WORLD BREAKER) in the fighter's colour; TIME STOP holds everyone within 14 m 3 s first, WEB SLAM 2.5 s; RAZOR WINGS and AFTERIMAGE dart from one to the next. Tripods and the giants take it too.
- Launcher: a combo's last blow (not in a riot or a giant fight) sends the nearest up 14 m/s instead of dusting it (launch); tap within 1.1 s to go after it (jugUp, jugWait), up to three blows, each bumping it higher, the third spikes it down (32 m/s) to burst 6.5 m on landing; she falls after it (jugFall). Missed, it still lands with a 4 m burst.
- Dash blow: a tap during a dash or within 0.45 s of one (ATK.dashBlow, a lunge of 10 m/s).
- The grab (corner stroke): a seized one is held in front (clutch), the key says Throw, Spin, Drag (check, caret, mirror; a tap throws). Throw: straight out at 24 m/s, a 4.5 m burst. Spin: the old spin, swipe to toss. Drag: the neck drag for 2.6 s (dragLen), then flung. No choice in 2.2 s: thrown.
- Test: melee-test.cjs (launch, two follow ups, the spike; signature 15 to 25 dusted; the dash blow; throw, spin, drag; thrown by itself; all six signatures).

## Free flight after a win (Sean, 2026-11-05; freefly.js, herogame.js freeFly, rhythm.js onStop)
- The win card has three buttons: BACK TO BASE (lit), FLY AROUND TOWN (blue), Back to the menu. Free flight starts over where the fight ended, the story's flight bar with Lock on and QUIT hidden (BASE is the way out, through the base autopilot and the fade, as Back to base), no TEAM buttons (F.homing). A row of five just over the bar, a status box at the top; tap one again to stop it. Each done is worth 40 XP.
- RINGS: ten glowing rings round the town 26 to 50 m up, each facing the next, the next one gold with a beam over it; through one (within 6.2 m of its middle, 3 m of its plane) and the clock starts; the best time kept per town in zday_rings.
- FIRES: five standing buildings apart from each other set alight, an orange beam over each; within 22 m across and 25 m over the roof she hoses it down (fire.douse, about 1.7 s, water drops from her to the roof).
- CATS: four on roofs, twice life size, a yellow beam over each; within 6 m she has it (carried under her), the green light in the street in front of its building appears; within 8 m of it and under 12 m up, it is home.
- REBUILD: up to three of the town's fallen buildings nearest her get a construction site (rebuild.js Site) with a blue beam; within 35 m the crew works ten times as fast. Nothing fallen: says so.
- BAND: the hero takes the singer's place (her rig in the vocalist's spot, the singer hidden) and the band game starts; Done puts the singer back and her in the air over the stage. No stage in the town: says so.
- Leaving an activity clears it: rings and beams gone, fires still burning put out, sites still going finished.
- Test: free-test.cjs (the card's three buttons, a ring course, five fires out, four cats home, three buildings raised, the band and back, BASE into the office).
- Also: genEnd falls back to where a street fight began when it hands back no position (seen once live after a neck drag in the riot).

## The rest of the enhancement brief (Sean, 2026-11-05: "keep going", plan item 2; horde.js, fighttest.js)
- Crowd kinds: zombie crowds (Sandbox and the zombie level; not aliens, robots or riots, which have their own) are dropped from ZMIX, more of the special types than a town rolls: walkers and their looks, runners (fast), crawlers, bloaters (3 health, burst when they go: a 3.2 m pop that hits those round them), juggernauts (6 health, armoured: a plain punch does 0.4 and says ARMOURED! HIT IT HARDER), spitters, screamers.
